We reached sub-micron spatial resolution and high imaging fidelity by correlating photocurrent maps with reflection maps, optical micrographs and AFM topography data on different devices fabricated with different materials (InSe, MoS2, WSe2, Gr), on different substrates (Si/SiO2, compact disk). Abstract: Precise knowledge of the total number of magnons, including both coherent and incoherent (e.g. thermal) excitations, is imperative for the advancement of fundamental spin-wave physics and the development of next-generation magnonic devices. We model the experimental data and extract the magnon number by simulating the magnon band structure of the sample, the magneto-optical response function, and the optical spot size used in the experiment. Our analysis establishes a connection between magnon mode calculations and experimentally accessible magnetic properties and fiducially reproduces the waveform and amplitude of the magneto-optical correlation signal for different experimental conditions. These results open a new pathway towards the optical tomography of magnon modes in non-linear or non-equilibrium conditions and can be readily extended to study ultrafast incoherent dynamics in other condensed matter systems.

Abstract: The recent advances in research on layered magnets have established them as invaluable materials for spintronic applications and precursors to 2D magnets. The GdAlGe magnetism is corroborated by a study of electron transport revealing the anomalous Hall effect and negative magnetoresistance. Abstract: Recent developments of universal machine learning interatomic potentials (UMLIPs) offer a fast route for screening molecular crystals based on geometry relaxation and energy ranking, but their reliability across chemically diverse energetic materials remains elusive. In particular, it is unclear whether or not these UMLIPs are over-sensitive to break the desired molecular connectivity for relaxing the periodic crystals. Herein we tested the hypothesis that classical force-field pre-relaxation can provide a more suitable starting geometry for subsequent UMLIP relaxation on a large database of high energy molecular crystals. Three models (MACE, MACE-OFF and UMA) in conjunction with the General Amber Force Field (GAFF) were applied to test this hypothesis. Among them, direct MACE-OFF and UMA showed very high relaxation success and preserved the reference geometries most closely, but they still exhibit failures for some rare cases. Using GAFF pre-relaxation can systematically reduce the number of failed relaxations with lower computational costs. Our comparative failure and robustness analyses revealed distinct trade-offs among the evaluated models. Among them, MACE-OFF achieves a better compromise between potential energy surface smoothness, structural fidelity, and stress convergence, serving as a good choice to provide a reliable foundation for automated structural optimization.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Dynamical control of altermagnetism is a key requirement for translating its unique spin-dependent functionalities into practical spintronic devices, yet effective switching mechanisms remain largely unexplored. Here, we open an unprecedented, versatile and programmable route based on spin-crossover to switch altermagnetism on demand in molecular materials. Using density functional calculations, we demonstrate an altermagnetic ground state in the layered van der Waals MOFs MnX$_2$(tdz)$_2$ (X = Cl, Br; tdz = thiadiazole), stabilized by anisotropic interlayer exchange interactions, which gives rise to a characteristic d-wave momentum-space spin splitting and an associated spin-splitter transport response. Our findings reveal that under hydrostatic pressure, a high-spin to low-spin transition reconfigures the Mn d-orbital occupation, thus modifying the magnetic exchange network, and stabilizing a different antiferromagnetic ground state whose symmetry suppresses the nonrelativistic spin splitting. Crucially, spin-crossover switches altermagnetism not by directly altering the electronic structure, but by changing the symmetry of the magnetic ground state. These results establish molecular spin-crossover altermagnets as a platform for externally reconfigurable spintronic devices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体铅卤钙钛矿纳米晶体是可扩展的单光子或纠缠光子对光源，但其激子相干性质尚未被充分理解，而相干性对量子光子技术至关重要。

Method: 在4 K下对单个CsPbBr3纳米晶体进行测量，分析声学声子介导的激子精细结构弛豫（EFSR）如何导致亮激子三重态之间的布居泄漏，并量化其相对于辐射复合速率的大小。

Result: EFSR泄漏路径的速率达到辐射速率的0.64±0.04，使态相干性被限制在变换极限的0.81倍；该泄漏是纳米晶体本征的，与外部环境无关。

Conclusion: 精细结构工程与声学声子工程是提高钙钛矿量子光源相干性的有效本征途径，为材料和器件设计提供了可操作的方向。

Abstract: Scalable sources of indistinguishable single photons or entangled photon pairs are fundamental to many quantum photonic technologies. Colloidal lead halide perovskite nanocrystals are promising such sources, but their exciton coherent properties are not fully understood. We show that in single CsPbBr3 nanocrystals at 4 K, acoustic phonon-mediated exciton fine structure relaxation (EFSR) drives leakage of population between the bright exciton triplet states, competing with the radiative lifetime. The leakage pathway reaches 0.64 $\pm$ 0.04 of the radiative rate, bounding state coherence to 0.81 times the transform limit at 4 K. This pathway is intrinsic to the nanocrystal, not its environment, making it a tractable target for materials and device design. We identify fine structure and acoustic phonon engineering as effective intrinsic routes to higher coherence in perovskite quantum light sources.

</details>


### [76] [High-Field Electron Transport in AlGaN alloys: A Full-Band Monte Carlo Study Based on Ab Initio Supercell Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07773)

Method: 使用基于超胞的全带蒙特卡罗方法模拟AlxGa1-xN中的高场电子输运。超胞方法显式捕获合金体系中的真实无序性，从而更真实地描述载流子输运；计算不同Al组分的速度-电场特性，并详细分析各散射机制的贡献；同时研究瞬态电子动力学和温度依赖关系。

Abstract: AlGaN alloys are promising wide and ultra-wide-bandgap semiconductors for next-generation power and RF electronics applications. To realize the full potential of AlGaN based devices, it is important to understand the electron transport to accurately predict device performance and identify material limits under various operating conditions. In this work, the high-field electron transport properties of AlxGa1-xN are investigated using a supercell based full band Monte Carlo method. The supercell approach is employed to explicitly capture the true disorder of the alloy system, enabling a more realistic description of carrier transport. The velocity field characteristics are calculated across a range of Al compositions to evaluate key transport metrics, including peak velocity, saturation velocity, and critical electric field. The role of different scattering mechanisms is studied in detail to understand the high field transport mechanism in the AlGaN alloy system. In addition to steady state transport, transient electron dynamics are examined for various Al fractions to study velocity-overshoot behavior, which is especially important for improving the performance of scaled RF devices. Finally, the temperature dependence of the velocity field characteristics in ultra-wide-bandgap Al0.75Ga0.25N is investigated to assess its transport performance under high temperature conditions. These results provide a detailed understanding of high-field transport in AlGaN alloys and offer guidance for the design of AlGaN-based RF and power electronic devices.

</details>


### [77] [Temperature-Dependent nonlinear optics from first-principles: Second-Harmonic Generation in few-layers MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07796)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度对固体非线性光学响应尤其是SHG的影响尚缺乏第一性原理理解；实验发现单层MoS₂的SHG强度随温度升高而反常增加，需要从电子-声子耦合和准粒子重整化层面给出机理解释。

Method: 采用实时含时演化框架，在独立粒子近似下处理电子激发；通过DFPT计算晶格动力学并获得电子-声子矩阵元；利用电子自能的Fan和Debye-Waller项计算准粒子能量重整化与寿命，并将寿命倒数作为退相干项引入运动方程。

Result: 单层和三层MoS₂的SHG均表现出非平凡的温度依赖，根源是强晶体动量依赖的准粒子重整化；声子模分析表明，单层中声学模、三层中剪切模分别主导这种动量依赖。

Conclusion: 该理论方法能解释实验中单层MoS₂在特定激光能量下SHG强度随温度升高而增强的现象，为有限温度非线性响应提供了一条清晰的从头算路径。

Abstract: We present a first-principles real-time approach to study non-linear response of solids at finite temperature. Finite temperature effects are included as renormalization of the quasiparticle energies and a dephasing term proportional to the quasiparticle lifetimes. We evaluate electron-phonon matrix elements from Density-Functional Perturbation Theory for lattice dynamics and then calculate quasiparticles renormalization and lifetime from the Fan and Debye-Waller terms of the electron self-energy. Electron excitations are treated at the independent particle level of approximation. We apply the approach to the second-harmonic generation (SHG) in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. We observe a nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG due to a strong crystal-momentum dependent quasiparticle renormalization. From the phonon-mode analysis we find that the coupling with acoustic and shear modes determines the overall crystal-momentum dependence respectively in monolayer and trilayer MoS$_2$. The nontrivial temperature-dependence of the SHG can help rationalize the increase of SHG intensity with increasing temperature observed in monolayer MoS$_2$ at a given laser energy [Adv. Optical Mater. 8, 2000441 (2020)].

</details>


### [78] [Atomistic modeling of molecular beam epitaxy growth of SrTiO3 and Sr2TiO4 thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08114)
*Guangfu Luo,Dane Morgan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算研究 SrTiO3 与 Sr2TiO4 薄膜在 SrTiO3 衬底上分子束外延生长的原子过程，发现三个关键机制：表面扩散可动态诱导氧空位并显著加速扩散；TiSr 缺陷可能促进 SrO 岛形成，偏离预期单层生长模式；SrO 双层上吸附的 Ti 原子和 TiO2 分子可插入双层，导致非预期生长顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBE 虽以原子层精度控制著称，但意外生长机制可能损害这种精度，因此需要从原子尺度理解 SrTiO3 等钙钛矿及 Ruddlesden-Popper 相薄膜的生长过程，以提升可控性。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，系统考察气相中潜在分子种类，并分析其在薄膜表面和层边缘的反应与扩散动力学。

Result: 揭示了三种对理解该类型生长至关重要的机制：一是在无缺陷衬底表面扩散期间可动态产生氧空位并加速扩散；二是尽管 SrO 层预期以单层模式生长，但 TiSr 缺陷可能促进 SrO 岛形成；三是吸附的 Ti 原子和 TiO2 分子能插入 SrO 双层，导致异常生长序列。

Conclusion: 这些发现对金属氧化物薄膜的 MBE 生长具有更广泛启示，可为改进其生长过程控制提供理论指导。

Abstract: Molecular beam epitaxy (MBE) is renowned for its potential for atomic layer control, but unexpected growth mechanisms can potentially compromise this level of precision. In this study, we employ first-principles calculations to investigate the atomistic processes governing the MBE growth of perovskite SrTiO3 and Ruddlesden-Popper Sr2TiO4 films on a SrTiO3 substrate. We systematically explore the potential molecular species in the gas phase and their reactions and diffusion dynamics on the film surfaces and layer edges. Our analyses uncover three mechanisms of importance for understanding this type of growth. First, oxygen vacancies can be dynamically induced during surface diffusion on defect-free substrate and noticeably accelerate the diffusion processes. Second, while the SrO layer is expected to grow in a single-layer growth mode, the presence of potential TiSr defects may promote the formation of SrO islands. Lastly, adsorbed Ti atoms and TiO2 molecules on the SrO bilayer can insert into SrO bilayers, resulting in an unexpected growth sequence. These findings may have broader implications for the MBE growth of metal oxide films and provide guidance for achieving improved control over their growth processes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索极端压缩条件下过渡金属原子体积与原子序数之间已知抛物线关系的演化，以理解压力如何改变元素周期性的体积趋势。

Result: 环境压力下的抛物线趋势在高压下转变为特征性的类立方行为；bcc过渡金属因较高的压缩性和相对较大的总能量增加驱动了这一转变。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了压力诱导的过渡金属体积趋势形变机制，并与已有实验及第一性原理计算结果相关联，为理解极端条件下元素性质演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigated the evolution of the well-known parabolic dependence of atomic volume on atomic number in transition metals under extreme compression at pressures up to 400 GPa using density functional theory calculations. Our results reveal that the ambient-pressure parabolic trend transforms into a characteristic cubic-like behavior at high pressures. This evolution is attributed to the higher compressibility of bcc transition metals associated with comparatively large increases in the total energy. The present findings are discussed in relation to previous experimental observations and first-principles calculations.

</details>

Result: 光生载流子显著降低了六方中间相与纤锌矿基态之间的能量差，从而急剧降低铁电翻转能垒；其物理起源为光致极性相部分金属化，屏蔽了稳定铁电序的偶极-偶极相互作用，转而有利于非极性竞争相。

Conclusion: 光激发提供了一种强效且非侵入式的纤锌矿铁电翻转控制手段，有望推动更快、更低电压、更节能的非易失存储技术发展。

Abstract: Wurtzite ferroelectrics combine large remanent polarization with full CMOS compatibility, positioning them as a leading platform for next-generation non-volatile memory. Their practical deployment, however, is hindered by an intrinsically large coercive field, rooted in the high energy barrier separating the polar wurtzite phase from the nonpolar hexagonal phase that mediates polarization switching. Here, using first-principles calculations, we propose an alternative, field-free strategy for lowering this barrier: above-bandgap electronic photoexcitation. Taking LaN as a representative wurtzite ferroelectric, we show that light induced carriers dramatically reduce the energy difference between the hexagonal intermediate phase and the wurtzite ground state, sharply reducing the energy barrier to ferroelectric switching. This effect originates from a photoinduced partial metallization of the polar phase, which screens the dipole-dipole interactions that stabilize ferroelectric order and thereby favors the competing nonpolar structure. The robustness of this mechanism is further confirmed for the rocksalt polymorph. Our results establish light as a powerful, non-invasive route to controlling ferroelectric switching in wurtzites, opening a path toward faster, lower-voltage, and more energy-efficient non-volatile memory technologies.

</details>


### [81] [Flexoelectric Polarization in Wrinkled Janus Transition-Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08295)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过面外褶皱产生的应变梯度工程可调控二维材料的电子与挠曲电性质。本研究结合DFT与连续弹性杆模型，系统研究了褶皱Janus MoSSe/MoSeS单层，发现全局面外极化随褶皱投影纵横比呈线性变化，并建立基于有效二维挠曲电系数的几何模型，无需高阶非线性参数即可准确预测极化。微观机制归因于Mo 4d轨道作为线性挠曲电核心，曲率驱动S/Se层间化学不对称电荷转移。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用褶皱诱发的应变梯度调控Janus二维材料的电子与挠曲电性能，建立清晰且可设计的几何规则，以服务于柔性纳米电子学和光电子学应用。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算结合连续弹性杆（elastica）建模，改变褶皱尺寸与压缩应变（5%-20%），系统分析极化行为；并通过原子与轨道分辨的电荷密度分析揭示微观电荷转移机制。

Result: 全局面外极化可由投影纵横比线性参数化；基于有效二维挠曲电系数的几何模型能准确复现DFT极化结果。中央Mo 4d轨道起稳健线性挠曲电核心作用，局部曲率则驱动S 3p与Se 4p亚层间的不对称电荷转移。

Conclusion: 确立了利用Janus褶皱实现挠曲电调控的几何设计准则，为柔性纳米电子与光电子器件的应变工程提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Strain-gradient engineering via out-of-plane wrinkling offers a powerful route to tune electronic and electromechanical properties in two-dimensional (2D) materials. Here, we systematically investigate the electronic and flexoelectric response of wrinkled Janus MoSSe/MoSeS monolayers using density functional theory (DFT) calculations coupled with continuum elastica modeling. Exploring varying wrinkle sizes and compressive strain levels ($5\%-20\%$), we show that the global out-of-plane polarization follows a linear behavior when parameterized by the projected aspect ratio of the nanowrinkles. On this basis, we develop a physically grounded geometric model incorporating an effective 2D flexoelectric coefficient, which accurately predicts DFT polarizations without requiring higher-order nonlinear parameters. Atom- and orbital-resolved charge density analyses reveal the microscopic origin of this behavior: while the central Mo $4d$-orbital manifold acts as a robust, linear flexoelectric core, local curvature drives continuous, chemically asymmetric charge transfer between the S $3p$ and Se $4p$ sublayer manifolds. Our findings establish clear geometric design rules for harnessing Janus-based flexoelectricity for flexible nanoelectronics and optoelectronics.

</details>


### [82] [EBSDmagus: Managing Multi-Stage Dynamical Electron Backscatter Diffraction Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08334)
*Ulrich Kerzel,Lukas Berners,Sanddra Korte-Kerzel*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: EBSDmagus 是一个用于管理 EMsoft 动态电子背散射衍射（EBSD）模拟工作流的开源工具，支持参数统一配置、中间结果复用、断点续算及完整溯源记录，提升大规模参数化模拟的效率与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 EBSD 模拟研究常需组合多种材料、结构、实验参数，而 EMsoft 涉及分离的配置、结构、散射和取向文件及多个依赖计算阶段。人工准备易出错，中断后难以判断可用结果，且单个图案的来源难以追溯。

Method: EBSDmagus 让研究者一次性定义固定与变化参数，自动准备所需 EMsoft 计算，复用兼容的中间结果，并校验生成文件符合研究设定。执行后检查预期输出，若中断则仅重新提交未完成的计算，保留已完成工作；同时生成可移植的运行记录，将每个输出与其输入和计算历史关联，提供 FAIR 数据所需的溯源信息。

Result: 通过已完成代表性计算、大型研究预先准备与检查、两次实际集群中断以及一次受控任务取消进行评估，恢复过程中 EBSDmagus 保留了已完成计算，且只重新提交未完成的部分。

Conclusion: EBSDmagus 有效解决了复杂 EBSD 模拟流程中手动管理易出错、中断恢复困难和溯源不完整的问题，增强了工作流的可管理性与可重复性，支持研究数据以 FAIR 原则发布。

Abstract: Dynamical electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) simulations increasingly support systematic studies of how material and experimental parameters affect diffraction patterns. Investigations that combine composition and crystal structure, including changes in atomic order, with accelerating voltage, temperature, and many orientations or detector conditions can require large sets of related calculations. Several packages provide dynamical EBSD simulation; we use the open-source, scriptable EMsoft programs because they are well suited to large studies on high-performance computing systems. An EMsoft investigation nevertheless spans separate configuration, structure, scattering, and orientation files and several dependent calculation stages. As variations multiply, manual preparation becomes difficult to check, interruptions obscure which results remain usable, and the origin of individual patterns becomes laborious to reconstruct.
  With EBSDmagus, researchers define fixed and varying parameters once. The software prepares the required EMsoft calculations, reuses compatible intermediate results, and checks that the generated files represent the requested investigation before execution. After execution, it checks the expected outputs and, following an interruption, resumes only unresolved calculations while preserving completed work. A portable run record links each output to its inputs and calculation history, providing the provenance needed when the results are deposited as findable, accessible, interoperable, and reusable (FAIR) data.
  We assess this approach using a completed representative calculation, a larger study prepared and checked before submission, two observed cluster interruptions, and a controlled job cancellation. During recovery, EBSDmagus retained completed calculations and resubmitted only incomplete work.

</details>


### [83] [Davydov Splitting Without a Davydov Pair and Highly Mobile Singlet Excitons in Perylene Red Microcrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08352)
*Chris Rehhagen,Tolibjon Abdurakhmonov,Magnus Frank,Oliver Kühn,Stefan Lochbrunner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过实验与理论结合，研究了含八个分子的苝红微晶的激子性质；实验观察到两个 Davydov 跃迁，其表观劈裂为 610 cm⁻¹，但对称性分析表明二者不属于同一 Davydov 对，因此该劈裂不能直接反映晶胞内库仑耦合。体系主要跃迁形成 J 型带，增强谱重叠并促进激子迁移；时间分辨荧光和超快瞬态吸收显示动力学由高迁移率单线态 Frenkel 激子主导，实验迁移率与 Kinetic Monte Carlo 模拟一致，支持非相干跳跃输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多分子晶胞有机晶体中激子的形成与输运机制，并澄清 Davydov 劈裂与分子间耦合之间的对应关系，因为名义上的八个 Davydov 跃迁中仅有少数可观测，且表观劈裂可能掩盖真实的物理图像。

Method: 结合实验与理论方法：实验上采用时间相关单光子计数和超快瞬态吸收光谱探测激子动力学；理论上使用基于密度泛函理论参数化的 Frenkel-Holstein 激子模型，并进行对称性本征态分析以及 Kinetic Monte Carlo 模拟。

Result: 实验观测到两个 Davydov 跃迁，表观劈裂 610 cm⁻¹，理论模型可合理重现；对称性分析表明这两个跃迁不属于同一 Davydov 对。主要跃迁构成 J 型带，增强了谱重叠和激子转移。实验测得激子迁移率与 Kinetic Monte Carlo 模拟结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 苝红微晶中的激子动力学由高迁移率单线态 Frenkel 激子主导，输运机制为非相干跳跃；表观 Davydov 劈裂不能简单地用作晶胞内 Coulomb 耦合的直接量度，需结合对称性分析才能正确解读复杂多分子体系的激子结构。

Abstract: We investigate the excitonic properties of perylene red microcrystals, whose unit cell contain eight molecules, using both experimental and theoretical methods. Only two of the nominal eight Davydov transitions are experimentally observed, with an apparent splitting of 610 cm$^{-1}$ that is reasonably reproduced by a Frenkel-Holstein exciton model parametrized from density functional theory. A symmetry-based analysis of the eigenstates reveals that the two observed transitions do not belong to the same Davydov pair, so the splitting does not directly report on the Coulomb coupling within the unit cell. The complex mixture of local excitations produces a J-like band for the dominant transitions, favouring exciton transfer through increased spectral overlap. Consistent with this picture, time-correlated single-photon counting of the emission and ultrafast transient absorption spectroscopy show that the dynamics is dominated by highly mobile singlet Frenkel excitons. The exciton mobility extracted from experiment agrees very well with that obtained from Kinetic Monte Carlo simulations, supporting a picture of incoherent hopping transport.

</details>


### [84] [A thermally grown SiO2 diffusion barrier enabling high-temperature investigation of Ag-Au-Pd-Pt thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08363)
*Elaheh Akbarnejad,Aleksander Kostka,Advika Chesetti,Alan Savan,Georg Fritz,Kamen Kozhuharov,Matthias Karl Klein,Yujiao Li,Alfred Ludwig*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以Ag-Au-Pd-Pt为模型体系，对比了热生长SiO2与原生Si氧化层作为扩散阻挡层的效果；热生长SiO2可将Si基组合处理平台在近原子尺度表征复杂组分固溶体薄膜的适用温度从约300°C提升至600°C，抑制硅化物形成与组分再分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Si微尖阵列结合组合薄膜合成与原子探针层析技术可用于复杂组分固溶体的近原子尺度表征，但在高温下，Pd、Pt等组分易与Si基底反应形成硅化物，限制其应用。虽然热生长SiO2对纯Pt与Si有效，但其对多元CCSS薄膜的阻挡性能尚不明确。

Method: 使用Ag-Au-Pd-Pt作为模型CCSS体系，在退火处理中对比25 nm热生长SiO2阻挡层与天然Si氧化层的效果，并采用原子探针层析和透射电子显微镜进行表征。

Result: 天然Si氧化层在300°C以下可阻止明显界面反应，但在400°C时Pd、Pt与Si反应形成硅化物并导致膜组分大量再分布；600°C时基底反应剧烈，破坏CCSS薄膜并形成针状硅化物形貌。相比之下，热生长SiO2在高达600°C时仍能抑制CCSS薄膜与基底的反应，保持薄膜组成不变。

Conclusion: 热生长SiO2可将Si基CPPs的适用温度范围扩展至至少600°C，从而支持CCSS薄膜在更高温度下的近原子尺度表征。

Abstract: Combinatorial processing platforms (CPPs), integrating Si microtip arrays with combinatorial thin film synthesis and atom probe tomography (APT), enable near-atomic-scale characterization of compositionally complex solid solutions (CCSSs) under diverse processing and reaction conditions, including oxidation, thermal phase stability and electrocatalytic reactions. Their application at elevated temperatures, however, can be limited when CCSS constituents such as Pd and Pt react with the Si support to form silicides. Although thermally grown SiO2 has proven effective as a diffusion barrier between pure Pt and Si, its performance for multicomponent CCSS thin films is unclear. Here, using Ag-Au-Pd-Pt as a model system, we compare a 25 nm thermally grown SiO2 barrier with native Si oxide during annealing using APT and transmission electron microscopy. Native Si oxide prevents detectable interfacial reactions up to 300°C, but at 400°C Pd and Pt react with Si, causing silicide formation and substantial redistribution of the film constituents. At 600°C, extensive substrate reactions disrupt the CCSS film and produce a pronounced needle-shaped silicide morphology. In contrast, thermally grown SiO2 suppresses CCSS thin film-substrate reactions up to 600°C and retains the CCSS composition. The thermally grown SiO2 thus extends the applicable temperature range of Si-based CPPs to at least 600°C for near-atomic-scale characterization of CCSS thin films.

</details>


### [85] [Laser Shock Peening in Hydrogen Environments: Coupled Stress Transport Trapping Mechanisms and Application Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08469)
*Elzbieta Gadalinska,Jan Kaufman,Jan Smaus,Jan Brajer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了激光冲击强化（LSP）作为抗氢脆表面层设计策略的研究进展，指出其通过深层压残余应力与近表面微观组织改性影响氢的传输与捕获，但现有结果受多因素干扰，且缺乏力学性能定量关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢脆限制了高强度钢和先进合金在氢基础设施中的使用。LSP 被广泛视为一种缓解手段，但现有研究多将其孤立为强化处理，缺少从应力、氢传输与捕获耦合机制层面对其作为表面层设计策略的系统评估。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，整合钢、镍基合金及增材制造材料中的实验证据，分析 LSP 后压残余应力、纳米结构、位错、孪晶和界面等对氢行为及损伤的影响，并审视充氢方式、粗糙度、污染、残余应力深度分布等混淆因素。

Result: 压残余应力可抑制应力辅助氢传输并延迟裂纹萌生，但 LSP 诱导的缺陷结构也可能促进局部塑性和损伤，导致氢重新分布效应具有两面性。现有数据受多种因素干扰，缺乏 LSP 后应力/缺陷结构与氢辅助疲劳裂纹扩展的定量关系，且几乎没有氢暴露下 LSP 冲击韧性的数据。

Conclusion: 需建立机制驱动的资格认证路径，将残余应力映射、氢表征、服役代表性力学测试与建模仿真相结合，以可靠评估 LSP 在氢环境中的适用性。

Abstract: Hydrogen embrittlement limits the deployment of high-strength steels and advanced alloys in hydrogen infrastructure. Laser shock peening (LSP) is increasingly considered as a mitigation route because it combines deep compressive residual stresses with near-surface microstructural modification. This review critically assesses LSP not as an isolated strengthening treatment, but as a surface layer design strategy governed by coupled stress, hydrogen transport and trapping mechanisms. Evidence from steels, nickel based alloys and additively manufactured materials shows that compressive residual stresses may suppress stress assisted hydrogen transport and delay crack initiation, while LSP-induced nanostructuring, dislocations, twins and interfaces can either redistribute hydrogen beneficially or promote localized plasticity and damage. Reported trends are frequently confounded by hydrogen charging mode, surface roughness, contamination, residual stress depth profiling and limited structure performance correlations. Two design critical gaps are identified: the lack of quantitative links between post-LSP stress/defect architectures and hydrogen assisted fatigue crack growth, and the near absence of impact toughness data after LSP under hydrogen exposure. The review proposes mechanism informed qualification routes combining residual stress mapping, hydrogen characterization, service representative mechanical testing and modelling.

</details>


### [86] [Thermal response of an in-situ STEM MEMS chip under rapid pulse heating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08473)
*Phillip Dumitraschkewitz,Thomas Kremmer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出一种利用任意波形发生器和串联分流电阻测量未镀膜商业Protochips Fusion MEMS芯片在STEM中原位快速凝固实验热历史的装置，实现亚毫秒级电阻/温度重构，测得最大冷却步长的时间常数约1.80 ms，平均冷却速率约7.9×10^4 K/s，并验证了系统稳定性与适用边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原位快速凝固研究需要高热时间分辨率的温度历史测量，而现有配置对商业MEMS芯片在STEM环境下的快速冷却动态表征不足。

Abstract: In-situ rapid solidification studies demand measurements of thermal histories with high temporal resolution. We present a simple, effective setup to quantify the cooling response of an uncoated commercial Protochips Fusion MEMS chip in an in-situ scanning transmission electron microscopy (STEM) context. We drive user-defined temperature programs via an arbitrary waveform generator (AWG), while recording the voltage drops across a series shunt to reconstruct chip resistance and temperature at sub-millisecond resolution. We confirm the response times inferred from the current; however, the temperature obtained from the physically linked resistance, $T(R)$, evolves more slowly. Analysis of the maximum cooling step reveals an exponential-like relaxation with time constant $τ=1.80$ ms, consistent with reported thermal lag constants for fast scanning calorimetry. From the time to reach $95\%$ of the temperature difference $ΔT$, we measure an average cooling rate of $\approx 7.9\times 10^{4}$ K/s. Robustness checks include repeated $R(T)$ measurements (revealing a modest downward drift approaching an asymptote), a 10 k$Ω$ test load, and characterization of small off-duty arbitrary waveform generator leakage/offsets. These findings define practical bounds on achievable thermal-path rates when planning in-situ electron microscopy experiments with this chip platform.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 相干声子可作为灵活、连续可调的手段，在自旋简并反铁磁体中激活并编程隐藏的非常规磁性，为声子驱动的自旋电子学应用提供新途径；完整分类为实验筛选和材料设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Spin-degenerate antiferromagnets can host hidden unconventional magnetism in their lattice degrees of freedom. We show that coherent phonons activate this magnetism by removing the spin-layer operations that enforce equilibrium band degeneracy without changing the collinear Néel order. The frequency and polarization of a pump electric field select a resonant \(Γ\)-point optical phonon and, within a doublet, its coordinate direction. This choice fixes the residual spin-layer symmetry. In monolayer MnPSe$_3$, an \(A_{2u}\) mode produces \(i\)-wave splitting odd in the mass-weighted phonon coordinate $Q$, reversing sign under $Q\to-Q$, whereas two orthogonal directions of the same doubly degenerate \(E_u\) doublet produce \(d\)- and \(s\)-wave splitting at a common resonance. Rotating the in-plane pump field \(\bm E_\parallel\) therefore programs both the
  spin-splitting texture and the thermoelectric spin current, continuously tuning the
  response between transverse pure-spin and longitudinal spin-polarized
  currents. A complete classification of two-dimensional collinear spin layer
  groups identifies the \(Γ\)-point coordinates that remove the
  degeneracy-enforcing operations and activate such unconventional magnetism.

</details>

*Michele Pisarra,Clara Rebanal,Enrique Arévalo Rodríguez,Marc Meléndez,Elena Blundo,Giacomo Amadore,Jonathan J. Finley,Fabián Calleja,Marc G. Cuxart,Jesús Álvarez,Ma-ría José Capitán,Fernando J. Urbanos,Julia García Pérez,Ramón Bernardo Gavito,Daniel Granados,Ji Dai,Massimo Tallarida,Antonello Sindona,Fernando Martín,Ferry Prins,Iolanda Di Bernardo,Amadeo L. Vázquez de Parga*

TL;DR: 本研究以MoS2xSe2(1-x)合金为模型，发现其光学与能带结构随组分连续变化，但载流子扩散系数在中度组分时出现显著不对称崩塌，源于随机硫属元素取代引起的局域势垒或深陷阱，表明合金中输运受无序主导，传统平衡态探针无法揭示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维半导体合金化广泛用于调控带隙，但其对电荷和能量输运的影响尚不明确；平衡态光学和光电子谱无法揭示无序对输运的潜在限制。

Method: 以MoS2xSe2(1-x)合金为模型，结合组分与层数变化，测量光学跃迁、价带色散、时间分辨空间载流子扩散映射，并用微观模拟解释实验趋势。

Result: 发现带隙弯曲可忽略、厚度重正化随组分变化；中间组分下载流子扩散系数显著且不对称地崩塌，不能由有效质量或能带排列解释，而源于局域能量景观的强实空间涨落；模拟表明无序性质依赖于合金化方向，形成散射势垒或深陷阱。

Conclusion: TMD合金输运受无序物理主导，输运是电子质量的严格度量，揭示了合金化在层状半导体器件中的内在限制。

Abstract: Alloying in two dimensional semiconductors is widely used to tune bandgaps, yet its implications for charge and energy transport remain poorly understood. Here, we investigate MoS2xSe2(1-x) alloys as a model system to study the interplay between composition, thickness, and disorder. Optical transitions and valence band dispersions evolve continuously with both stoichiometry and number of layers, with negligible bandgap bowing and a composition dependent attenuation of thickness driven renormalization. In contrast, time resolved spatial mapping of photoexcited carriers reveals a pronounced and asymmetric collapse of carrier diffusivity at intermediate compositions, which cannot be accounted for by changes in effective mass or band alignment, and instead emerges from strong real space fluctuations in the local energetic landscape generated by random chalcogen substitution. Microscopic simulations reproduce the experimental trends and show that the character of disorder depends critically on the direction of alloying, producing either scattering barriers or deep trapping sites. Together, these results demonstrate that transport in TMD alloys is governed by disorder physics, overlooked by conventional optical and photoemission probes at equilibrium. Our findings establish transport as a stringent metric of electronic quality and high-light intrinsic limitations in the usage of TMD alloys for layered semiconductor devices.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块体SnS的热电性能受本征点缺陷和能带结构限制，低维工程被认为是克服这些限制、提升热电性能的关键策略。

Method: 采用时间域热反射（TDTR）和薄膜热电参数测试系统（ZEM-3）测量不同厚度SnS纳米薄膜的塞贝克系数、电导率和热导率，计算ZT值。

Result: SnS纳米薄膜的热电优值（ZT）强烈依赖薄膜厚度和温度，四种厚度薄膜的热电性能均显著优于块体SnS，低维化有效提升了热电转换效率。

Conclusion: 工作为SnS纳米薄膜的性能优化提供了实验证据和理论见解，并为开发高效、环保的中温热电材料奠定了基础。

Abstract: SnS as an environmentally friendly, cost-effective, and earth-abundant narrow-bandgap semiconductor material, has demonstrated significant application potential in the field of medium-temperature thermoelectric conversion. However, the thermoelectric performance of its bulk counterpart is inherently constrained by intrinsic point defects (e.g., vacancies) and the material's specific band structure. Low-dimensional engineering has emerged as a pivotal strategy for overcoming these limitations and enhancing thermoelectric performance. In this work, we systematically investigate the thermoelectric properties of SnS nanofilms with distinct thicknesses (82 nm, 199 nm, 616 nm, and 813 nm) across a temperature range of 300-600 K. Measurements were conducted using time-domain thermoreflectance (TDTR) and a dedicated thin-film thermoelectric parameter test system (ZEM-3). Our results confirm that low-dimensionalization effectively boosts the thermoelectric performance of SnS, with the thermoelectric figure of merit (ZT) displaying a pronounced dependence on both film thickness and temperature. All four SnS thin films exhibit thermoelectric performance that is markedly superior to that of bulk SnS. This enhancement is primarily attributed to the quantum confinement effect, energy filtering effect, and intensified phonon scattering, all of which are induced by the low-dimensional structural characteristics. This work provides not only experimental evidence and theoretical insights for the performance optimization of SnS nanofilms but also establishes a foundational framework for the development of high-efficiency, eco-friendly medium-temperature thermoelectric materials, thereby holding significant scientific value and practical implications.

</details>


### [90] [Pulse-Burst Excitation Reveals Time-Dose Reciprocity Breakdown in Mixed-Halide Perovskites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08632)
*Alexandr Marunchenko,Shivam Singh,Daniel Lizotte,Bhaskar De,Yana Vaynzof,Ivan G. Scheblykin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 混合卤化物钙钛矿在脉冲串激发下，即使总光子剂量相同，仅改变光子在微秒至毫秒时间尺度上的分配即可导致不同的亚稳态，表明光致卤化物偏析与再混合过程违反时间-剂量互易性（Bunsen-Roscoe定律），光子时序成为新的材料态控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光化学常假设响应只取决于总曝光剂量，但混合卤化物钙钛矿的光致卤化物偏析呈现阈值行为，可能违反时间-剂量互易性。若证明确实如此，则可通过光子定时而非仅靠通量/重复率/平均功率来控制材料状态，并揭示竞争性光物理机制。

Method: 采用脉冲串激发，在常规激发参数之外引入时间维度的控制；通过将相同光子剂量重新分配在微秒到毫秒时间尺度上，产生不同的非平衡激发条件，观察混合卤化物钙钛矿的演化路径和最终亚稳态，并与时间-剂量互易性预言比较。

Result: 相同总光子剂量下，仅改变脉冲串的时间结构即可使钙钛矿演化到不同的亚稳态，明确观察到时间-剂量互易性在卤化物偏析与再混合联合过程中的失效；该时间自由度能控制材料状态，并为解耦竞争过程提供实验框架。

Conclusion: 光子时序是钙钛矿光化学的有效控制参数，为光学存储和神经形态光子学器件提供了新的可能。

Abstract: Time-dose reciprocity, commonly associated with the Bunsen-Roscoe law, states that the response of a photosensitive system depends only on the total exposure dose, regardless of how that energy is delivered over time. Light-sensitive processes in mixed-halide perovskites, such as photoinduced halide segregation, often exhibit threshold-like behavior that may violate this principle and enable material-state control by photon timing. We test this using pulse-burst excitation, which introduces an additional temporal control dimension beyond conventional parameters such as pulse fluence, repetition rate, and average power. By redistributing the same photon dose over microsecond-to-millisecond timescales, we create distinct nonequilibrium excitation conditions and show that mixed-halide perovskites can evolve into different metastable states, revealing a breakdown of time-dose reciprocity in the combined processes of halide segregation and remixing. This additional temporal degree of freedom not only enables control of the material state but also provides a new experimental framework for disentangling the competing processes underlying photoinduced halide redistribution. Our findings establish photon timing as a control parameter for perovskite photochemistry and open additional opportunities for optical memory and neuromorphic photonic applications.

</details>


### [91] [Pressure Dependence of Ferromagnetism in Uranium Hydride UH$_3$ up to 20 GPa Revealed by NV Centers Magnetometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08707)
*Valentin Schmidt,Loïc Toraille,Florent Occelli,Jean-François Roch,Paul Loubeyre*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: β-UH3的铁磁性起源至今仍有争议，其高居里温度和极短的铀-铀间距使其不符合铀化合物的已知规律；压力可有效调控并抑制磁性，从而揭示其内在机制，但β-UH3的高压行为此前几乎没有被探索。

Method: 在金刚石对顶砧中发展纯β-UH3的合成方法，并采用宽场氮-空位中心磁测量技术；提出两种数据分析方法，即矢量磁场重建和光探测磁共振响应的统计分析，以在等压升温过程中直接成像β-UH3的磁偶极。

Result: 观察到β-UH3的居里温度随压力线性下降，压力系数为dTC/dP=-3.86(8) K/GPa；外推表明约44 GPa时铁磁性被抑制至绝对零度。

Conclusion: β-UH3的铁磁性会在约44 GPa处消失，可能在该压力点出现量子临界点，这为理解其铁磁性机制及铀化合物体系提供了重要约束和新的研究途径。

Abstract: The origin of ferromagnetism in the stable $β$-UH$_3$ phase is still debated. With a high Curie temperature and a short uranium-uranium interatomic distance, $β$-UH$_3$ is placed outside the known systematics of uranium compounds. Pressure provides a powerful means of tuning magnetism toward its suppression, thereby offering valuable insights into its underlying mechanisms, yet the high-pressure behavior of $β$-UH$_3$ has remained largely unexplored. Here, we combine in the Diamond Anvil Cell (DAC) the development of pure $β$-UH$_3$ synthesis and widefield nitrogen-vacancy (NV) center magnetometry to measure the pressure dependence of the Curie temperature in $β$-UH$_3$ up to about 20~GPa. We present two data analysis methods, vector magnetic field reconstruction and statistical analysis of the optically detected magnetic resonance (ODMR) response, which enable us to directly image the magnetic dipole of $β$-UH$_3$ during isobaric warming. We observe a linear decrease in the Curie temperature with pressure, yielding a slope of $\text{d}T_C/\text{d}P=-3.86\,(8)$~K/GPa. Ferromagnetism in $β$-UH$_3$ is predicted to reach 0~K at approximately 44~GPa, where a quantum critical point may emerge.

</details>


### [92] [The absence of a central metal ion destabilizes phthalocyanine on In$_2$O$_3$(111)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08742)
*Viktoria Waidbacher,Sarah Tobisch,Faith J. Lewis,Moritz Eder,Michael Schmid,Gareth Parkinson,Ulrike Diebold,Margareta Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过低温STM、nc-AFM和STS研究了无金属酞菁（H₂Pc）在In₂O₃(111)表面的吸附行为，发现其吸附位点与CuPc/CoPc一致，但热稳定性极差，约50°C即分解，无法形成有序单层；对比表明中心金属离子不仅是稳定大环的结构锚点，还通过调控前线轨道特性决定是否出现第二类吸附构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属酞菁（MPc）在有机光电器件和单原子催化中具有重要应用，其与氧化物电极的界面吸附行为直接影响电荷注入/传输；同时M-N₄配位结构决定其催化活性。In₂O₃既是ITO的模型体系，也是CO₂还原的活性催化材料。理解无金属H₂Pc与金属MPc在In₂O₃(111)上吸附差异，有助于厘清中心金属离子的作用机制。

Method: 使用低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）、非接触原子力显微镜（nc-AFM）和扫描隧道谱（STS），在化学计量及还原的In₂O₃(111)表面上表征H₂Pc的吸附构型、电子结构及热稳定性，并与已报道的CoPc和CuPc结果进行系统对比。

Result: H₂Pc与CuPc及多数CoPc占据相同的吸附位点和构型，但在约50°C即开始分解，远低于CoPc/CuPc单层生长温度，因此无法通过温和退火形成有序单层。在化学计量表面上未观察到自金属化现象；在存在In⁰吸附原子的还原表面上出现新的H₂Pc相关特征，但仅靠成像无法确认其身份。对比三种分子显示：中心金属离子作为结构锚稳定大环，并通过改变前线轨道特性决定第二类吸附构型是否被占据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在MPc/In₂O₃(111)体系中，中心金属离子并非旁观者，而是对分子的表面稳定性（抗分解）和吸附构型多样性起关键调控作用；H₂Pc因缺少金属锚定而热稳定性差，限制了其有序单层制备，这为设计基于酞菁的氧化物界面功能材料提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Metal phthalocyanines (MPc) are a versatile molecular platform for applications ranging from organic optoelectronic devices to single-atom catalysis (SAC). Their adsorption and layer formation on the prototypical transparent electrode substrates of organic optoelectronic devices is directly relevant for charge injection and transport across the organic-oxide interface. Moreover, the well-defined M-N$_4$ coordination of the metal cation defines their activity as SACs for (electro-)catalysis. Here, the adsorption of the metal-free phthalocyanine (H$_2$Pc) is characterized on In$_2$O$_3$(111) using low-temperature STM, nc-AFM, and STS. In$_2$O$_3$ is not only a model system of indium tin oxide (ITO) but also an active catalytic material for CO$_2$ reduction. H$_2$Pc adsorbs in the same site and configuration reported for copper phthalocyanine [J. Mater. Chem. C 13, 17650-17661 (2025)] and for the majority of cobalt phthalocyanine [Surf. Sci. 722, 122065 (2022)]. Despite this shared preference in adsorption site, H$_2$Pc cannot be organized into extended and ordered monolayer structures by gentle annealing: the molecule starts to decompose at $\approx$50$^\circ$C, well below the temperature used to grow monolayers of CoPc and CuPc. Self-metalation is not observed on stoichiometric In$_2$O$_3$(111). On the reduced surface where In$^0$ adatoms are present, new H$_2$Pc-related features appear but cannot be identified by imaging only. The comparison of H$_2$Pc with CoPc and CuPc identifies distinct roles of the central metal ion in the metal-Pc/In$_2$O$_3$(111) systems: it acts as a structural anchor that stabilizes the macrocycle against decomposition on the surface, and it modifies the frontier-orbital character in ways that determine whether a second adsorption configuration is populated.

</details>


### [93] [Contrasting roles of the superexchange and direct exchange interactions in the metal-insulator transitions of the 2D ferromagnetic $1T$-Fe$X_2$ ($X$=Cl, Br, I) monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08807)
*Hongxu Luo,Baoyang Zhou,Sai Lyu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 以二维铁磁1T-FeX2（X=Cl, Br, I）单层为模型，通过计算研究发现，在库仑相互作用驱动的金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）附近，超交换相互作用倾向于稳定半金属态，直接交换相互作用倾向于稳定半导体态；半金属态具有更强的铁磁耦合和更高的居里温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁磁材料中的交换相互作用决定其铁磁性，而通过调控实现金属-绝缘体转变对低维电子器件设计至关重要，但MIT附近交换相互作用的临界行为及其对居里温度的影响尚不明确。

Method: 以二维铁磁1T-FeX2（X=Cl, Br, I）单层为测试体系，采用计算模拟方法，研究在位库仑电子-电子相互作用驱动的MIT附近交换相互作用的临界行为，并区分超交换与直接交换作用的贡献。

Result: 发现超交换相互作用和直接交换相互作用分别能量上有利于半金属态和半导体态；半金属态具有更强的铁磁耦合和更高的居里温度。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了MIT与交换相互作用之间的关联，为需要同时具备MIT和强铁磁耦合、高居里温度的二维铁磁材料器件设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Within the rapidly growing family of two-dimensional (2D) materials, 2D ferromagnetic (FM) materials have attracted a great deal of recent attention. The ferromagnetism in 2D FM materials originates from the underlying exchange interactions. In particular, metal-insulator transitions (MITs) in 2D FM materials induced via various modulation strategies is critical to the design of 2D electronic devices with reduced sizes (e.g. transistor). However, the critical behaviors of exchange interactions and their effects on the Curie temperatures near MITs remains to be clarified. By taking the 2D FM 1T-FeX2 (X=Cl, Br, I) monolayers as the testbeds, we computationally study the critical behaviors of the exchange interactions in the vicinity of the MITs driven by the on-site Coulomb electron-electon interactions. The FM superexchange interactions and the direct exchange interactions are found to energetically favor the halfmetallic and semiconducting states, respectively. In addition, the halfmetallic states have the stronger FM couplings and thus the higher Curie temperatures. This study provides a comprehensive understanding of the interrelations between MITs and exchange interactions and could be useful for the device design requiring both MITs and exchange-interaction related magnetic properties, particularly robust magnetic ordering and higher Curie temperatures, in 2D FM materials.

</details>


### [94] [Controlling Hydrogen Isotope Retention at Helium Cavities through Radiation-Induced Segregation in Fusion Steels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08878)
*Lihao Shi,Logan N. Clowers,Qing Peng,Gary S. Was,Fei Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究发现辐照诱导的溶质偏析通过增强氢同位素在腔体表面的稳定性和捕获能力，显著提高F82H钢中的氢滞留，并揭示了Ta和Cr调控电子结构的具体机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变装置中面向等离子体材料和结构材料对氢同位素的滞留是氘氚聚变的关键挑战，理解辐照环境下溶质偏析如何影响氢捕获机制对控制氚库存至关重要。

Method: 结合三重离子辐照实验（F82H钢）、第一性原理密度泛函理论计算，分析辐照诱导Cr和Ta向腔体表面偏析后的氢结合能、迁移势垒及电子结构变化。

Result: 观察到Cr和Ta在腔体表面显著偏析；计算表明这两种元素分别通过Ta促进H 1s-金属d轨道杂化、Cr调节表面d带至更有利成键构型，增强H稳定性并提高迁移障碍，从而大幅提升氢同位素滞留。

Conclusion: 建立了辐照诱导偏析与高温氢同位素滞留之间的原子尺度关联，并指出合金化学设计可作为调控聚变环境中氚库存的有效途径。

Abstract: Hydrogen isotope retention in plasma facing and structural alloys is a central materials challenge for deuterium-tritium fusion. Motivated by ion beam irradiation experiments and first principles calculations, we identify an irradiation-enabled mechanism whereby solute segregation to defect sinks enhances hydrogen isotope trapping. Triple-ion irradiation of reduced activation ferritic-martensitic steel F82H reveals pronounced segregation of Cr and Ta to cavity surfaces. Density functional theory shows that these segregants markedly increase H stability at cavities by strengthening binding energies and increasing migration barriers, leading to substantially higher attention. The effect originates from solute-tuned electronic structure: Ta promotes strong H 1s-metal d orbital hybridization, whereas Cr shifts the surface d-band toward a more favorable bonding configuration. These findings provide an atomistic link between irradiation-induced segregation and elevated-temperature hydrogen isotope retention and alloy chemistry routes to control tritium inventory in fusion environments.

</details>


### [95] [Zirconium Carbide as a High-Temperature Benchmark for the Beyond Quasi-Harmonic Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08913)
*Christopher M. Stanley*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文采用BQH方法计算岩盐ZrC的定压摩尔热容，有效包含非简谐振动贡献，加上电子热容修正后与完全非简谐理论基准在约1200 K内高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定压摩尔热容是材料科学的核心热力学量，但当非简谐振动效应显著时，从第一性原理精确计算仍很困难，因此需要能充分处理非简谐贡献的方法。

Method: 以岩盐ZrC作为高温基准，采用BQH（Beyond Quasi-Harmonic）预制备超胞第一性原理方法，直接从DFT能量评估中提取非简谐振动能；将所得C_p与CALPHAD参考值、全非简谐热力学积分计算、本工作计算的C_v(T)以及准谐近似结果对比，并根据费米能级态密度估计电子热容修正。

Result: BQH计算的C_p显著优于C_v(T)和准谐近似；加入电子热容修正后，计算结果在约1200 K内与完全非简谐理论基准吻合良好。

Conclusion: BQH方法能够完整捕捉普通准谐近似所缺失的重要非简谐声子贡献，是准确计算高温热容的有效途径。

Abstract: Molar heat capacity at constant pressure, C_p (T), is a central thermodynamic quantity in materials science, but it remains difficult to calculate accurately from first principles when anharmonic vibrational effects become important. In this work, rocksalt ZrC is used as a high-temperature benchmark for the Beyond Quasi-Harmonic (BQH) method, a prepared-supercell first-principles approach that extracts anharmonic vibrational energy directly from density-functional-theory energy evaluations. The calculated BQH molar heat capacity is compared with CALPHAD reference values, a fully anharmonic thermodynamic-integration calculation, a calculated C_v (T) curve from the present work, and a quasi-harmonic calculation. The BQH curve gives a substantial improvement beyond C_v (T) and beyond the quasi-harmonic approximation. Because ZrC is electronically conductive, an electronic heat-capacity correction was also estimated from the density of states at the Fermi level and added to the vibrational BQH result. This correction brings the calculated molar heat capacity into close agreement with the fully anharmonic theoretical benchmark through approximately 1200 K. These results show that the BQH method fully captures important anharmonic phonon contributions missing from ordinary quasi-harmonic calculations.

</details>


### [96] [Edge-Dominated Twist Mechanics at van der Waals Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08931)
*Yifan Shao,Zhaoheng Zhang,Hao Li,Oded Hod,Michael Urbakh,Quanshui Zheng,Xiang Gao,Deli Peng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过统一实验框架研究了范德华界面的扭转力学行为，发现峰值扭矩与接触面积近似线性关系，并揭示了边缘主导的屈服机制，与平移滑动的体相应力传递机制根本不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭转在调控范德华界面物理性质中至关重要，但其力学响应机制尚不明确，尤其是扭转与平移滑动行为的关系缺乏统一理解。

Method: 结合同质与异质范德华界面的扭转实验测量、滑动摩擦对比以及大规模原子模拟，系统分析扭矩与接触面积的标度关系及微观屈服机制。

Result: 两类界面的峰值扭矩均随接触面积近似线性增加，不同于线弹性和刚性模型预测；滑动摩擦差异超三个数量级，但扭矩仅差约二十倍；模拟显示扭转屈服由边缘重构主导，载荷区移至边缘，而平移滑动依赖体相传力。

Conclusion: 扭转与平移特性在范德华界面上无法互相预测，这种对称性导致的差异为可重构微纳机电系统设计提供了关键物理见解。

Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of twist in modulating physical properties at van der Waals (vdW) interfaces, the mechanics governing torsional response remain poorly understood. Here, we probe twist mechanics at homo- and heterogeneous vdW interfaces, together with their sliding behaviors within a unified experimental framework. For both systems, the peak torque scales nearly linearly with contact area, in contrast to predictions from linear elastic and rigid models. Remarkably, while the sliding friction of the two interfaces diverges by over three orders of magnitude owing to different scaling laws, the corresponding torque follows the same linear scaling and differs by only about twenty-fold. Large-scale atomistic simulations reveal an edge-dominated yielding mechanism for torsional motion, wherein elastic reconstruction shifts the effective load-bearing region toward the edges, eliminating torque from the contact interior. This mechanism contrasts with the bulk-mediated stress transmission governing translational sliding, a distinction rooted in the different loading geometries inherent to the two motion modes, where torsional loading necessitates perimeter actuation, whereas sliding enables center-driven loading. This symmetry-imposed divergence demonstrates that translational and torsional properties cannot be predicted from one another at vdW interfaces, providing critical insights for the design of dynamically reconfigurable micro- and nanoelectromechanical devices.

</details>

### [97] [First principles calculations of electric-field-driven topological phase transitions in silicene, germanene and stanene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05633)
*Julián Antonio Villarreal Murúa,Pablo Roura-Bas,Javier Daniel Fuhr,Ricardo Faccio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种结合密度泛函理论、最大局域化瓦尼尔函数和瓦尼尔电荷中心演化的第一性原理框架，通过在每个电场下进行全自洽模拟并构建瓦尼尔紧束缚哈密顿量，基于Z2拓扑不变量精确表征硅烯和锗烯中的拓扑相变，得到临界电场分别为0.020 V/Å和0.250 V/Å，较紧束缚模型显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维第14族单层材料（硅烯、锗烯、锡烯）的垂直电场可调控能隙并驱动非平庸到平庸的拓扑相变，但精确确定临界电场Ez^cr十分关键；传统紧束缚模型往往低估该值，因此需要能够正确描述屏蔽效应的第一性原理方法。

Method: 在每个电场强度下运行完全自洽的从头算模拟，同时考虑电子和离子的介电响应以获得屏蔽后的电子结构；从收敛结果构造每个电场下的瓦尼尔紧束缚哈密顿量，并利用瓦尼尔电荷中心演化进行规范不变的Z2拓扑不变量计算，从而定位拓扑相变临界电场。

Result: 该方法给出硅烯和锗烯的临界电场分别为0.020 V/Å和0.250 V/Å，相比以往方法在预测拓扑相边界上实现了显著的定量改进。

Conclusion: 该框架能够更可靠地预测二维材料的拓扑相变边界，对基于二维材料的拓扑场效应晶体管和静电控制量子器件的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: The emergence of two-dimensional topological materials, particularly the group-14 monolayers known as silicene, germanene, and stanene has opened promising pathways for next-generation nanoelectronics and spintronics. Their buckled honeycomb structure and strong spin-orbit coupling allow for bandgap engineering via a perpendicular electric field, leading to topological phase transitions (TPTs) from non-trivial to trivial insulating states. However, precise determination of the critical electric field $E_z^{\text{cr}}$ at which these transitions occur remains challenging, with tight-binding models often underestimating these values. Here, we present a first-principles framework that combines density-functional theory (DFT), maximally localized Wannier functions, and evolution of the Wannier charge centers (WCC) to accurately characterize TPTs in silicene, germanene, and stanene through the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological invariant. In contrast to earlier work, at each electric-field strength we run fully self-consistent ab initio simulations to obtain the screened electronic structure, accounting for the material's dielectric response from both electrons and ions. From these converged results we construct a Wannier tight-binding Hamiltonian at each electric field strength, which then enables a gauge-invariant calculation of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological invariant. This methodology yields significantly more accurate numerical predictions of $E_z^{\text{cr}}$, $0.020$ and $0.250$ V/Å for silicene and germanene, respectively. Compared to previous approaches, our framework delivers a marked quantitative improvement for predicting topological phase boundaries, essential for guiding the design of topological field-effect transistors and electrostatically controlled quantum devices based on two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [98] [Fast universal parametric spin control in an acoustically modulated quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05634)
*Mateusz Kuniej,Michał Gawełczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于光缀饰自旋和声学调制光学跃迁能的参数控制方案，实现半导体量子点单自旋的快速、近任意轴普适量子门控，将Pauli-X门缩短至约155 ps，并在约44 GHz声驱动下达到≥99.9%的平均门保真度，克服了传统声学自旋控制在旋转轴范围和门时间上的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信、分布式计算和混合架构需要既能相干控制量子比特又能耦合传播量子模式的节点。半导体量子点自旋可与微波及光学光子耦合，但与机械波相互作用弱，限制了单QD自旋比特集成到声学耦合混合系统。现有声学QD自旋控制理论旋转轴角度范围有限，导致门原语实现复杂且门时间长，几乎没有余量抵抗trion衰变和准静态核自旋噪声导致的退相干。

Method: 采用远失谐光学耦合到trion态来修饰并混合自旋态，同时用声学场调制光学跃迁能量。不依赖与自旋劈裂直接声学共振，而是利用与修饰自旋劈裂的共振来实现参数式自旋旋转，类比于电荷态激发的“swing-up”方案，并天然支持高次谐波过程。

Result: 实现近任意旋转轴的快速普适量子控制。约155 ps的Pauli-X门比先前声光方案快约290倍，比光学Faraday几何自旋旋转快约14倍。数值模拟显示，在约44 GHz声驱动下，对于GaAs和InAs量子点在未冷却核自旋环境中，平均门保真度≥99.9%，且trion寿命≥1.25 ns。

Conclusion: 该参数控制方案提供了实际可用的高速高保真自旋控制，并可能建立高相互作用率、多功能且含多声子过程的自旋-声子接口，对未来声耦合混合架构至关重要。

Abstract: Quantum communication, distributed computing, and hybrid architectures rely on nodes enabling coherent control of qubits and coupling to propagating quantum modes. While semiconductor quantum-dot (QD) spins couple to microwave and optical photons, weak interaction with mechanical waves has limited the integration of single-QD spin qubits into on-chip, acoustically coupled hybrid systems. The existing theory of acoustic QD spin control suffers from a limited range of rotation-axis angles, enforcing complex realizations of gate primitives and long gate times, leaving little margin against decoherence from trion decay and quasi-static nuclear-spin noise. We propose parametric control that overcomes these problems. We use far-detuned optical coupling to a trion state to dress and thus mix spin states, combined with acoustic modulation of the optical transition energy. Instead of relying on direct acoustic resonance with the spin splitting that leads to significant bottlenecks, we induce spin rotations parametrically via resonance with the dressed-spin splitting. We thus develop a spin analog of the ``swing-up'' charge-state excitation. Our scheme provides fast universal qubit control with nearly arbitrary rotation axes. Our ${\sim}$155 ps Pauli-$X$ gate duration is ${\sim}290\times$ faster than in the previous acousto-optical formulation and ${\sim}14\times$ faster than optical Faraday-geometry spin rotation. The parametric scheme naturally enables higher-harmonic processes. Numerical simulations for ${\sim}44$ GHz acoustic driving show average gate fidelity $\ge99.9\%$ even for uncooled nuclear-spin environments of GaAs and InAs QDs for trion lifetime $\gtrsim1.25$ ns. These metrics suggest practically usable control and may introduce a spin-phonon interface with high interaction rates, versatility, and multi-phonon processes, essential for future acoustically coupled hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [99] [Gauge-covariant magnetic Bloch sums for general multiorbital Hofstadter models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05646)
*Hao Shi,Tianyu Qiao,Wangqian Miao,Jin-Tao Jin,Quansheng Wu,Xi Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种统一框架，用规范协变的磁布洛赫求和基处理一般二维多轨道Peierls紧束缚模型，在有理磁通下将哈密顿量约化为最小qN_orb×qN_orb块，并给出数值验证与广义Harper方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Hofstadter问题处理方法通常受限于特定晶格几何、轨道位置或跃迁形式，难以统一处理包含任意胞内轨道位置、复杂跃迁表和第一性原理Wannier模型的一般多轨道系统。

Method: 构造规范协变的磁布洛赫求和基，完整保留Bravais几何、任意胞内轨道位置和原始跃迁表；利用有理磁通下对易的磁平移将Peierls哈密顿量块对角化为最小qN_orb维稀疏矩阵，并在斜Landau规范下推导显式形式；同时从同一跃迁数据导出广义Harper方程并与磁布洛赫块建立联系。

Result: 块维度仅取决于原胞通量而与分数轨道坐标是否无理无关；电磁规范变换仅通过酉共轭作用且不改变磁超胞；建立了能带计数、谱冗余、Chern数公式、磁空间约束和通量周期性；数值算例覆盖简单晶格、拓扑/平带模型以及单层MoS2的22带自旋ful Wannier哈密顿量，验证了与第一性原理计算的直接接口。

Conclusion: 该统一构造为任意二维多轨道紧束缚系统的Hofstadter物理提供了普适且计算高效的框架，并可直接嵌入第一性原理Wannier计算，拓展了拓扑、平带及磁场下能带理论的研究范围。

Abstract: We formulate a unified treatment of the Hofstadter problem for general two-dimensional multiorbital Peierls tight-binding Hamiltonians using a gauge-covariant magnetic Bloch-sum basis. The construction retains the full Bravais geometry, arbitrary intracell orbital positions, and the original hopping table, so lattice geometry, orbital embedding, hopping range, and orbital content can all be handled within the same framework. At rational flux $Φ/Φ_0=p/q$, commuting magnetic translations reduce the Peierls Hamiltonian to minimal $qN_{\rm orb}\times qN_{\rm orb}$ blocks. Their dimension depends only on the flux through the primitive cell, even when the fractional orbital coordinates are irrational. Electromagnetic gauge transformations act by unitary conjugation within the construction and do not alter the required magnetic supercell. We derive an explicit sparse matrix in an oblique Landau gauge and establish the associated band counting, spectral redundancy, Chern-number formulation, magnetic spatial constraints, and flux periodicity. Numerical examples include elementary lattices, topological and flat-band models, and a spinful 22-band Wannier Hamiltonian of monolayer $\mathrm{MoS}_2$, demonstrating a direct interface with first-principles electronic-structure calculations. As a complementary representation, we also derive exact generalized Harper equations from the same hopping data and relate them to the finite magnetic-Bloch blocks.

</details>


### [100] [A magneto-mechanical gyroscope with spintronic readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05668)
*Andrea Meo,Francesca Garesci,Pedro Bossi Nunez,Maddalena Fiorentino,Teresa Natale,Ludovico Dindelli,Victor Lopez-Dominguez,Banibrato Sinha,Riccardo Tomasello,Francesco Dell'Olio,Pedram Khalili Amiri,Mario Carpentieri,Giovanni Finocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种基于磁隧道结(MTJ)整流功能的类MEMS二自由度陀螺仪，利用MTJ作为读出机制和机械动力学换能器，通过实验标定与磁-机械模型设计，验证了角速率输出线性及动态情况下零差解调提取角速率的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MEMS陀螺仪大多依赖电容式传感机制，其灵敏度受限于微米级电容间隙及尺寸缩放限制，难以进一步小型化并兼容CMOS工艺；因此需要探索新的读出和换能机制，以放宽对紧密电容间隙的依赖并实现更紧凑的多物理场设计。

Method: 将磁隧道结的整流功能用作陀螺仪的读出机制，构建结合微磁理论和二自由度机械方程的磁-机械模型；利用实验中表征的MTJ数据对模型进行校准与设计，并采用零差解调方法处理动态角速率信号。

Result: 实验表明该器件的输出与角速率呈线性关系，并能通过零差解调在动态情况下有效提取角速率，验证了所提出的磁-机械陀螺仪方案。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现紧凑、CMOS兼容且无需依赖紧密电容间隙的陀螺仪读出路径提供了新思路，并促进多物理场协同的MEMS类器件设计。

Abstract: Gyroscopes are essential elements in navigation, consumer electronics, robotics, and aerospace applications. Most micro electro-mechanical systems (MEMS) implementations rely on capacitive sensing mechanisms, which limit the dimensional scaling to the micrometer scale. In this work, we introduce a MEMS-like two-degree-of-freedom (2-DOF) gyroscope that exploits the rectification functionality of magnetic tunnel junctions (MTJs) as its readout mechanism and as the transducer of the mechanical dynamics. Experimentally characterized MTJs have been used to calibrate and perform an experiment-informed design of the magneto-mechanical model combining micromagnetic theory with 2-DOF mechanical equations. We demonstrated that the output is linear with angular rate, and that the proposed device is able to extract the angular rate in dynamic cases exploiting a homodyne demodulation approach. The results open a path towards a compact, complementary metal-oxide semiconductor (CMOS)-compatible readout pathway that relaxes reliance on tight capacitive gaps and motivates multi-physics designs of the device.

</details>


### [101] [Spectroscopy With Intelligent Feature Tracking for automated characterization of excited-state structure in semiconductor quantum dot devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05686)
*A. R. Huffman,Daniel Schug,Merritt Losert,Sanghyeok Park,Daniel King,Yuna Chun,Giordano Scappucci,M. A. Eriksson,Justyna P. Zwolak*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT框架，结合机器学习特征识别与物理几何处理，从脉冲门光谱数据中提取量子点激发态能级分裂，并在Si/SiGe器件上实现离线与实时自动跟踪，将中位分裂误差从0.11 mV降至0.05 mV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点器件的激发态结构表征对自旋量子比特调谐至关重要，但传统二维光谱分析依赖人工且难以适应大规模器件，需要自动化且鲁棒的能级分裂提取方法。

Method: SWIFT利用机器学习辅助识别光谱特征，利用特征几何将二维光谱分析降维为一维峰值检测问题；结合集成置信度指标与低信噪比扫描的序贯累积，随证据积累动态重估谱图，实现实时跟踪。

Result: 在255个手动标注扫描上，SWIFT将中位分裂误差从经典基线的0.11 mV降至0.05 mV，尤其在低质量测量上改进最大，并成功演示离线与实时自动跟踪激发态与铅共振。

Conclusion: SWIFT为自主量子点表征与调谐提供了可行路径，有助于将激发态光谱整合进大规模量子点器件的自动化优化流程中。

Abstract: The characterization of excited-state structure in semiconductor quantum dot (QD) devices is an important component of tuning them for spin-qubit operation. We present Spectroscopy With Intelligent Feature Tracking (SWIFT), a framework that combines machine-learning (ML)-assisted feature identification with physics-informed geometric processing to extract energy-level splittings from pulsed-gate spectroscopy data. SWIFT isolates the relevant spectral features and exploits their characteristic geometry to reduce the two-dimensional spectroscopy analysis to a one-dimensional peak-detection problem. It further combines an ensemble-based confidence metric with sequential accumulation of rapid, low-SNR scans, allowing the inferred spectrum to be reevaluated as experimental evidence accumulates. Using Si/SiGe QD devices, we demonstrate SWIFT both offline and in real time, including automated tracking of QD excited states and lead resonances. Benchmarking on 255 manually labeled scans shows that SWIFT reduces the median splitting error to 0.05 mV from 0.11 mV for a classical baseline, with the largest improvement on lower-quality measurements. These results provide a path toward incorporating excited-state spectroscopy into autonomous QD characterization, tuning, and optimization, which will be essential in large-scale quantum dot devices.

</details>


### [102] [Large Nernst effect in chemically derived multilayer graphene at millitesla magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05898)
*Valentin Semkin,Denis Borisenko,Yana Litun,Oleg Kononenko,Dmitry Mylnikov,Alexey Bocharov,Dmitry Svintsov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在化学衍生的多层石墨烯薄膜中，利用扫描激光加热在低磁场（约4 mT）下观察到与纵向热电电压相当的横向Nernst热电电压，其横向热电势在315 mT时可达250 μV/K，展示了多层石墨烯在热电发电中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨基热电发电机因其合成简单、导电性高且易于集成而受到关注，但石墨烯的半金属特性导致电子-空穴补偿，使热电功率几乎为零，限制了其应用。

Method: 通过化学方法制备具有厚度和载流子密度强波动的多层石墨烯薄膜，使用扫描激光诱导加热宏观薄膜，并测量横向（Nernst）热电电压，同时利用霍尔探针在直流磁场方向与局部Nernst电流共向时增强信号。

Result: 在B≈4 mT和环境条件下，横向热电电压与金属掺杂石墨烯接触处的纵向热电电压相当；估算的横向热电势S_xy在B*时约为10 μV/K，并随磁场亚线性增加，在B≈315 mT（厘米级永磁体最大场）时达到250 μV/K。

Conclusion: 结果表明，多层石墨烯薄膜在低磁场下展现出显著横向热电效应，为大面积石墨烯用于热电能量转换提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Simple synthesis of graphitic compounds, their high conductivity, and integrability with other materials motivate the effort toward graphite-based thermoelectric generators. At the same time, semimetallic nature of graphite and graphene results in nearly-zero thermopower due to electron-hole compensation. Here, we observe large transverse thermopower in chemically derived multilayer graphene films with strong fluctuations of thickness and carrier density at low magnetic fields $B$. Using the scanning laser-induced heating of macroscopic film, we find that transverse (Nernst) thermoelectric voltage becomes comparable to the longitudinal thermoelectric voltage at the metal-doped graphene contact at $B^*\approx4$ mT and ambient conditions. Estimates of transverse thermopower $S_{xy}$ based on the known laser-induced temperature show that it is as large as $\sim 10$ $μ$V/K at $B^*$, and raises in a sub-linear fashion to 250 $μ$V/K at $B\approx315$ mT, the maximum field we reach with centimeter-sized permanent magnet. Extra increase in thermoelectric signal is achieved upon voltage measurement at Hall probes when the dc field lines are co-directional with local Nernst current. Our results show the promise of large-scale multilayer graphene for thermoelectricity generation.

</details>


### [103] [One-dimensional quasicrystals with tensor-network finite-state automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06040)
*Milla Kolehmainen,Jose L. Lado,Anouar Moustaj*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代换规则与自动机理论的框架，将一维准晶的紧束缚哈密顿量精确表示为矩阵乘积算符，并利用张量网络与核多项式方法高效计算超过10^9位点的谱密度，从而解析谱的分形层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶介于晶体平移有序与无序非晶之间，其模拟因缺乏平移对称性而困难，通常需对大型有限近似体进行昂贵对角化；现有方法难以高效处理任意大尺寸的准晶系统。

Method: 利用准晶代换规则在适配记数系统中定义确定性有限自动机，输入位点索引数字并输出该位点字母；进一步将代换转移矩阵等价为矩阵乘积态的张量，其键维数等于自动机状态数且与系统尺寸无关，从而得到精确的矩阵乘积算符；结合张量网络压缩与核多项式方法计算谱密度。

Result: 对金属平均族和k-bonacci族（含Fibonacci、silver-mean、Tribonacci）显式构造了框架，并对超过10^9位点的一维链计算谱密度，直接分辨出谱的层级结构。

Conclusion: 该自动机-张量网络方法为准晶物理提供了一种高效、精确且可扩展的模拟工具，避免了有限近似对角化的瓶颈，并能揭示准晶谱的普适分形特征。

Abstract: Quasicrystals occupy a distinctive position between the translational order of crystals and the disordered amorphous matter. Simulating this physics has remained challenging, since quasiperiodic structures lack the translational symmetry exploited for crystals and generally require costly diagonalization of large finite approximants. Quasicrystalline order admits two equivalent descriptions, a cut-and-project scheme from a higher-dimensional periodic crystal, and a discrete set of substitution rules acting on a finite alphabet. We show that the latter, written in a numeration system adapted to the substitution, defines a deterministic finite automaton with output, the digits of a site index are fed, and the automaton returns the letter occupying that site. We further exploit another equivalence to a different construction, the transition matrices are exactly the tensors of a matrix product state, whose bond dimension is the number of automaton states and is independent of system size. This allows efficient representation of extremely large tight-binding Hamiltonians in the tensor-train language, thereby yielding an exact matrix product operator for the quasicrystal Hamiltonian at any system size. We show how this framework works for two families of one-dimensional quasicrystals, the metallic-mean and $k$-bonacci families and we explicitly construct the Fibonacci, silver-mean, and Tribonacci quasicrystals. By leveraging efficient tensor-network compression and the kernel polynomial method, we compute spectral densities for chains with more than $10^9$ sites and directly resolve the hierarchical structure of the spectrum.

</details>


### [104] [Static and Ensemble-Dependent Thermodynamics of the Strain-Induced Parity Anomaly in Gapped Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05999)
*Ara Sedrakyan,Klaus Ziegler*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究具有子格能隙的石墨烯中，静态形变产生的赝磁场（谷奇磁场）如何导致两个谷在宇称奇响应中相加，并系统推导了该效应的平衡态热力学性质，提出了通过反转赝磁场来隔离一种无需真实磁场的平衡态电机械反常的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯的静态形变可作为谷奇磁场作用于狄拉克电子；在子格能隙体系中，两个谷在宇称奇响应中不再抵消而是叠加，从而产生可观测效应。此前相关分析常混淆平衡热力学与有限频率输运，且缺乏对能斯特、比热等热力学量的清晰边界条件描述，因此需要严格分离平衡响应并明确电学测量边界条件的影响。

Method: 基于连续谱理论推导固定电化学势下的平衡热力学；采用大质量狄拉克态密度和精确有限场赝朗道能级计算；对比固定势与固定栅压（定容）电路条件，引入几何电容，并给出无迹三轴应变几何、无序-相互作用窗口及量热和量子电容尺度。

Result: 在固定电化学势下，反转赝磁场可移除所有非零赝朗道能级，剩余巨势差由谱不对称的零级能级决定；静态电荷响应为热展宽的平台，局限于能隙内，无金属的m/|μ|尾部。低温窗口内赝场反转每个未分裂零模态转移B ln2的熵；固定μ比热每边缘有两个侧瓣并存在普适峰0.439229 D_B k_B。固定载流子数时，低温边缘值为-2(ln2)^2 D_B k_B而非节点；有限几何电容给出可实验调谐的连续插值。

Conclusion: 场反转协议可在无真实磁场条件下隔离平衡电机械反常；测量到的片状比热依赖于电学边界条件，这为实验提供了可调谐的热力学探针，并为石墨烯应变工程中的量热与量子电容测量提供了定量预测。

Abstract: A static deformation of graphene can act on its Dirac electrons as a valley-odd magnetic field. In sublattice-gapped graphene this field makes the two valleys add in the parity-odd response rather than cancel. We derive the equilibrium thermodynamics of this effect and separate it from the finite-frequency transport response. At fixed electrochemical potential, reversing the pseudomagnetic field removes every nonzero pseudo-Landau level in the continuum theory. The remaining grand-potential difference is fixed by the spectrally asymmetric zeroth level. The static charge response is a thermally broadened plateau confined to the gap and has no metallic $m/|μ|$ tail. Near a band edge, pseudofield reversal transfers $\mathrm{B}\ln2$ of entropy per unsplit zero-mode state in the low-temperature window. The fixed-$μ$ heat capacity has two side lobes per edge and a universal peak $0.439229\,D_B k_{\mathrm B}$. We then formulate a definite constant-gate-voltage circuit and show that the measured sheet heat capacity depends on the electrical boundary condition. The full massive-Dirac density of states and an exact finite-field pseudo-Landau-level calculation give the same gate crossover in their common limit. At fixed carrier number, the low-temperature edge value is $-2(\ln2)^2 D_B k_{\mathrm B}$, rather than a node. A finite geometric capacitance gives a continuous and experimentally tunable interpolation. Finally, we give a trace-free triaxial strain geometry, a disorder--interaction window, and realistic calorimetric and quantum-capacitance scales. The field-reversal protocol isolates an equilibrium electromechanical anomaly without a real magnetic field.

</details>


### [105] [Purely Electric-Field Control of Topological Magnetism in Two-Dimensional Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06215)
*Zhonglin He,Naafis Ahnaf Shahed,Kai Huang,Himanshu Mavani,Evgeny Y. Tsymbal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种通过静电场掺杂二维范德华磁半导体中特定轨道角动量极化带边，从而纯电场可逆调控拓扑磁性相变（铁磁、斯格明子、螺旋、双斯格明子）的通用策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电学控制拓扑磁性对节能拓扑自旋电子学至关重要，但现有电场方法通常通过非选择性地重整低能电子态来改变竞争磁相互作用，导致相控粗糙且常需外磁场稳定拓扑准粒子；少数纯电场方案又局限于特定导电材料。

Method: 利用邻近非磁性范德华金属的静电掺杂效应，在二维范德华磁半导体中实现对特定轨道角动量极化带边的选择性掺杂，从而主要调控磁各向异性，同时几乎不改变交换相互作用和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用，并演示于CrBr3/graphene与Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2异质结。

Result: 静电掺杂可逆驱动铁磁、斯格明子、螺旋和双斯格明子相之间的转变；该机制在不同轨道特征的电子/空穴口袋中均有效。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了纯电场控制拓扑磁性的普适策略，为拓扑自旋电子学中的低能耗器件设计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Electrical control of topological magnetism is central to realizing energy-efficient topological spintronics. Yet most electric-field approaches modify the competing magnetic interactions through a nonselective rearrangement of low-energy electronic states, yielding coarse magnetic phase control that often requires external magnetic fields to stabilize topological quasiparticles, while few schemes solely based on electric fields are restricted to specific conducting materials. Here, we establish a general approach for purely electric-field control of topological magnetism, in which an applied electric field electrostatically dopes a selected orbital-angular-momentum-polarized band edge of a two-dimensional (2D) van der Waals (vdW) magnetic semiconductor via proximity to an adjacent nonmagnetic vdW metal. We show that the resulting electrostatic doping predominantly tunes magnetic anisotropy of the 2D magnet, while leaving exchange interaction and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction nearly unchanged, thereby reversibly driving the system through ferromagnetic, skyrmion, spiral, and bimeron phases. We demonstrate this mechanism for CrBr3/graphene and Cr2Ge2Te6/TaS2 vdW heterostructures hosting electron and hole pockets of different orbital characters. These results establish a broadly applicable strategy for purely electric-field control in topological spintronics.

</details>


### [106] [Continuum Limit of Nonlocal Electrostatics in Random Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06354)
*Prashant K. Jha,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 针对晶格平移下平稳遍历的随机电荷密度场，建立了静电能量的双尺度连续极限，证明局域与非局域能量几乎必然收敛到确定性极限，并揭示单元去极化项在总能量中抵消、有效极化控制非局域极限的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为含随机微结构（如无序介质、复合材料）的静电问题提供严格的均匀化理论，特别是在每个微观晶胞保持电中性但偶极矩存在随机涨落的情况下，理解微观随机性如何涌现为宏观连续能量描述。

Method: 采用两尺度渐近分析结合随机均匀化框架，对微观势和有限体积场施加平稳遍历与积分性假设，将能量分解为局域与非局域两部分，分别求其几乎必然极限；并构造独立单元随机位移模型以验证所提假设。

Result: 局域极限由系综平均的微观库仑能加上单元去极化项构成，非局域极限由有效极化张量决定；总能量中单元去极化项相互抵消，只剩下系综平均微观单元能与宏观静电场能。确定性退化为经典周期双尺度极限。在独立单元随机位移模型中，中心化的微观偶极波动仅改变局域能量，不改变有效极化与非局域能量。

Conclusion: 给出了随机电荷密度场静电能量均匀化的严格两尺度极限定理，厘清了微观随机涨落与有效宏观性质之间的关系，即局部能量可受中心化扰动影响而宏观极化响应不受影响。

Abstract: We derive a two-scale continuum limit for the electrostatic energy of random charge density fields that are stationary and ergodic under lattice translations. Each microscopic cell is charge neutral in every realization, while its dipole moment may fluctuate and have a nonzero mean. Under assumptions on the stationary microscopic potential and the finite-volume fields, the local and nonlocal energies converge almost surely to deterministic limits. The local limit consists of the ensemble-averaged microscopic Coulomb energy and a cell-depolarization term, whereas the effective polarization determines the nonlocal limit. The cell-depolarization terms cancel in the total energy, which consists of the ensemble-averaged microscopic cell energy and the macroscopic electrostatic field energy. The deterministic specialization recovers the periodic two-scale limit. For an independent-cell random-displacement model, we verify the assumptions of the continuum-limit theorem and show that centered microscopic fluctuations can change the local energy without changing the effective polarization or the nonlocal energy.

</details>


### [107] [Simple invariants for band topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07775)
*Adam Yanis Chaou,Adolfo G. Grushin,Peru d'Ornellas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文表明，将谱局部子（由哈密顿量与位置算符构造）视为一个辅助零维哈密顿量，其拓扑可编码父哈密顿量的高维拓扑相；由此可把任意非相互作用拓扑不变量简化为矩阵签名或Pfaffian符号，从而为晶态与非晶态系统提供统一、高效、实空间的拓扑不变量构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管对非相互作用拓扑物态已有详尽理解，但尚缺乏一种能系统性地给出简单、数值高效、且同时适用于所有晶态与非晶态体系的拓扑不变量方法。

Method: 重新审视由哈密顿量与位置算符构造的谱局部子，将其作为辅助零维哈密顿量；利用其拓扑对应父哈密顿量的高维拓扑相，将所有拓扑不变量化为适当局部子的矩阵签名或Pfaffian符号，这些量可在实空间简单解释并高效计算。

Result: 该方法成功导出了此前超出谱局部子 formalism 范围的弱拓扑相与旋转不变晶态相的简单实空间不变量，也适用于散射不变量无法捕捉的原子极限、对称性指标方法失效的相，以及此前没有已知不变量的相。

Conclusion: 该工作为构造任意非相互作用拓扑不变量提供了系统性途径，适用于晶态与非晶态体系，为分类和预测此前未探索材料类别的拓扑性质开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Despite the exhaustive understanding gathered around non-interacting topological states of matter, there is no single method capable of systematically delivering simple, numerically efficient topological invariants that is applicable to all crystalline and non-crystalline systems alike. Here we revisit the spectral localizer operator, constructed from the Hamiltonian and position operators, and show how it can be treated it as an auxiliary zero-dimensional Hamiltonian whose topology encodes the higher dimensional phases of the parent Hamiltonian. Its classification reduces every topological invariant to a matrix signature or the sign of a Pfaffian for an appropriate localizer, both of which are simple to interpret and efficient to compute in real space. We validate this approach by deriving simple real-space invariants for weak and rotationally invariant crystalline phases that were previously beyond the grasp of the spectral localizer formalism, atomic limits that escape scattering invariants, phases that evade symmetry-based indicator methods, as well as phases that had no previously known invariant. Our work provides a systematic way to construct any non-interacting topological invariant for a crystalline or non-crystalline systems, opening avenues to classify and predict the topology of previously unexplored classes of materials.

</details>


### [108] [Near-Field Spin Seebeck Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06411)
*Gaomin Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并论证了一种由近场热辐射驱动、光子介导的自旋塞贝克效应，在单层过渡金属硫族化合物中通过Rashba自旋轨道耦合将光学轨道激发转化为电子自旋极化，为纳米尺度自旋操控提供了非接触式辐射路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋塞贝克效应依赖界面热传导产生自旋流，受限于材料接触和声子输运，缺乏非接触、辐射驱动的自旋操控机制；因此需要探索利用近场热辐射实现自旋极化的新物理途径。

Method: 建立理论模型，将单层过渡金属硫族化合物与热发射体以真空间隙隔开；考虑近场热辐射光子与Rashba自旋轨道耦合的相互作用，分析两种机制：手性热光子直接转移角动量，以及磁化二维电子气对非偏振热涨落的整流效应。

Result: 证明近场热辐射可在单层TMD中诱导电子自旋极化，且由两种互补机制协同贡献；该效应是光子介导的，无需声子热传导或直接界面接触。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一种全新的辐射型自旋塞贝克机制，扩展了热自旋电子学范畴，为远距离、无接触的纳米尺度自旋信息操控与热辐射能量转换提供了原理性基础。

Abstract: The conventional spin Seebeck effect generates spin currents through interfacial thermal conduction. In this Letter, we establish a photon-mediated spin Seebeck effect driven by near-field thermal radiation. Using a monolayer transition metal dichalcogenide separated from a thermal emitter by a vacuum gap, we demonstrate Rashba spin-orbit coupling can convert optical orbital excitations into an electron spin polarization. This occurs via two complementary mechanisms: the direct transfer of angular momentum from chiral thermal photons, and the rectification of unpolarized thermal fluctuations by a magnetized two-dimensional electron gas. These findings unveil a radiative pathway for nanoscale electron spin manipulation.

</details>


### [109] [Divergent Orbital Diamagnetism from Chiral Edge States in Chern Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09065)
*Nobuyuki Okuma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究发现，开放边界条件下的陈绝缘体（Chern insulator）具有与二维无质量狄拉克系统（如石墨烯）类似的发散轨道抗磁磁化率，其随体系尺寸线性增长，且该巨抗磁对无序具有鲁棒性，表明其拓扑起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维无质量狄拉克系统的轨道抗磁磁化率已知会随体系尺寸线性发散，而手性边界态可类比为放大的苯环结构的一半，这促使作者研究陈绝缘体在开放边界条件下是否也呈现类似的发散抗磁行为。

Method: 作者以手性边界态与苯环放大的类比重构物理图像，对陈绝缘体在开放边界条件下进行理论研究，分析其轨道磁响应并考察无序效应的影响。

Result: 陈绝缘体在开放边界条件下同样表现出随体系尺寸线性发散的巨大抗磁磁化率，且该效应不受无序破坏，凸显其拓扑本质；该行为与无质量狄拉克系统的发散抗磁性存在深刻联系。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了陈绝缘体中巨抗磁性的拓扑起源，并建立了与无质量狄拉克系统发散抗磁性之间的概念联系，丰富了对拓扑材料磁响应的理解。

Abstract: Two-dimensional massless Dirac systems, exemplified by graphene, are known to exhibit a divergent orbital diamagnetic susceptibility that scales linearly with system size. Motivated by viewing a chiral edge state as one half of an enlarged analogue of a benzene ring, we study Chern insulators under open boundary conditions and find the same divergent scaling. This giant diamagnetism is robust against disorder, revealing its topological nature. Our results further suggest a profound connection to the divergent diamagnetism of massless Dirac systems.

</details>


### [110] [Emergent Chiral Metal Phase in Compressible Quantum Hall Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06532)
*Suparna Sahoo,Suvankar Purkait,Pooja Agarwal,Tanmay Maiti,Sourin Das,Vladimir Umansky,Biswajit Karmakar*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在可压缩量子霍尔流体中，实验测得所有透射电导之和普遍趋近于霍尔电导 νe²/h，形成一个与温度、几何、材料及相互作用无关的求和规则；理论将该行为归因于强磁场下时间反演对称性破缺导致的单向手性输运，并指出纵向电阻源于接触处的电化学势平衡而非体态耗散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可压缩量子霍尔流体中手性金属相的输运规律，理解其普适电导行为背后的物理机制，并检验传统耗散图像在强磁场短程屏蔽体系中的适用性。

Method: 实验上测量源端与无反射接触之间连接可压缩量子霍尔流体的透射电导，并通过浮置接触测量确认手性输运；理论上论证在强磁场下，当屏蔽长度小于磁长度、势能涨落近乎平坦且存在点状无序时，载流子沿单向轨迹运动，从而推导透射电导求和规则。

Result: 观测到透射电导总和在整数与分数量子霍尔区都普适地等于 νe²/h，且不随温度、样品几何、材料质量和准粒子相互作用变化；该求和规则被确立为手性金属相的传导定律，并得到浮置接触实验对手性输运的验证。

Conclusion: 研究提出一种新的手性输运范式，适用于具有短程屏蔽和破缺时间反演对称性的宽泛无间隙二维体系；在此类体系中，纵向电阻不由体耗散主导，而是由接触处的电化学势均衡决定。

Abstract: We report transmitted conductance measurements between a source and reflection-less contacts connected to a compressible quantum Hall fluid with filling fraction $ν$. We observe that total sum of transmitted conductances universally approaches Hall conductance $ν(e^2/h)$. The universality of this sum rule is established experimentally across integer and fractional quantum Hall regimes, remaining invariant under variation in temperatures, sample geometries, material qualities and quasi-particle interactions. Chiral transport in compressible quantum Hall fluids, characterized by suppressed dissipative transport with a distinct handedness, is confirmed by floating contact measurements. Consequently, this sum rule emerges as a conduction law of the chiral metal phase. Theoretically, we argue that time-reversal-symmetry breaking under a strong magnetic field within this chiral metal gives rise unidirectional trajectories of carriers in a nearly flat potential landscape with point-like disorder potentials, a regime realized when the screening length is smaller than the magnetic length. Within this chiral framework, longitudinal resistance does not originate from bulk dissipation but primarily from the equilibration of electrochemical potentials at the contacts. Our study introduces a new paradigm of chiral transport across a broad class of gapless two-dimensional systems characterized by short-range screening and broken time-reversal symmetry.

</details>


### [111] [Topological Impurity Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09118)
*Arnab Seth,Itamar Kimchi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文发现无序中的局域缺陷可以诱导全新的拓扑相变，通过形成拓扑杂质带（TIB）使原本平庸的带绝缘体转变为拓扑非平庸相，而无需依赖传统拓扑安德森绝缘体（TAI）的长波无序平均机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为无序对拓扑相的作用主要是保持或破坏拓扑，且无序也能通过平均化长波随机性扩大已有拓扑相（TAI）。但基于微扰平均处理的方法可能忽略了局域缺陷带来的额外效应，因此需要探索有限密度随机分布缺陷是否能够产生新型拓扑相变。

Method: 作者提出轨道的缺陷束缚态涡旋（orbital-dependent vorticity of impurity bound states）机制，在狄拉克锥相变附近解析推导，并用Qi-Wu-Zhang和Haldane陈绝缘体晶格模型进行数值验证，在3%缺陷密度下计算相图。

Result: 数值相图显示在TIB和TAI区域均存在鲁棒的拓扑相变，证明局域缺陷可以生成不同于传统TAI的拓扑杂质带，并使初始平庸的带绝缘体进入拓扑相。

Conclusion: 揭示了无序诱导拓扑的新局域机制：有限密度局域缺陷即可驱动拓扑相变，拓展了无序拓扑相的概念和实现途径。

Abstract: The effects of disorder on topological phases of matter are typically either preservation of topology or its destruction. For example, the quantum Hall effect's quantization and chiral edge mode remain robust until a strong-disorder percolation transition exits the topological phase. Disorder can also enlarge a preexisting topological phase, yielding a topological Anderson insulator (TAI).
