However, the reliance of TAI and other theoretical treatments on perturbatively averaging out long-wavelength randomness suggests that additional effects might appear when the disorder involves localized defects. Here we show that a finite density of randomly distributed defects can generate various new topological phase transitions. This route to disorder-induced topology can be viewed as the formation of topological impurity bands (TIB) and is applicable even if the initial clean model is a trivial band insulator. The theory relies on an orbital-dependent vorticity of impurity bound states, which we derive analytically near a generic Dirac cone phase transition as well as using the Chern insulator lattice models of Qi-Wu-Zhang and Haldane. Their numerically computed phase diagrams at 3% defect density show robust topological transitions in distinct TIB and TAI regimes, suggesting a local route to topological phases.

</details>


### [112] [Three-Dimensional Fermiology and Thickness-Tuned Magnetotransport in Single-Crystalline Antimony Flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06561)
*Mikhail Gaponov,Jicheng Wang,Liang Zha,Rui Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过化学气相沉积生长的单晶Sb薄片（厚度110-783 nm）结合纵向/霍尔输运测量与温度及角度依赖的Shubnikov-de Haas振荡，发现厚度增加使14 T、2 K下的非饱和磁阻提升近30倍（达7.13×10^5%），而主振荡频率F_α保持约99 T不变；三通道联合分析表明该演化源于近补偿电子-空穴对迁移率增大而非费米面重构，角度依赖测量证实闭合三维α pocket及L点电子β轨道（335-377 T），说明厚度通过维度依赖散射调控极端磁阻但保持体相费米面拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿半金属的极端磁阻同时受费米面几何和载流子弛豫影响，但在有限尺寸结构中难以区分两者的贡献，亟需系统研究厚度如何分别作用于能带结构和散射机制。

Method: 对CVD生长的110-783 nm厚单晶Sb薄片进行纵向与霍尔磁输运测量，并开展温度及角度依赖的Shubnikov-de Haas振荡分析；进一步采用三通道联合拟合ρ_xx(B)和ρ_xy(B)，结合角分辨频率识别费米面轨道。

Result: 厚度从110增至783 nm时，2 K、14 T下磁阻从低值增长近30倍至7.13×10^5%，主频率F_α始终约99 T无系统移动；三通道分析显示演化由近补偿电子-空穴对迁移率升高主导，角度测量确认三维α闭合轨道及与体相Sb L点电子β轨道对应的高频（335-377 T）信号。

Conclusion: 厚度通过调节维度依赖的载流子散射来调控极端磁阻幅值，而不改变体相费米面拓扑，揭示了有限尺寸下输运与量子振荡结合可分离几何与弛豫贡献的研究范式。

Abstract: The extreme magnetoresistance of compensated semimetals is governed by both the Fermi-surface geometry and carrier relaxation, but these contributions are difficult to disentangle in finite-size structures. Here, we combine longitudinal and Hall magnetotransport measurements with temperature- and angle-dependent Shubnikov--de Haas oscillations in single-crystalline Sb flakes grown by chemical vapor deposition (CVD), with thicknesses ranging from 110 to 783 nm. As thickness increases, the non-saturating MR at 2 K and 14 T rises nearly 30-fold, reaching $7.13\times10^{5}\%$, while the primary frequency $F_α$ remains approximately 99 T without any systematic shift. A joint three-channel analysis of $ρ_{xx}(B)$ and $ρ_{xy}(B)$ reveals that this evolution is driven by an increase in the mobility of a nearly compensated electron--hole pair, rather than by a reconstruction of the primary pockets. Angle-dependent measurements confirm the existence of a closed three-dimensional $α$ pocket, and a reproducible high-frequency sector (335-377 T) is consistent with the electron $β$ orbit of the $L$-point pockets in bulk Sb. Together, the transport and quantum-oscillation results show that thickness tunes extreme MR through dimension-dependent scattering while preserving bulk-like fermiology.

</details>


### [113] [Evidence of Haldane-Chain Physics in an Fe-Dehydroindigo Coordination Polymer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06684)
*Ritam Chakraborty,Shobhana Narasimhan,T. V. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算与精确对角化/密度矩阵重正化群方法，发现顺式脱氢靛蓝铁配位聚合物链具有接近各向同性的反铁磁耦合（J_H=32.206 meV）与较弱单离子各向异性，其基态非简并且自旋激发隙约13–14 meV，与自旋-1 Haldane链特征一致，表明该孤立链可作为实现Haldane物理的配位聚合物平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不含衬底的本征配位聚合物是否能够支持Haldane链物理，特别是Fe-脱氢靛蓝自旋交叉配位聚合物是否具备合适的微观磁相互作用以实现自旋-1 Haldane隙。

Method: 采用自旋-轨道耦合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，结合磁能量映射提取磁交换参数；随后使用精确对角化和密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）方法计算基态、激发谱与动力学自旋结构因子，评估各向异性对低能模的影响。

Result: 得到近各向同性反铁磁耦合J_H=32.206 meV及很弱的单离子各向异性；基态非简并，与最低三重态激发之间存在约13–14 meV的能隙，与自旋-1 Haldane隙尺度一致；各向异性对低能模仅产生微小劈裂，最低谱权重位于反铁磁波矢q=π附近。

Conclusion: 孤立顺式脱氢靛蓝铁配位链的微观磁相互作用使其处于有能隙的Haldane型物理区域，可作为材料特异的配位聚合物平台用于研究Haldane链物理。

Abstract: We investigate whether an Fe--dehydroindigo spin-crossover coordination polymer can support Haldane-chain physics in its intrinsic, substrate-free limit. Spin-orbit-coupled density-functional-theory calculations and magnetic energy mapping yield a nearly isotropic antiferromagnetic coupling, $J_{\mathrm H}=32.206$~meV, together with much weaker single-ion anisotropy. Using these parameters, exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization group calculations give a nondegenerate ground state separated from the lowest triplet-derived excitations by a gap of about $13$--$14$~meV, on the scale expected for the spin-1 Haldane gap. The anisotropy produces only a small splitting of the low-energy modes. The dynamical spin structure factor places the lowest spectral weight near the antiferromagnetic wave vector $q=π$. These results identify the isolated cis-dehydroindigo chain as a material-specific coordination-polymer platform whose microscopic interactions place it in a gapped regime consistent with Haldane-chain physics.

</details>


### [114] [Self-Trapping Enabled Highly Bright Momentum-Indirect Interlayer Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06927)
*Dong Yang,Zisheng Gong,Hao Wen,Yue Hu,Kaichen Jiang,Baixu Xiang,Weibo Gao,Qihua Xiong,Dehui Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在二维钙钛矿与单层过渡金属二硫属化物形成的异质结构中，实现了高亮度动量间接层间激子发射，平均量子产率达35.2%，最高超过60%，其辐射复合效率比基于单层TMD的动量直接层间激子高两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间激子具有大束缚能和长寿命，但空间分离的电子和空穴使其振子强度比层内激子低两个数量级，难以实现高效率发射，需要优选材料并实现动量匹配和精细器件制备。

Method: 构筑二维钙钛矿/单层TMD异质结构，利用软晶格二维钙钛矿的强激子-声子耦合诱导层间激子自陷，实现动量间接但高效的辐射复合。

Result: 层间激子发射平均量子产率达35.2%，比对应TMD单层高50倍以上，最高超过60%；该动量间接激子的辐射复合效率比TMD异质结构中的动量直接激子高两个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过激子自陷实现高效层间激子发射的新机制，为长寿命激子器件和高效率激子发光提供了新途径。

Abstract: Interlayer excitons in two dimensional material heterostructures exhibit large exciton binding energies and long lifetimes, making them ideal platforms for studying excitonic devices and many body quantum phenomena. However, the spatially separated electron and hole nature of IXs reduces their oscillator strength by two orders of magnitude compared to intralayer excitons. Achieving high efficiency IX emission remains challenging and requires optimal material selection with appropriate momentum matching and meticulous device fabrication. Here we demonstrate a highly bright momentum indirect IX emission within heterostructures formed between 2D perovskites and monolayer transition metal dichalcogenides. The quantum yield of IX emission reaches 35.2% on average, over 50 times higher than that of the corresponding constituent TMD monolayer, with the highest value exceeding 60%. Notably, the radiative recombination efficiency of this momentum indirect IX exceeds that of momentum direct IXs in monolayer TMD-based heterostructures by two orders of magnitude. We suggest that the remarkably bright IX emission in our heterostructure originates from IX self trapping, induced by strong exciton phonon coupling arising from the soft lattice nature of the 2D perovskite. Our findings provide new insights into achieving high IX emission efficiency and open new avenues for exploring long lifetime excitonic devices.

</details>


### [115] [Theory of staggered damping-like spin-orbit torque on synthetic antiferromagnetic domain walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07055)
*Yuta Yamane,Takeshi Seki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种在对称重金属夹层合成反铁磁体中普遍存在的交错阻尼类自旋轨道矩理论，推导出畴壁速度闭合解，并预言其符号反转、标度律及Néel-Bloch转变阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Néel自旋轨道矩受限于特殊晶体结构且仅场类分量被证实，缺乏普遍可行的阻尼类交错矩机制，限制了反铁磁序的高效电学调控与畴壁运动研究。

Method: 从对称重金属夹层合成反铁磁结构出发，构建Néel矢量拉格朗日量，解析推导畴壁速度与电流、面内磁场及层间交换耦合的关系，并通过微磁模拟验证理论。

Result: 畴壁速度对电流和面内磁场呈双线性依赖，对Néel壁和Bloch壁符号相反，且可通过层间交换耦合调节；微磁模拟证实了符号反转、标度律及Néel-Bloch转变阈值。

Conclusion: 畴壁结构成为控制畴壁运动的旋钮，畴壁运动同时提供其结构的电学读出途径，为实验验证提供了明确可测的目标。

Abstract: The Néel, or staggered, spin-orbit torque (SOT) enables an efficient electrical manipulation of antiferromagnetic order. However, it has been tied to special crystal structures, and only its field-like component is established. We develop a theory of staggered damping-like SOT, which arises generically in synthetic antiferromagnets sandwiched by the same heavy metal on both sides. Deriving a Néel-vector Lagrangian, we obtain the domain-wall (DW) velocity in closed form: bilinear in current and in-plane field, opposite in sign for Néel and Bloch walls, and tunable through the interlayer exchange coupling. DW structure thus becomes a control knob for DW motion, while the DW motion, in turn, offers an electrical readout of its structure. Micromagnetic simulations confirm the theory. The predicted sign reversals and scaling laws of the DW velocity, together with the thresholds for the Néel-Bloch transition, give immediate experimental targets.

</details>


### [116] [Lindbladian Phase Geometry and Hall Transport in Open Bloch Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07228)
*Zhihao Jiang,Longjun Xiang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在Lindblad动力学理论框架下研究开放量子体系中量子几何输运，发现跳出传统Berry曲率框架的Lindblad相位几何可独立产生霍尔响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bloch系统输运中的量子几何效应已被广泛研究，但电子与环境耦合时，量子几何输运如何被修正仍不清楚。环境不仅改变电子态，还改变物理电流算符，因此需要从开放系统动力学出发重新建立响应理论。

Method: 作者在保迹Lindblad运动学理论中构建直流线性响应，将物理速度定义为Liouvillian时间导数下的位置算符。由此得到环境诱导的电流顶点修正，其动量空间旋度给出规范不变的相位几何，并区分来自非对角和对角跳变振幅的贡献，分别对应带间shift-vector和diagonal-vorticity响应。

Result: 在耗散耦合的最低阶下，Lindblad跳变振幅的相位几何产生两类新的霍尔响应。即使在传统Berry曲率反常霍尔效应完全为零的情况下，这两种机制仍可产生有限霍尔电导。

Conclusion: Lindblad相位几何是开放量子物质中横向输运的独立几何起源，拓展了量子几何输运的范畴，为环境中量子材料输运研究提供了新理论框架。

Abstract: Quantum geometry underlies a wide range of transport phenomena in Bloch systems. How quantum-geometric transport is modified when Bloch electrons are coupled to an environment, however, remains largely unexplored, even though the environment can alter both the electronic state and the physical current operator. Here we formulate dc linear response within a trace-preserving Lindblad kinetic theory by defining the physical velocity as the Liouvillian time derivative of the position operator. This construction reveals an environment-induced contribution to the current vertex whose momentum-space curl generates new Hall responses governed by the gauge-invariant phase geometry encoded by Lindblad jump amplitudes, which characterize electron-environment coupling. To leading order in the dissipative coupling, this geometry gives rise to interband shift-vector and diagonal-vorticity Hall responses arising from off-diagonal and diagonal jump amplitudes, respectively. Remarkably, both mechanisms can produce a finite Hall conductivity even when the conventional Berry-curvature anomalous Hall effect vanishes identically. Our work establishes Lindbladian phase geometry as an independent geometric origin of transverse transport in open quantum matter.

</details>


### [117] [Unveiling the Scaling Potential of Drain Merge through Active (DMtA) in CFETs: Breaking the Super-Via Bottlenecks and Unlocking New PPA Boosters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07329)
*Jingru Jiang,Haoran Lu,Kairong Guo,Yibo Zhang,Yifei Chen,Wanyue Peng,Yu Liu,Jiacheng Sun,Xiaoyan Xu,Ming Li,Yibo Lin,Runsheng Wang,Ru Huang,Heng Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 首次系统研究了一种名为Drain Merge through Active (DMtA)的新型CFET工艺技术，通过完整的DTCO流程证明其能显著降低寄生、提升频率、功耗和面积效率，并解锁超单元设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在互补FET（CFET）中，垂直连接堆叠n/pFET共享源漏端的Drain Merge (DM)结构会阻碍寄生优化和单元缩放，成为性能和PPA进一步提升的瓶颈。

Method: 构建了覆盖工艺集成、接触配置相关（CTCD）紧凑建模、标准单元设计、环振（RO）评估及32位RISC-V Ibex核块级PPA基准测试的综合DTCO框架，系统对比DMtA与传统Drain Merge through Field (DMtF)技术，并应用有源区扩大、横向空间借用、跨行有源区合并实现超单元，以及布局后浮空输出引脚感知优化。

Result: DMtA通过降低DM寄生和实现DM宽度优化使RO频率提升11.7%；有源区扩大与Area Borrowing使Ibex最大频率提升34.8%；跨行有源区合并使CFET实现GAA专属Hyper-cells并额外获得8.7%频率增益；浮空输出引脚感知优化移除冗余S/D接触使功耗降低5.3%；同时DMtA支持更面积高效的2.5T单元缩放并保持单行兼容性，使布局布线后核面积减少25.7%。

Conclusion: DMtA是一种可行的DM优化方案，能同时改善CFET的性能、功耗和面积，并为在CFET平台推广超单元和先进单元架构提供了新路径，是未来CFET技术发展的重要方向。

Abstract: Drain merge (DM), a super via vertically connecting the common S/D terminals of stacked n/pFETs in Complementary FETs (CFETs), blocks further parasitic optimization and cell scaling. For the first time, this work systematically investigates the state-of-the-art Drain Merge through Active (DMtA), a revolutionary technology reported recently with the DM embedded in the active region, through a comprehensive DTCO framework spanning process integration, contact-configuration-dependent (CTCD) compact modeling, standardcell design, RO evaluation and block-level PPA benchmark on a 32-bit RISC-V Ibex core. By reducing DM parasitics and enabling DM-width optimization, DMtA improves RO frequency by 11.7% over its conventional Drain Merge through field (DMtF) counterpart. Active widening and Area Borrowing, the latter first reported in [8] and exploiting spatial slack in adjacent cells to further enlarge the nanosheet width (WNS), increase the maximum Ibex-core frequency by up to 34.8%. More importantly, DMtA also enables the once GAA-exclusive Hyper-cells on CFETs by merging the active regions across adjacent cell rows, providing a further 8.7% frequency gain. A post-routing floating-output-pin-aware optimization further removes redundant S/D contacts (CTs) and reduces power by 5.3%. Finally, DMtA facilitates more area-efficient 2.5T cell scaling by preserving single-row cell compatibility, reducing post-PR core area by 25.7%.

</details>


### [118] [Trion-mediated quantum operations on a double-dot charge qubit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07342)
*Alexander Tsukanov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种在半导体非对称双量子点中控制电荷量子比特的方案，通过激光脉冲将逻辑单电子态转换为辅助三重态，借库仑相互作用补偿结构不对称性以恢复共振隧穿条件，实现高保真度、高速且可靠的正交单量子比特旋转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电荷量子比特受非对称双量子点中隧穿耦合抑制影响，难以实现高效可控的量子操作，需要一种能克服结构不对称、同时保证高速与高保真度的量子控制方案。

Method: 利用激光脉冲把量子比特的逻辑单电子态激发为辅助trion态，利用粒子间库仑相互作用抵消结构不对称造成的能级失配，重新满足共振隧穿条件；据此设计正交单量子比特旋转操作，并在二维结构中计算量子比特参数及激光场与耗散速率对保真度的影响。

Result: 给出了实现正交单量子比特旋转所需的条件；针对二维结构获得了具体量子比特参数，并得到反转操作保真度对激光场参数和耗散速率的依赖关系；结果表明该算法具有高速度和高可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于辅助trion态与库仑补偿机制的方案可有效控制非对称双量子点电荷量子比特，兼顾高速与高保真度，具有实际可行性。

Abstract: A scheme for controlling a charge qubit on a semiconductor asymmetric double quantum dot with suppressed tunnel coupling between individual quantum dots is proposed. Laser pulses convert the logical single-electron states of the qubit into auxiliary trion states, for which the resonant tunneling condition is restored due to the compensation of structural asymmetry by the Coulomb interaction of the particles. The conditions for performing orthogonal single-qubit rotations are formulated. For a two-dimensional structure, the parameters of the qubit are calculated, and the dependence of the fidelity of the inversion operation on the parameters of the laser field and the rates of dissipative processes is obtained. It is shown that the considered algorithm is characterized by high speed and reliability.

</details>


### [119] [Static-Dynamic Correlations and Complex Spin-Wave Eigenmodes in Single- and Multilayer Diamond-Shaped Nanomagnets Without Bias Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07368)
*Krishna Begari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用微磁模拟系统研究了单层与多层菱形纳米磁体的静态和动态磁行为，发现通过简单磁场初始化可获得两种不同的剩磁构型，并可用纳秒级磁场脉冲调控其共振特性，实现可重构微波操作。多层结构因垂直层间偶极耦合增强，共振频移达3 GHz，远大于单层结构的0.9 GHz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有可重构特性的微波器件需要简单有效的纳米磁体平台。菱形纳米磁体具有潜力，但对其静磁构型、动态响应以及多层耦合如何影响频率调谐性仍需系统研究。

Method: 采用微磁模拟方法，分别对单层和多层菱形纳米磁体进行静态与动态磁行为分析；通过简单的磁场初始化流程获得两种不同的剩磁磁化构型；施加纳秒级磁场脉冲，比较不同构型及单层/多层结构的共振频率变化。

Result: 两种剩磁构型表现出不同的磁化图案和动态响应。单层结构出现亚GHz范围（0.9 GHz）的清晰共振频移，多层结构则呈现显著更大的GHz范围（3 GHz）频移。多层结构频率可调性增强主要源于垂直耦合磁层之间偶极耦合增强，改变了局域有效磁场并影响磁化动力学。

Conclusion: 菱形纳米磁体为可重构微波功能提供了一条简单有效的实现路径，有望应用于超低功耗、超快及频率可调的微波器件。

Abstract: Diamond-shaped nanomagnets provide a suitable platform for developing microwave devices with reconfigurable characteristics. In this study, the static and dynamic magnetic behaviour of single layer and multilayer diamond-shaped nanomagnets was systematically investigated using micromagnetic simulations. Two distinct remanent magnetic configurations were obtained through a simple magnetic field initialization process. These configurations exhibited different magnetization patterns and dynamic responses. Their resonance characteristics could be modified by applying a nanosecond-scale magnetic field pulse, enabling reconfigurable microwave operation. A clear resonance frequency shift in the sub-GHz (0.9 GHz) range was observed for the single-layer structure, while the multilayer structure exhibited a significantly larger frequency shift in the GHz range (3 GHz). The larger frequency tunability observed in the multilayer configuration arises primarily from the enhanced dipolar coupling between the vertically coupled magnetic layers, which modifies the local effective magnetic field and consequently influences the magnetization dynamics. These findings demonstrate that diamond-shaped nanomagnets can provide a simple and effective route toward reconfigurable microwave functionality, with potential applications in ultralow-power, ultrafast, and frequency tunable microwave devices.

</details>


### [120] [Layer-Dependent Phonons, Excitons, and Magneto-Optical Phenomena in CrSBr: A Mini Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07425)
*Muhammad Aftab,Chinmay Kumar Mohanty,Zain Ashfaq,Warisha Mehmood,Xiaoli Wang,Clément Faugeras,Wajid Ali,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文综述了二维层状磁性材料CrSBr从体材料到单层极限的层数依赖振动与激子性质、磁光响应及自旋-声子/激子耦合，并探讨其在自旋电子学、磁光与量子光子技术中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维层状磁性材料为研究低维磁性和多体相互作用提供了平台，而CrSBr兼具空气稳定性、半导体性、强面内各向异性和A型反铁磁序，是先进自旋电子与光电器件的理想候选，但需系统理解其层数依赖的物理性质。

Method: 回顾了CrSBr的晶体结构、磁各向异性和层间自旋重取向，分析了振动动力学与自旋-声子耦合，重点研究了激子景观，包括磁场敏感光致发光、局域激子态以及Frenkel和Wannier-Mott激子共存现象。

Result: CrSBr表现出明显的层数依赖振动和激子行为，激子对磁场敏感，存在局域激子态，且带隙中Frenkel与Wannier-Mott激子共存，磁光响应丰富。

Conclusion: 尽管CrSBr展现出独特应用潜力，但实现其在自旋电子、磁光和量子光子技术中的利用仍面临挑战，未来需进一步调控层数依赖特性并探索集成器件。

Abstract: Two-dimensional layered magnetic materials offer a versatile platform for exploring low-dimensional magnetism and coupled many-body interactions in these materials. Chromium sulfur bromide (CrSBr) is a promising candidate for advanced spintronic and optoelectronic applications because of its intrinsic air stability, semiconducting nature, strong in-plane anisotropy, and A-type antiferromagnetic ordering. This review summarizes recent advances in the understanding of the layer-dependent vibrational and excitonic properties of CrSBr, as well as its magneto-optical response, from bulk crystals to the monolayer limit. We examined its crystal structure, magnetic anisotropy, and interlayer spin reorientation, followed by insights into vibrational dynamics and spin-phonon coupling. Particular emphasis is placed on the excitonic landscape, including magnetic-field-sensitive photoluminescence, localized excitonic states, and the coexistence of Frenkel- and Wannier-Mott excitons in the bandgap. Finally, we discuss the challenges and prospects of harnessing the unique layer-dependent properties of CrSBr in spintronic, magneto-optical, and quantum photonic technologies.

</details>


### [121] [Charge-spin conversion in altermagnets: electronic anisotropy versus magnon-mediated spin drag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07426)
*Koki Mizuno*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过统一模型计算铁磁、反铁磁和交变磁体的自旋分辨电导，发现电荷-自旋转换的各向异性主要由电子自旋劈裂主导，磁振子贡献很小；磁振子介导的自旋翻转通道在C4对称性下为偶，不能一阶区分交变磁体与常规反铁磁体，但对降低B1g对称性的子晶格选择性微扰呈现唯一响应，可用于无需单畴样品的交变磁体探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体具有无净磁化或自旋轨道耦合的动量依赖自旋劈裂，产生了一个关键问题：输运各向异性源于电子还是磁振子？以及磁振子介导的通道能否将交变磁体与常规反铁磁体区分开？

Method: 在一个统一模型中，对金属铁磁体、反铁磁体和交变磁体计算自旋分辨电导：自旋对角通道用磁振子自能处理，自旋翻转通道则处理到电子-磁振子耦合的领头阶。

Result: 电荷-自旋转换的各向异性主要来自电子自旋劈裂，磁振子支贡献很小。磁振子介导的自旋翻转通道在C4下为偶，故一阶各向异性无信号，不能单独区分交变磁体与常规反铁磁体；但它对降低体B1g对称性的子晶格选择性微扰呈线性响应，该磁化率在反铁磁体中恒为零、铁磁体中很小、交变磁体中有限，且对磁畴指标为偶。

Conclusion: 在无净磁化的磁体中，该响应为交变磁序所独有，且无需制备单磁畴样品即可测量，提供了一种区分交变磁体与常规反铁磁体的可行方案。

Abstract: Altermagnets acquire a momentum-dependent spin splitting without net magnetization or spin-orbit coupling.
  This raises the question of whether the transport anisotropy originates in the electrons or the magnons, and whether the magnon-mediated channel distinguishes an altermagnet from a conventional antiferromagnet.
  We compute the spin-resolved conductivity of a metallic ferromagnet, antiferromagnet, and altermagnet within a common model, treating the spin-diagonal channel with the magnon self-energy and the spin-flip channel to leading order in the electron-magnon coupling.
  The anisotropy of the charge-spin conversion is dominated by the electronic spin splitting, the magnon branches contributing only marginally.
  The magnon-mediated spin-flip channel is even under $C_{4}$, so it carries no signature at first order in the anisotropy and cannot on its own distinguish an altermagnet from a conventional antiferromagnet.
  It does, however, respond linearly to a sublattice-selective perturbation that lowers the bulk $B_{1g}$ symmetry.
  The resulting susceptibility vanishes identically in the antiferromagnet, is small in the ferromagnet, and is finite in the altermagnet, where the electronic anisotropy ties spin and sublattice together, so that among magnets with no net magnetization it is unique to altermagnetic order.
  Being even in the magnetic domain index, this response can be measured without preparing a single magnetic-domain sample.

</details>


### [122] [Direct Realization of Near-Ideal Carbyne in Ultrathin Boron Nitride Nanotubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07443)
*Iryna Ivanenko,Pietro Marabotti,Yifan Zhang,Getulio Silva e Souza Júnior,Johannes M. A. Lechner,Pablo Hernández López,Martin Magg,Shivani Shivaprakash,Carlo Spartaco Casari,Sebastian Heeg,Benjamin S. Flavel*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在超薄氮化硼纳米管内封装并热转化氢封端多炔，制备出近理想态的卡宾链，其振动频率分布窄一个数量级，非谐性符合卡宾类材料的普适规律，键长交替与自由链的相关计算吻合，且无光致发光，表明首次实验上接近本征卡宾性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡宾被预测为最刚硬的已知材料，其电子和光学性质由单一结构参数（键长交替）决定，但本征性质从未被直接测量：通过分子化学合成的链存在端基和有限长度扰动，而在碳纳米管内生长的链与宿主强耦合，使振动频率发生高达110 cm⁻¹的直径依赖重整化。

Method: 将氢封端多炔封装入超薄氮化硼纳米管（结构类似碳纳米管但电绝缘），并通过热转化使其形成卡宾链；随后采用统计拉曼光谱（245个位置）测量振动频率分布、非谐性和键长交替，并检测光致发光。

Result: 振动频率分布比碳纳米管中窄一个数量级；非谐性与卡宾类材料的普适定律一致；键长交替匹配自由链的相关计算；未检测到光致发光，符合偶极禁戒发射的预期。

Conclusion: 氮化硼纳米管提供了实验上接近理想态的卡宾链，使端基、有限长度和宿主-客体扰动降至次要效应，从而首次实现对其近本征性质的实验测量。

Abstract: Carbyne, the sp-hybridized one-dimensional allotrope of carbon, is predicted to be the stiffest known material, with electronic and optical properties set by a single structural parameter, the bond length alternation. However, its intrinsic properties have never been measured: chains synthesized through molecular chemistry carry endgroup and finite-length perturbations that persist even in the longest molecules available, while chains grown inside carbon nanotubes strongly couple to the host, which renormalizes their vibrational frequency by up to 110 cm$^{-1}$ in a diameter-dependent manner. Here, we show that encapsulating and thermally converting hydrogen-capped polyynes inside ultrathin boron nitride nanotubes, structural analogues of carbon nanotubes but electrically insulating, yields carbyne chains in a near-ideal regime, where endgroup, finite length, and host-guest perturbations are reduced to secondary effects. Statistical Raman spectroscopy across 245 locations returns a vibrational frequency distribution an order of magnitude narrower than in carbon nanotubes, an anharmonicity consistent with the universal law for carbyne-like materials, and a bond length alternation matching correlated calculations for the free chain. No photoluminescence is detected, despite the transparent host, as expected for the dipole-forbidden emission of an unperturbed carbyne chain. Boron nitride nanotubes give experimental access to carbyne in its near-ideal form.

</details>


### [123] [From London to Morse via Binnig, Quate, and Gerber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08425)
*Sofia Alonso Perez,Matthew O. Blunt,Frederick Carlisle,Neil R. Champness,Janette L. Dunn,Matthew Edmondson,Rowan Evers,Connor Fields,Subhashis Gangopadhyay,James Hayton,Samuel P. Jarvis,Filipe Junqueira,Lev Kantorovich,Brian Kiraly,Natalio Krasnogor,Ioannis Lekkas,Morten Møller,Philip Moriarty,Chris Pakes,Emmanuelle Pauliac-Vaujour,Oliver Phillips,Adrian Radocea,Philipp Rahe,Mohammad Abdur Rashid,Hongqian Sang,Alex Saywell,Nikhil Seeja Sivakumar,Peter Sharp,Andrew Stannard,Julian Stirling,Adam Sweetman,Simon Taylor,Richard A. J. Woolley*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文是纪念原子力显微镜问世四十周年的前瞻性综述，回顾了诺丁汉团队二十年中围绕“探针作为主动参与者而非被动观察者”这一主题的研究成果，涵盖从范德华力、氢键到共价键乃至单化学键极限的力谱测量，并延伸至原子级金属团簇组装、扩散动力学中的针尖扰动效应以及机器学习在针尖驱动操纵中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFM自1986年发明以来，其探针-样品间的力不仅本身可被测量，也普遍存在于扫描隧道显微镜并影响其结果。作者希望借四十周年特刊，系统总结团队长期积累的证据，说明探针的主动参与如何贯穿从弱范德华力到强共价键的完整相互作用谱，并强调理解针尖作用对于非侵入性测量和原子尺度操纵的重要性。

Method: 采用前瞻性综述的形式，遴选诺丁汉团队二十年的代表性实验工作；这些工作在方法上以原子力显微镜和扫描隧道显微镜为核心，结合力的定量测量、单分子/单键尺度实验、垂直针尖-样品原子转移技术，以及针对吸附分子扩散的动力学测量，并讨论了机器学习在自动化和智能操纵中的潜在应用。

Result: 结果显示探针能够敏感地跟踪从范德华/Hamaker力、氢键到共价键的连续力谱，并在单化学键极限下实现探测；同时通过垂直针尖-样品转移实现了金属团簇的原子级逐原子组装。此外还发现针尖诱导的不均匀性会显著影响吸附分子的首过动力学扩散测量，揭示了传统测量可能带来的扰动，而机器学习则展现出自动化完成复杂原子操纵的前景。

Conclusion: 作者认为，将探针视为主动参与者而非被动观察者，不仅有助于理解AFM/STM中无处不在的针尖作用力，也为在从微观到单键的尺度上主动设计和操纵物质提供了可能；未来机器学习将推动这一领域向更自动化、更高精度的方向发展，但实现非侵入式测量仍是重要的挑战。

Abstract: In their landmark paper introducing the atomic force microscope [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{56}, 930 (1986)], Binnig, Quate, and Gerber presciently anticipated that the technique would ultimately be capable of probing interactions running the gamut from weak van der Waals interactions to strong covalent bonding. They also highlighted that the tip-sample forces central to AFM are present, and often highly influential, in scanning tunnelling microscopy; indeed, this realisation directly inspired the invention of the force microscope. In this perspective for the \textit{Forty Years of AFM} special issue, we review selected aspects of two decades of work from our group at the University of Nottingham that span the force range highlighted by BQG and are united by a common, central theme: the probe as active participant rather than passive observer. Our selection of results also covers length- and correlation-scales from the microscopic right down to the single chemical bond limit, tracking a spectrum of interactions from van der Waals/Hamaker forces, through hydrogen bonding, to covalent bonds and, finally, atom-by-atom assembly of metal clusters via vertical tip-sample transfer. Echoing BQG's own observations on the prevalence of probe-sample forces in STM, we also discuss recent evidence that tip-induced heterogeneity underpins first-passage dynamics in molecular diffusion and highlight the challenges in acquiring non-invasive measurements of diffusion barriers for adsorbed molecules that are readily perturbed by the probe. We close with a perspective on machine learning's growing role in automating tip-driven atomic and molecular manipulation.

</details>


### [124] [Encapsulated macroscopic WS$_2$ monolayers enable room-temperature exciton-polariton lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07587)
*Shiyu Huang,Sander Scheel,Jiang Qu,Johannes Düreth,Dominik Horneber,Edith Wietek,Simon Widmann,Libo Ma,Monika Emmerling,Martin Kamp,Simon Betzold,Alexey Chernikov,Sven Höfling,Sebastian Klembt*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文实现基于1-十二醇封装的毫米级WS₂单层膜，在开放微腔中展示室温激子-光子强耦合（Rabi劈裂约31 meV），并进一步构建二维极化激元kagome晶格，通过角分辨光谱与实空间成像解析出狄拉克色散和近平带及其对应的模式分布，确立大面积单层TMD作为可扩展室温极化激元平台的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械剥离的单层半导体面积小、光学不均匀且器件间差异大，严重阻碍了可扩展室温极化激元学及跨越多原胞的极化激元晶格研究；需要一种大面积且光学均匀的单层半导体平台来构建可扩展的极化激元能带结构与合成量子材料。

Method: 采用1-十二醇封装的WS₂单层，获得毫米级覆盖面积并在约300 μm横向距离上保持高度均匀的光学特性；将该单层集成到可调开放微腔中，实现室温激子-光子强耦合；利用该均匀平台制作二维极化激元kagome晶格，结合角分辨光谱、动量空间与实空间成像测量其能带结构和模式分布，并与线性无相互作用模型对比。

Result: 在室温下观察到明显的反交叉行为，Rabi劈裂约为31 meV；二维极化激元kagome晶格的s带中解析出狄拉克型色散能带和弱色散的类平带分支，测量结果与线性无相互作用模型吻合；动量空间和实空间成像进一步识别出对应的键中心和格点中心模式轮廓。

Conclusion: 大面积均匀WS₂单层与开放微腔的结合为室温下工程化极化激元能带结构提供了可扩展的实验平台，并为环境条件下探索合成量子材料铺平了道路。

Abstract: Large-area, optically homogeneous monolayer semiconductors are a critical prerequisite for scalable room-temperature polaritonics and for realizing polariton lattices extending across many unit cells. Yet, the small size, optical inhomogeneity, and device-to-device variability of conventional exfoliated flakes have remained major obstacles. Here, we overcome these limitations using 1-dodecanol-encapsulated WS$_2$ monolayers that combine millimeter-scale coverage with remarkably uniform optical properties over lateral distances approaching $300\,$$μ\mathrm{m}$. Integrated into a tunable open microcavity, these monolayers exhibit robust room-temperature exciton-photon strong coupling, evidenced by a pronounced anti-crossing and a Rabi splitting of $\hbar Ω_{\mathrm{R}} \approx 31\,\mathrm{meV}$. Leveraging the exceptional uniformity of this platform, we realize a two-dimensional polaritonic kagome lattice and directly resolve its characteristic band structure. Angle-resolved spectroscopy reveals Dirac dispersive bands together with a weakly dispersive flat-band-like branch within the $s$-band, in good agreement with a linear non-interacting model. Complementary momentum- and real-space imaging further identifies the associated bond-centered and site-centered mode profiles. These results establish large-area WS$_2$ monolayers in open microcavities as a scalable platform for engineering polariton band structures and exploring synthetic quantum materials under ambient conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Metal-Insulator Coexistence and Gap-Crossing Domain-Wall Modes in an Aubry-André Model with Nonlocal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07704)
*Xiarui Zhan,Mingsheng Tian,Qiongyi He,Kaiye Shi,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在具有系统尺度跳跃（非局域跳跃范围与系统尺寸成正比）的扩展Aubry-André模型中发现了金属-绝缘体共存相：同一系统内金属与绝缘空间区域共存并被尖锐边界分隔；绝缘区在无准周期势时呈现平带型局域化，有准周期势时则产生不同于传统指数局域化的多点局域化；粒子可在被绝缘区分隔的金属区域间自由传播与隧穿，并存在跨越能隙的畴壁模，其梳状空间分布支持跨分隔金属区域的非局域多点输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡输运是连接凝聚态物理与合成系统的重要主题，而扩展Aubry-André模型中非局域跳跃对局域化与输运的影响尚未被充分理解，尤其是系统尺度跳跃如何改变传统的金属-绝缘体转变图景。

Method: 作者研究了一个带有系统尺度跳跃（跳跃范围随系统大小线性增长）的扩展Aubry-André模型，分析其空间实空间的局域化性质、能谱结构以及输运行为，并与传统指数局域化和平带局域化对比。

Result: 发现了一个金属-绝缘体共存相，其中金属和绝缘区域被尖锐边界分开；绝缘区在无准周期势时为平带局域化，在准周期势下呈现多位置局域化；金属区之间的粒子可自由传播并隧穿绝缘区；同时识别出具有梳状剖面、跨越能隙的畴壁模，能介导分离金属区域之间的非局域多点输运。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了非局域跳跃系统中局域化、非局域性与输运之间丰富的相互作用，拓展了对金属-绝缘体转变和非平衡输运的理解。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium transport remains a central theme in modern physics, spanning from condensed matter to synthetic systems. Here, we investigate particle transport in an extended Aubry-André model with system-scale hopping, namely nonlocal hopping with a range proportional to the system size, and uncover a metal-insulator coexistence regime in real space, where metallic and insulating spatial domains coexist within the same system and are separated by sharp spatial boundaries. In the insulating region, particles exhibit flat-band-like localization in the absence of quasiperiodic potentials, while a quasiperiodic potential induces distinct multi-point localization, different from conventional exponential localization. Meanwhile, particles can freely propagate and tunnel across spatially disconnected metallic domains separated by the insulating region. Beyond this coexistence phase, we identify unconventional gap-crossing domain-wall modes with comb-like spatial profiles that mediate nonlocal, multi-point transport across separated metallic domains. Our findings reveal a rich interplay between localization, nonlocality, and transport in systems with nonlocal hopping.

</details>


### [126] [Creation and characterization of leviton excitations in tight-binding chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07820)
*Stephen R. McMillan,Thomas Frederiksen,Géza Giedke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究有限一维紧束缚链中非相互作用费米子由洛伦兹电压脉冲驱动的leviton产生，发现干净类leviton态仅存在于中间脉冲速度区间；洛伦兹脉冲即使被截断也优于非洛伦兹形状，并识别了有限晶格中能带填充、脉冲幅度、线性电压降几何及非整数电荷条件残留偏差等效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levitons是电子量子光学的关键资源，但以往理论多基于连续散射描述；而实际集成电子量子电路平台往往是有限、离散且受晶格色散影响的。因此需要理解leviton在有限紧束缚晶格中的形成条件和质量，以评估这类格点平台的可行性。

Method: 采用非相互作用费米子的一维紧束缚链模型，施加含时电压脉冲；通过单粒子密度矩阵计算费米海之上的激发，并用平均激发数及其涨落量化leviton质量，系统比较不同脉冲形状与参数条件。

Result: 本文研究有限一维紧束缚链中非相互作用费米子由洛伦兹电压脉冲驱动的leviton产生，发现干净类leviton态仅存在于中间脉冲速度区间；洛伦兹脉冲即使被截断也优于非洛伦兹形状，并识别了有限晶格中能带填充、脉冲幅度、线性电压降几何及非整数电荷条件残留偏差等效应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了超越理想连续极限的leviton形成的微观框架，为评估基于格点的相干少电子输运平台提供了理论基础，并指明了实际系统中实现高质量leviton的参数约束。

Abstract: Levitons are minimal-excitation electronic wave packets generated by Lorentzian voltage pulses and constitute a central resource for electron quantum optics. Their creation and defining properties are usually formulated in continuum scattering descriptions, whereas many candidate platforms for integrated electronic quantum circuits are finite, discrete, and strongly shaped by lattice dispersion. We study leviton generation in finite one-dimensional tight-binding chains of non-interacting fermions driven by time-dependent voltage pulses. Using the single-particle density matrix, we resolve the excitation above the initial Fermi sea and quantify its quality through the average excitation number and its fluctuations. We find that clean leviton-like states emerge only in an intermediate regime where the pulse is slow enough to be resolved by the dynamics on the lattice, but not so slow that truncation and finite-size effects distort the Lorentzian profile in the time domain. Lorentzian pulses (even if truncated) systematically outperform non-Lorentzian pulse shapes in approaching the low-noise limit with increasing system size. We further identify finite-lattice signatures associated with band filling, pulse amplitude, linear voltage-drop geometry, and residual deviations from the continuum integer-charge condition. These results establish a microscopic framework for understanding leviton formation beyond the ideal continuum limit and for evaluating lattice-based platforms for coherent few-electron transport.

</details>


### [127] [Steady reservoir-mediated self-trapping of polariton condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07962)
*Pavel N. Kozhevin,Oleg I. Utesov,Igor S. Aranson,Sergey V. Koniakhin,Anton V. Nalitov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究表明，非平衡激子极化激元玻色凝聚体在非相干光泵浦下，可通过凝聚体对激子库的背向作用引发局域库耗尽，形成自诱导势阱中的稳态凝聚体。该凝聚体由持续汇聚的极化激元流构成，而非通常的 ballistic 膨胀态，且可在传统凝聚阈值以下存在，并在纳秒尺度后因子弹型不稳定性被射出，可用于脉冲神经形态动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论预测紧聚焦非相干光泵浦产生的激子极化激元凝聚体通常形成稳态 ballistic 膨胀态，但实验观察到紧凑自俘获态，其形成机制尚不明确，需要理论解释。

Method: 理论分析凝聚体与光泵浦激子库之间通过玻色子受激散射的背向作用，建立包含库耗尽和自诱导势阱的模型，研究稳态解及其稳定性。

Result: 发现库耗尽可产生自诱导势阱，使凝聚体形成自俘获稳态，具有持续汇聚的粒子流；该态低于常规凝聚阈值仍存在，并在纳秒尺度后因子弹型不稳定性发生喷射。

Conclusion: 该机制解释了实验观察到的自俘获态，并为极化激元凝聚体网络中的脉冲神经形态动力学提供了新的物理基础。

Abstract: Nonequilibrium bosonic condensates of exciton-polaritons generated by tightly focused incoherent optical beams are typically expected to form a stationary ballistically expanding state with the notable exception of experimentally observed compact self-trapped states [Phys. Rev. Lett. 123, 047401 (2019)]. We show that the back-action of the condensate on the optically pumped excitonic reservoir via bosonic stimulated scattering can cause local reservoir depletion, producing a steady condensate confined in a self-induced potential trap. Notably, this self-trapped state is formed by persistent converging rather than radially expanding polariton currents and exists even below the conventionally defined condensation threshold. This state remains steady over nanosecond-scale times and is eventually ejected from the self-induced trap due to a bullet-type instability, enabling spiking neuromorphic dynamics in networks of polariton condensates.

</details>


### [128] [Nonlinear Topological Orbital Responses of Antiferromagnetic Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08022)
*Amir N. Zarezad,Arne Brataas,Alireza Qaiumzadeh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在无自旋轨道耦合的PT对称六角反铁磁体中，预测了孤立斯格明子产生的非线性拓扑轨道响应：扩散弱涌现场下，自旋依赖的涌现洛伦兹力导致载流子分布重塑，产生与电场二次方成正比的局域轨道霍尔电流修正和局域轨道积累；前者需自旋不对称纵向散射，后者在自旋对称散射下仍存在，二者均对电场反向为偶、对拓扑荷Q反向为奇，且与手性无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁斯格明子虽可规避斯格明子霍尔效应，但其补偿特性抑制了传统拓扑电荷霍尔信号，亟需寻找新的可探测拓扑响应机制。

Method: 作者在无自旋轨道耦合的PT对称六角反铁磁体中，针对孤立斯格明子采用半经典非线性响应理论，在扩散区弱涌现场条件下，分析自旋依赖的涌现洛伦兹力对载流子分布的影响，推导局域轨道霍尔电流修正与局域轨道积累，并讨论自旋散射对称性、自旋扩散长度与条带宽度关系以及电场/拓扑荷对称性。

Result: 局域轨道电流修正正比于电场平方，需要自旋不对称纵向散射；局域轨道积累同样为电场二次效应，但在自旋对称散射下也能存活。当自旋扩散长度远大于条带宽度时，电流修正保持非零而积累趋近于零。二者对电场反向不变，对拓扑荷Q反向变号，且在此模型内与斯格明子手性无关。

Conclusion: 该非线性机制呈现电场反向偶、Q反向奇的对称性，可实现整流探测，并能与量子区Q为偶的拓扑轨道霍尔效应明确区分，为反铁磁斯格明子提供了一种无需自旋轨道耦合的新型电探测途径。

Abstract: Antiferromagnetic skyrmions evade the skyrmion Hall effect, but compensation suppresses their conventional topological charge Hall signal. We predict a semiclassical nonlinear topological orbital response of an isolated skyrmion in a $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric hexagonal antiferromagnet without spin-orbit coupling. In the diffusive, weak-emergent-field regime, the spin-dependent emergent Lorentz force reshapes the carrier distribution, generating a local orbital Hall-current correction and a local orbital accumulation, both quadratic in the applied electric field. The current correction requires spin-asymmetric longitudinal scattering, whereas the accumulation survives spin-symmetric scattering. When the spin-diffusion length greatly exceeds the strip width, the current correction persists while the accumulation approaches zero. Both signals are even under electric-field reversal, odd under reversal of the skyrmion topological charge $Q$, and helicity independent within this model. These symmetries enable rectified detection and distinguish this mechanism from the $Q$-even quantum-regime topological orbital Hall effect.

</details>


### [129] [Edge energy fluctuation: A unified theory of friction scaling laws in twisted layered material interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08063)
*Ze Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出边缘能量波动（EEF）概念，证明其决定有限尺寸滑块在扭曲范德华界面上的滑动能垒，并展示通过设计边缘方向与层级边缘可实现摩擦调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扭曲范德华层状材料中的莫尔超晶格可调控电子与力学性能，但有限尺寸滑块滑动能垒的起源尚不清楚，尤其边缘效应的作用未被揭示。

Method: 基于两个关键洞察——完整莫尔瓦片滑动时势能不变，以及滑块边缘引起的对称性破缺——提出EEF概念，并分析不同边缘切割方向、固定扭曲角和滑动方向下的摩擦行为。

Result: 在固定扭曲角和滑动方向下，滑块边缘方向不同会产生显著的摩擦各向异性，导致不同的摩擦标度律；EEF主导滑动能垒。

Conclusion: 提出了通过有意引入层级边缘来工程化扭曲层状界面摩擦的范式，为超滑器件中的摩擦控制提供了新路径。

Abstract: Interlayer twisting in van der Waals (vdW) layered materials generates moire superlattices, unlocking unique opportunities for engineering their electronic and mechanical functionalities. Here, building on two key insights - the invariance of the potential energy of complete moire tiles during sliding, and the symmetry-breaking effect induced by the slider edge - we propose the concept of edge energy fluctuation (EEF) and demonstrate that the EEF governs the sliding energy barrier of finite-sized sliders. Interestingly, we found that even at a fixed twist angle and sliding direction, significant frictional anisotropy emerges when the slider edge is cut along different directions, resulting in distinct friction scaling laws. Furthermore, we present a friction engineering paradigm for twisted layered material interfaces through the deliberate introduction of hierarchical edges, offering a promising route for friction control in superlubric devices.

</details>


### [130] [Electronic Orbital Angular Momentum Driven by Finite-Momentum Phonons: Beyond Chiral and Axial Phonons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08100)
*Yongho Park,Jeonghun Sohn,Sejoong Kim,Kyoung-Whan Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文证明即使无手性或轴向性的声子，只要具有有限动量，也能通过规范场机制产生电子轨道角动量（OAM），其响应由声子波矢 q 与频率 ω 控制，并建立了 AC/DC 响应的标度律及与电子能带几何的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注手性/轴向声子驱动电子 OAM，但未澄清声子角动量是否唯一决定电子 OAM。作者希望探索更普适的机制，并建立可调谐的轨道电子学方案，尤其是可用表面声波实现的波矢调控路径。

Method: 发展了一般规范场理论，通过幺正变换将声子位移吸收为作用于电子的规范势和标量势，从而在 q 和 ω 下对 OAM 响应做微扰分类；推导了 AC 和 DC 响应的标度律，并用含时紧束缚模拟独立验证。

Result: 发现线性极化声子在 AC 条件下也能产生 OAM，且大小和符号可由 q 调谐；产生条件取决于声子几何与电子轨道纹理的匹配，而非仅由声子角动量决定；DC 响应与电子贝里曲率关联，进一步说明声子角动量不是唯一自由度。

Conclusion: 该工作将声子驱动的电子 OAM 扩展到非手性、非轴向声子，建立了基于波矢可调的轨道电子学途径，为利用表面声波实现相关效应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We show that finite-momentum phonons generate electronic orbital angular momentum (OAM) even without phonon axiality or chirality, with the response controlled by the phonon wave vector $q$ and frequency $ω$. We develop a general gauge-field theory in which a unitary transformation absorbs the phonon displacement into emergent vector and scalar potentials acting on the electrons, providing a perturbative classification of the OAM response in $q$ and $ω$. We derive $q$- and $ω$-scaling laws for AC and DC responses. Notably, AC OAM arises even for linearly polarized phonons, with its magnitude and sign tunable by $q$. Its generation is governed by the matching between the phonon geometry and the electronic orbital texture rather than solely by the phonon angular momentum. The connection of the DC response to the electronic Berry curvature further supports that phonon angular momentum is not the only degree of freedom governing electronic OAM generation. Time-dependent tight-binding simulations under acoustic-phonon driving independently confirm the core predictions. Our results extend phonon-driven OAM beyond chiral and axial phonons and establish a wave-vector-tunable route to orbitronics, accessible with surface acoustic waves.

</details>


### [131] [Tunable topological narrow bands in twisted bilayer-trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08104)
*Dong Wang,Federico Escudero,Zhen Zhan,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过紧束缚与连续模型系统研究了四种堆叠构型的扭转型双层-三层石墨烯，揭示了平带拓扑随扭转角、电场及Hartree势的转变规律，并构建了可调Chern数的拓扑相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扭转型多层石墨烯中平带拓扑的调控机制，尤其是堆叠构型、扭转角和电场如何协同影响窄带及其拓扑性质，为可调Chern数拓扑平带工程提供理论指导。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型和连续模型对比计算低能带结构；系统扫描扭转角、垂直电场和自洽Hartree势，计算平带谷Chern数；结合手性极限的Chern数和规则分析拓扑转变；构建扭转角-电场空间的拓扑相图。

Result: 两种模型在低能区吻合良好；较大扭转角与电场下出现平带间拓扑转变，总Chern数符合手性极限求和规则；平带与远程带杂化时发生另一类拓扑转变并伴随Chern数与电荷密度转移；电场能有效调控Chern带的稳定性与转变；Hartree势主要引起弱带偏移和重塑，但与电场结合时产生丰富的拓扑相图。

Conclusion: 阐明了堆叠、扭转角与电场对扭转型双层-三层石墨烯窄带拓扑的协同作用，为在真实扭转型多层石墨烯中设计具有可调Chern数的拓扑窄带提供了理论依据与调控方案。

Abstract: We investigate the low-energy band structure and topology of twisted bilayer--trilayer graphene with four stacking configurations: AB--ABC, BA--ABC, AB--ABA, and BA--ABA. Using both tight-binding and continuum models, we first establish that the two approaches show good agreement in the band structure in low-energy regime. We then study the evolution of the flat bands and their valley Chern numbers as functions of twist angle, perpendicular electric field, and the self-consistent Hartree potential. At relatively large twist angles and under electric field, we find a topological transition between the narrow bands, with the total Chern number of the flat bands following the Chern number sum rules derived from the chiral-limit description. We also observe another type of topological transition when the flat bands hybridize with adjacent remote bands, where gap closing and reopening processes lead to Chern number and charge density transfer. By constructing topological phase diagrams in the space of twist angle and electric field, we show that the perpendicular electric field provides an efficient tuning knob for controlling the stability and transitions of the Chern bands. Finally, we find that the Hartree potential mainly induce weak band shifts and reshaping in the narrow bands. However, with a combination of Hartree potential and the electric fields, the narrow bands show rich topological phase diagram. Our results clarify the interplay between the stacking, twist angle and electric field in manipulating the narrow bands and their topology in twisted bilayer--trilayer graphene, and provide guidance for engineering topological narrow bands with tunable Chern numbers in realistic twisted multilayer graphene systems.

</details>


### [132] [The First Magic Angle Beyond the Chiral Limit in Twisted Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08347)
*Leonardo A. Navarro-Labastida,Pierre A. Pantaleon,Francisco Guinea,Gerardo G. Naumis*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出超越手性极限的平方哈密顿量描述，证明魔角鲁棒性源于晶格弛豫使系统接近近均匀约束点，同时同子格隧穿产生的类电流通道与手性轨道通道保持平衡；高阶魔角因平衡破坏而失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何在晶格弛豫作用下，扭曲双层石墨烯的第一魔角仍然稳健，而高阶魔角被强烈去稳定化；并说明这种手性魔角层级破坏背后的单粒子机制。

Method: 从具有有限同子格隧穿的非手性Bistritzer–MacDonald模型出发，发展平方哈密顿量框架，将晶格弛豫对有效约束势的重构纳入分析，而非简单视为对手性理论的微扰。

Result: 发现现实弛豫重正化后的隧穿比靠近一个特殊约束点，在该点对称约束势的振荡部分几乎抵消，使体系处于近均匀约束状态；同时有限同子格隧穿激活了额外的子格间类电流通道，与手性轨道通道竞争。第一魔角因两类贡献平衡而存活，高阶魔角则因远程能带杂化增强及AA区实空间局域化加剧而失衡。

Conclusion: 给出了手性魔角层级破坏的单粒子机制，阐明实验相关的第一魔角是手性平带结构最稳定的残留，而非高阶魔角。

Abstract: We develop a squared-Hamiltonian description of twisted bilayer graphene beyond the chiral limit to explain why the first magic angle remains robust under lattice relaxation, while higher-order magic angles are strongly destabilized. Starting from the non-chiral Bistritzer--MacDonald model with finite same-sublattice tunneling, we show that lattice relaxation reshapes the effective confinement landscape rather than acting as a simple perturbation of the chiral theory. A central result is that the realistic relaxation-renormalized tunneling ratio lies close to a special confinement point where the oscillatory part of the symmetric confinement potential nearly cancels. This places realistic twisted bilayer graphene near a nearly uniform confinement regime. At the same time, finite same-sublattice tunneling activates an additional inter-sublattice current-like channel that competes with the chiral orbital channel. The first magic angle survives because these confinement and current-like contributions remain balanced, whereas higher-order magic angles lose this balance through stronger remote-band hybridization and enhanced real-space localization around AA regions. Our results provide a single-particle mechanism for the breakdown of the chiral magic-angle hierarchy and clarify why the experimentally relevant first magic angle remains the most stable remnant of the chiral flat-band structure.

</details>


### [133] [Impurity as a probe of Berry curvature and wavefunction winding in gapped two-band models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08447)
*Reda Nabil,Pascal Simon,Andrej Mesaros*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出利用杂质附近的准粒子干涉（局域态密度）来提取能带的非局域几何性质。在描述多谷系统的最小二带模型中，非磁性点状杂质诱导的LDOS可分别在各谷局域地探测Berry曲率与波函数环绕数；在强杂质势下，围绕杂质的等能LDOS图案能清晰展示波函数环绕数，而Berry曲率也可通过其与波函数环绕数及色散参数的一般关系由谱学LDOS直接读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能带的Berry曲率和波函数环绕等几何/拓扑信息通常难以直接测量，传统方法多依赖全局或输运性质。杂质散射产生的准粒子干涉在实空间和能量上携带局域能带结构信息，但如何从LDOS中系统提取非局域带几何仍不清晰，尤其在多谷、不同色散类型及有能隙情形下。

Method: 作者从包含多谷的最小二带模型出发，分别分析Dirac、半Dirac、二次接触及高阶接触等不同能量色散体系在点状非磁杂质散射下的LDOS。通过研究围绕杂质某等能路径上的LDOS角向与径向结构，系统讨论波函数环绕数的可提取性；同时推导Berry曲率随动量的局域函数与波函数环绕数、能带色散参数之间的解析联系，从而建立从谱学LDOS读取Berry曲率的方法。

Result: 结果表明，在强杂质势极限下，围绕杂质的LDOS图案能无歧义地反映波函数环绕数；相反，在无隙Dirac类色散中，LDOS的径向振荡可能掩盖环绕信息。作者还给出了Berry曲率局域动量依赖性与波函数环绕数及能带参数之间的一般关系，使局域Berry曲率可借助LDOS谱学直接观测。

Conclusion: 该工作将LDOS/准粒子干涉成像发展为探测局域能带几何的实用探针，尤其适合多谷及拓扑材料体系。通过选择合适的强散射条件，可同时获取波函数环绕数和Berry曲率，为从扫描隧道谱学实验提取能带非平凡几何性质提供了清晰的理论框架。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of using quasiparticle interference near impurities to extract non-local properties of bands. Focusing on the minimal two-band description of a system with multiple valleys, we show that the local density of states (LDOS) induced by non-magnetic impurities can effectively probe both the Berry curvature and the wavefunction winding, locally in each valley. We analyze gapped models based on Dirac, semi-Dirac, quadratic touching, and higher-order touching energy dispersions, in presence of a point-like potential scatterer. We find that for strong enough impurity potentials the LDOS along a contour around the impurity unambiguously shows the wavefunction winding number, in contrast to the case of gapless Dirac-like dispersions where the winding can be precluded by LDOS oscillations in the radial direction. We also establish a general connection between, on the one hand, the Berry curvature as a function of momentum locally in a valley, and on the other hand, the wavefunction winding number and the energy dispersion parameters, making hence the local Berry curvature directly observable from spectroscopic LDOS information.

</details>


### [134] [Memory Control of Ice Growth During Non-Equilibrium Freezing of Water](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08535)
*Abhigyan Hazarika,Sudeep N. Punnathanam,Biman Bagchi,Prabal K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究发现过冷水结晶存在记忆驱动的Mpemba效应：初始温度越高（接近300 K）结晶反而越慢，较热的水可以比较凉的水更快冻结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过冷水冻结是经典非平衡问题，但热历史对结晶的影响尚不清楚，传统认知与微观机制之间缺乏联系。

Method: 采用TIP4P/Ice模型的分子动力学模拟，将不同初始温度Ti的水快速淬火至250 K，监测氢键环结构演化；结合非平稳广义Langevin方程分析双时记忆核，并进行环类型相关性的结构分析。

Result: 冻结时间tF对Ti呈非单调依赖，约300 K附近结晶最慢；五元环充当动力学陷阱，环类型间相关性调节冰状构型的可达性；双时记忆核保留系统热历史信息并直接影响结晶动力学。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了记忆驱动冻结的分子起源，确立结构记忆为非平衡相变的关键驱动力。

Abstract: Freezing of supercooled water is a classic non-equilibrium problem, yet the influence of thermal history on crystallization remains unclear. Using molecular dynamics simulations with the TIP4P/Ice model, we investigate how the initial temperature $T_i$ shapes freezing following rapid quenching to 250 K. By monitoring the evolution of hydrogen-bonded ring structures, we find a non-monotonic dependence of the freezing time $t_F$ on $T_i$, with the slowest crystallization occurring near 300 K. Remarkably, this means that initially hotter water can freeze faster than cooler water, a molecular-scale analogue of the Mpemba effect. A non-stationary generalized Langevin equation framework shows that two-time memory kernels retain information about the system's thermal past, directly influencing crystallization dynamics. Structural analysis further reveals that five-membered rings act as kinetic traps, while correlations among ring types regulate the accessibility of ice-like motifs. These results uncover a molecular origin of memory-driven freezing and establish structural memory as a key driver of non-equilibrium phase transitions.

</details>


### [135] [Equivalent Circuit Representation and Thermodynamic Limitations of Non-Adiabatic Spin-Transfer Torque effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08544)
*Wataru Koshibae*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出电流驱动畴壁Thiele方程的等效电路表示，发现非绝热自旋转移力矩（β项）无法由无源元件表示，必须包含有源元件（负耗散），从而在仅以驱动电流为能量源时与热力学原理矛盾，暗示仅用β项解释实验不足以涵盖完整物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电流驱动畴壁动力学中的β项本质及其物理合理性，并检验仅凭β项解释实验是否自洽。

Method: 将Thiele方程映射为等效电路，分析各转矩项的电路实现性质；考察β项对应的无源与有源元件条件，并结合热力学功率平衡约束进行论证。

Result: β项不能由无源元件等效，须依赖有源元件即负耗散；若唯一边界功率源为驱动电流，则直接引入β项会违背热力学第二定律，故实验解释需引入超出自旋动力学的额外效应。

Conclusion: 基于β项的唯象解释在能量学上不自洽，应重新审视畴壁驱动机制并考虑与自旋动力学不同的物理过程。

Abstract: We present an equivalent circuit representation of the Thiele equation for the current-driven domain walls. We show that the non-adiabatic spin-transfer torque (β-term) cannot be represented by passive elements alone (such as resistors, inductors, and capacitors) and necessarily requires active elements, corresponding to negative dissipation. Consequently, assuming that the driving current is the only power source for the texture dynamics, directly incorporating the β-term introduces a fundamental contradiction with thermodynamic principles. This equivalent circuit theory indicates that the interpretation of experiments solely through the β-term does not fully capture the underlying physics, highlighting the need to account for effects distinct from the spin dynamics.

</details>


### [136] [Wafer-Level 1/f Noise Characterization of Scaled P-Type Nanosheets and Comparison with Planar HKMG Transistors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08674)
*Ruben Asanovski,Anabela Veloso,Ben Kaczer,Naoto Horiguchi,Jacopo Franco*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 对栅极全环绕（GAA）p型纳米片晶体管进行了晶圆级1/f噪声统计研究，并将其与大面积平面高k金属栅（HKMG）器件对比。基于188个纳米片的噪声数据提取有效陷阱密度，发现纳米片与平面器件的陷阱密度相当，表明架构转变未增加噪声，噪声仍由栅堆栈质量主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估从平面HKMG晶体管转向GAA纳米片架构时，器件架构变化对可靠性的影响，特别是1/f噪声所反映的栅介质陷阱特性，以验证新架构是否引入额外噪声或可靠性退化。

Method: 对188个GAA p型纳米片进行晶圆级1/f噪声统计测量，采用相同的栅堆栈和可比的热预算，且不进行特定可靠性退火；将纳米片的噪声数据与大面积平面HKMG器件对比，并提取有效介质陷阱密度。

Result: 平面器件与GAA纳米片架构的有效陷阱密度相当，表明GAA纳米片未导致1/f噪声增加，噪声水平仍由栅堆栈质量决定。

Conclusion: 从平面HKMG向GAA纳米片过渡不会恶化1/f噪声相关的可靠性，栅堆栈质量而非器件架构是噪声主导因素，支持GAA纳米片在先进节点中的可靠应用。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive wafer-level 1/f noise study of scaled Gate-All-Around (GAA) p-type nanosheet transistors and benchmark them against large-area planar High- \k{appa} Metal Gate (HKMG) devices to assess the impact of device architecture on reliability. By statistically analyzing noise data from 188 nanosheets, we extract the effective trap density in the dielectric and compare it to that of planar transistors with identical gate stack, comparable processing thermal budgets, and no specific reliability anneals. The effective trap density extracted from 1/f noise is comparable in planar and nanosheet architectures. This indicates that transitioning to GAA nanosheets does not increase noise, which remains governed by gate stack quality.

</details>


### [137] [Observation of linear magnetoresistance and planar Hall effect in the Dirac semimetal PdTe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08675)
*Bhawana Mehra,Bibek Ranjan Satapathy,V S Abhishek,Reena,Ritu Gupta,Yogesh Singh,S. Chakraverty,Amit Vashist*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文首次在高质量PdTe单晶中观察到线性磁阻（LMR）和平面霍尔效应（PHE），并证明其源于无序驱动的迁移率涨落和各向异性轨道磁阻，而非量子极限机制或手性反常，表明这些输运现象可在半经典框架内理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PdTe是一种兼具超导性的狄拉克半金属，为研究拓扑超导和非常规磁输运提供了平台，但其超导性质已被广泛研究，而详细的磁输运现象尚未探索；同时需厘清PdTe中看似拓扑相关的输运信号是否真由非平庸拓扑机制主导。

Method: 在高质量PdTe单晶上测量了面内（B∥I）和面外（B⊥I）两种构型下的磁阻，并通过旋转面内磁场测量平面霍尔效应（PHE）；分析了磁阻从抛物线到线性的临界场Bc依赖关系，以及PHE振幅随场和温度的变化，并结合参数图对比不同物理机制。

Result: 观察到温度依赖的非饱和线性磁阻（LMR），磁阻在临界场Bc处由抛物线交叉到线性；分析表明LMR来源是无序驱动的迁移率涨落，而非Abrikosov量子极限机制。同时观察到明显的PHE，其场、温度依赖及参数图分析表明PHE主要来自各向异性轨道磁阻，而非手性反常。

Conclusion: 尽管PdTe中狄拉克点靠近费米能级，其所观测到的LMR和PHE仍可在半经典输运框架下解释，这强调了在拓扑材料中区分拓扑输运机制与经典传统输运机制（如无序效应和轨道磁阻）的重要性。

Abstract: PdTe is a Dirac semimetal that also exhibits superconductivity, providing an intriguing platform to explore topological superconductivity and unconventional magnetotransport phenomena. While the superconducting properties of PdTe have been extensively studied in recent years, the detailed magnetotransport phenomena have remained unexplored. Here, we present the first observation of linear magnetoresistance (LMR) and the planar Hall effect (PHE) in a high-quality single crystal of PdTe. We observe temperature-dependent unsaturated LMR in both in-plane ($B \parallel I$) and out-of-plane ($B \perp I$) configurations. The magnetoresistance (MR) shows a crossover from parabolic to linear dependence at the critical field Bc, and detailed analysis indicates that disorder-driven mobility fluctuations are the origin rather than the Abrikosov quantum-limit mechanism. Furthermore, prominent PHE has been observed by rotating the magnetic field within the plane of the sample. The detailed analysis of the field and temperature dependence of PHE-amplitude, along with the parametric plot, suggests that PHE originates predominantly from the anisotropic orbital magnetoresistance rather than the chiral anomaly. Our results demonstrate that although the Dirac point is close to the Fermi level, the observed LMR and PHE in the Dirac semimetal PdTe can be understood within a semiclassical transport framework, highlighting the importance of distinguishing between topological and conventional classical transport mechanisms in topological materials

</details>


### [138] [Decorated electronic kagome lattice in twisted bilayer germanene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08750)
*Dennis J. Klaassen,Rian A. M. Ligthart,Andrés R. Botello Mendez,Lumen Eek,Esra van 't Westende,Paul L. de Boeij,Y. Wang,D. Vanmaekelbergh,Pantelis Bampoulis,Cristiane Morais Smith,Ingmar Swart,Zeila Zanolli,Harold J. W. Zandvliet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Ge2Pt上制备的共度扭转双层锗烯可形成两种大角度摩尔纹相，其电子性质取决于子晶格交换奇偶性：奇宇称呈半导体性，偶宇称呈金属性且表现出具有C3对称性的装饰Kagome态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人造Kagome晶格能实现电子平带、几何阻挫和关联驱动相，但在原子级控制的二维材料中实现仍然稀少，因此需要探索可在真实二维体系中形成Kagome电子态的平台。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜测量与密度泛函理论计算，系统研究共度扭转双层锗烯/Ge2Pt体系中两种不同对称性（奇偶宇称）的大角度摩尔纹相，并分析其局域电子结构及实空间态密度分布。

Result: 子晶格交换奇对称的扭转双层表现为半导体性，而偶对称的扭转双层表现为金属性，并在空态共振中呈现具有C3对称性的装饰Kagome结构。

Conclusion: 大角度扭转锗烯可作为在翘曲二维材料中工程化Kagome类电子态的平台，拓展了人工Kagome晶格在真实材料中的实现途径。

Abstract: Artificial kagome lattices provide a route to electronic flat bands, geometric frustration, and correlation driven phases, but their realization in atomically controlled two-dimensional materials remains scarce. Here, we show that commensurate twisted bilayer germanene on Ge2Pt produces two electronically distinct large-angle moiré phases. Scanning tunneling microscopy measurements and density functional theory calculations reveal that commensurate twisted bilayers that are odd under an exchange of sublattices are semiconducting, whereas the twisted bilayers with an even parity are metallic. The twisted bilayers with an even parity host an empty state resonance that exhibits an emergent decorated kagome structure with a C3 symmetry. These results establish large-angle twisted germanene as a platform for engineering kagome-like electronic states in a buckled two-dimensional material.

</details>


### [139] [Non-perturbative theory of valley splitting in Si qubits from variational wave function: periodic effects of shear strain and asymptotic freedom in the wiggle-well potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08839)
*Johannes L. P. Steinschuld,Hendrik J. Bluhm,Seyed Akbar Jafari*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文使用valleyor基组和两带有效质量模型，解析研究了硅量子阱中剪切应变与wiggle-well势对谷劈裂的非微扰影响，给出了与精确数值计算高度吻合的封闭表达式，并揭示了wiggle-well共振附近的量子比特‘渐近自由’行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谷劈裂决定了硅量子阱自旋量子比特中自旋与谷自由度杂化的能量尺度，但它对界面结构极其敏感，难以直接预测和调控，因此需要一种能处理非微扰效应并给出清晰的解析物理图像的模型。

Method: 基于valleyor基组构建有限量子阱的区间耦合两带有效质量模型；对均匀剪切应变V_sτ_2，用平面波valleyor与硬壁包络构造变分态，推导两个最低态及劈裂的闭式表达式；对wiggle-well势V_w cos(k_w z)τ_1，利用valleyor表示分析其共振与失谐行为。

Result: 变分结果在真实耦合参数下与精确对角化误差约1%，并解析给出劈裂的|sin(k_min L)|振荡依赖；有限势垒仅重正化有效阱宽。wiggle-well在k_w=2k_1共振附近出现从wiggle主导到轨道量子化的交叉，且高wiggle振幅下空盒能量尺度主导劈裂，wiggle-well势仅为亚领头修正；失谐时劈裂呈宽共振峰及衍射状旁瓣。

Conclusion: 该解析框架为调控硅量子阱的谷劈裂提供了简单有效的配方，并揭示了wiggle-well势下类似‘渐近自由’的物理机制，对自旋量子比特的器件设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Valley splitting sets the energy scale at which spin and valley degrees of freedom hybridize in silicon quantum-well qubits, but its sensitivity to interface structure makes it difficult to predict. Using the valleyor basis, we formulate a two-band effective-mass model for intervalley coupling in a finite quantum well and develop an analytical framework for treating non-perturbative effects of shear strain and wiggle-well potentials. For a $z$-independent coupling $V_sτ_2$, corresponding to uniform shear strain, we construct a variational state from the exact plane-wave valleyors and a hard-wall envelope. This yields closed-form expressions for the two lowest states and their splitting, including the characteristic oscillatory $|\sin(k_{\rm min}L)|$ dependence. The variational result agrees with exact diagonalization to within $\sim1\%$ for realistic couplings, and provides a simple prescription for tuning the shear strain away from the nodes to enhance the valley splitting. We further show that finite barrier heights primarily renormalize the effective width of the quantum well while preserving the oscillatory dependence. For a wiggle-well coupling $V_w\cos(k_wz)τ_1$, the valleyor representation reveals a crossover near the $k_w=2k_1$ resonance from wiggle-dominated splitting to orbital quantization. Remarkably, at high wiggle-well amplitudes, the empty-box energy scale predominantly dictates the near-resonant valley splitting, while the wiggle-well potential enters only as a sub-leading correction. This behavior can be viewed as asymptotic freedom for spin qubits. Away from resonance, the splitting exhibits a broadly peaked resonance with diffraction-like side-lobes as a function of detuning.

</details>


### [140] [Probing Antialtermagnetism via Orbital-Field-Induced Spin Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08996)
*Zi-Ting Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出了利用面内磁场的轨道效应将反交替磁体中隐藏的自旋纹理转化为可观测的自旋劈裂，从而无需层分辨即可识别隐藏的磁有序参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反交替磁体中，层补偿效应可能使自旋极化隐藏在自旋简并的体态能谱之后，阻碍了通过光谱手段直接探测潜在的磁有序。

Method: 理论分析面内磁场的轨道效应如何诱导自旋劈裂，并通过最小模型研究弱场下的响应函数，包括能带劈裂的带求和规则以及净自旋极化的场振幅和角度依赖。

Result: 隐藏的自旋纹理在轨道场作用下转化为具有相反动量宇称的自旋劈裂，恢复光谱可测性；通过带求和规则可区分隐藏偶宇称序，而奇宇称扇区则表现特征性的场振幅与角度依赖。

Conclusion: 轨道场诱导的自旋劈裂为识别隐藏反交替磁有序参数提供了一种通用途径，消除了对层分辨实验的需求。

Abstract: Layer compensation can conceal spin polarization behind a spin-degenerate bulk spectrum in antialtermagnets, preventing spectroscopic identification of the underlying magnetic order. Here we show that the orbital effect of an in-plane magnetic field converts the hidden spin texture into an observable spin splitting of opposite momentum parity, thereby restoring spectroscopic access. Once the parity of the hidden exchange order is determined, complementary response functions in the weak-field regime further resolve its wave character: within a minimal model, we illustrate that a band sum rule over the induced band splittings isolates the momentum gradient of the form factor and differentiates hidden even-parity orders, whereas for odd-parity sectors the induced net spin polarization exhibits characteristic field-amplitude and angular dependences. These results establish orbital-field-induced spin splitting as a generic route to identifying the hidden altermagnetic order parameter without layer resolution.

</details>


### [141] [Hilbert space connectivity in non-Hermitian many-body systems: emergent scale-dependent amplification and constraint-induced skin localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09017)
*Zichang Hao,Wen-Tan Xue,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作揭示在非厄米多体系统中，希尔伯特空间连通性对谱放大与态局域化具有根本性控制作用；非厄米希尔伯特空间图包含复杂竞争放大通道和由粒子对称性联系的远程福克态全局反馈回路，导致非传统标度与局域化行为。粒子占据约束可移除特定通道，在原本互易过程中产生稳健的单极或非对称双极趋肤效应，表明希尔伯特空间连通性可作为多体非厄米临界转变的通用调控旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将量子疤痕、分形子等奇异多体现象与希尔伯特空间碎片化关联，但非厄米环境下希尔伯特空间连通性的影响尚不清晰。作者希望探究其在非厄米多体系统中的普适作用，并理解其如何控制谱放大和态局域化等基本行为。

Method: 作者分析非厄米多体希尔伯特空间图结构，相较于实空间晶格，该图存在多条竞争放大通道，以及由粒子对称性连接远程福克态形成的全局反馈回路。通过考察粒子占据约束对通道的移除效应，系统研究谱放大标率、局域化性质及趋肤效应出现条件。

Result: 发现希尔伯特空间连通性导致非常规标度与局域化的放大行为；占据约束可以删除部分通道，在互易过程中产生稳健的单极或非对称双极趋肤局域化。以上结果不限于简单相互作用玻色模型，并表明该机制可推广至多体非厄米临界转变。

Conclusion: 希尔伯特空间连通性不仅是碎片化相关现象的来源，更是多体非厄米系统中控制谱响应和局域化的普适机制，为设计和理解非厄米多体临界现象提供了新调控维度。

Abstract: Various exotic many-body phenomena such as quantum scars and fractons have been linked to Hilbert space fragmentation. In this work, we find that in non-Hermitian settings, Hilbert space connectivity has an even more universal and fundamental influence, tightly controlling the nature of spectral amplification and state localization. Far more complicated than real-space lattices, non-Hermitian many-body Hilbert space graphs not only possess intricate competing amplification channels, but also global feedback loops connecting remote Fock states related by particle symmetry. These features lead to amplification behavior with unconventional scaling and localization properties. Particle occupation constraints can furthermore remove selected Hilbert space pathways, leading to robust unipolar and asymmetric bipolar skin localization in otherwise reciprocal processes. These results extend beyond simple interacting bosonic models and establish Hilbert space connectivity as a versatile control knob for many-body non-Hermitian critical transitions.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [142] [Differentiable Solvation Shell Model for Rational Electrolyte Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05671)
*Hancheng Zhao,Hongyi Lin,Celia Kelly,Venkatasubramanian Viswanathan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Ising模型的平均场建模框架，用于从供体数、受体数、摩尔比和分子尺寸等分子性质定量预测锂离子溶剂化壳层组成，并通过梯度优化从分子动力学数据中参数化，在LHCE等电解质体系中实现了高精度和良好泛化，同时展示了其设计电解质的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂金属电池电解质的合理设计受限于对分子性质如何控制锂离子溶剂化壳层组成的定量理解不足；现有经验规则缺乏热力学基础，且高精度MD模拟成本高昂，难以用于快速筛选和设计。

Method: 引入一个基于Ising模型的平均场框架，输入为供体数(DN)、受体数(AN)、摩尔比和分子尺寸，输出为溶剂化壳层组成；该框架端到端可微，可通过梯度优化直接从MD模拟的溶剂化结构中学习参数；通过分析模型相互作用项揭示DN的主导作用，并用于预测LiTFSI/G4体系的稀释剂和盐浓度窗口。

Result: 在LHCE体系中，模型对壳层组成的RMSE为10.7%，R²=0.87；对自由溶剂比例的RMSE为2.3%。模型能复现真实LHCE电解质的溶剂化趋势，并推广到其他电解质体系。分析表明DN主导锂离子溶剂化能，提供了经验设计规则的热力学依据。在LiTFSI/G4体系中，模型预测氟苯(FB)是有前景的稀释剂，并预言存在富阴离子壳层和低自由溶剂的盐浓度窗口；该预测得到高保真MD验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个可解释、可微分的锂离子溶剂化壳层组成预测框架，兼具高精度和低计算成本，为LMB电解质乃至其他电解质类别的理性设计提供了新工具和热力学洞见。

Abstract: Tailoring Li+ solvation structures has emerged as a promising design principle across multiple Li metal battery (LMB) electrolytes, with localized high concentration electrolytes (LHCE) emerging as a leading candidate. However, rational design of these electrolytes remains limited by a poor quantitative understanding of how molecular properties govern Li+ solvation shell composition. Here we introduce a mean field modeling framework, built on an Ising model, that predicts solvation shell composition from donor number (DN), acceptor number (AN), molar ratio, and molecular size. This framework is end-to-end differentiable, which enables parameterization directly from molecular dynamics (MD) solvation structures via gradient-based optimization. Focusing on LHCE, the model achieves 10.7% RMSE and R2=0.87 for shell composition, 2.3% RMSE on free solvent ratio, and further reproduces solvation trends in real LHCE electrolytes, at a fraction of MD's cost. Our model also shows good generalizability on other electrolyte systems in addition to LHCE. Analysis of its interaction terms shows that DN dominates Li+ solvation energetics, providing a thermodynamic basis for empirical DN-based design rules. We further demonstrate the model's design utility on the LiTFSI/tetraglyme (G4) system absent from training. The model identifies fluorobenzene (FB) as a promising diluent and predicts a salt-concentration window with anion-rich solvation shells and low free solvent, favorable for stable anion-derived SEIs and high oxidative stability, respectively. This prediction is validated by higher-fidelity MD. This work establishes an interpretable differentiable framework for predicting Li+ solvation shell composition, with potential to extend to other electrolyte classes.

</details>


### [143] [cuSOAP: a GPU-accelerated Generator of Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions Descriptor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05709)
*Hongyu Yan,Yuqing Xia,Yong Wei,Minghan Chen,Hanning Chen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: cuSOAP是一个基于PyTorch的GPU加速SOAP描述符及解析导数生成器，通过融合CUDA/Triton核消除张量中间量，在GB200节点上比CPU版DScribe快13–227倍，并可在4 GPU上以约90%并行效率处理百万原子体系，使大规模凝聚相模拟的实时SOAP计算成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOAP描述符在分子机器学习中广泛使用，但其及其导数的计算成本是SOAP势函数的主要瓶颈，尤其在大径向和角向基组、复杂多物种凝聚态环境下更为突出；现有CPU参考实现DScribe难以满足大规模模拟需求。

Method: cuSOAP在PyTorch中实现，支持Gaussian-type-orbital和polynomial径向基的投影系数及笛卡尔梯度的闭式或求积公式，并通过融合CUDA与Triton核避免朴素张量公式产生的数GB中间量；保持与DScribe的构造签名、特征顺序和输出兼容，并直接接受ASE Atoms对象。

Result: 在NVIDIA Grace–Blackwell节点上，单GPU对1000分子水团簇的描述符加雅可比计算较DScribe快13至227倍（随l_max增大而加速）；描述符生成时间对原子数呈t∝n^1.34近线性标度，百万原子团簇单GPU用时19.5秒，四GPU用时5.7秒，并行效率约90%且无饱和迹象。

Conclusion: cuSOAP大幅降低SOAP及其导数计算成本，使大规模凝聚相模拟中的在线SOAP评估切实可行，且具有良好的可扩展性与易用性。

Abstract: The Smooth Overlap of Atomic Positions (SOAP) descriptor is one of the most widely adopted representations of atomic environments in molecular machine learning, but the cost of evaluating it and its derivatives remains a principal bottleneck of SOAP-based interatomic potentials, particularly at the large radial and angular basis sizes demanded by complex, multi-species condensed-phase environments. We present cuSOAP, a GPU-accelerated generator of atom-wise SOAP vectors and their analytic derivatives. Built on PyTorch, cuSOAP evaluates closed-form expressions or quadratures for the projection coefficients and their Cartesian gradients for Gaussian-type-orbital and polynomial radial bases, through fused CUDA and Triton kernels that eliminate the multi-gigabyte intermediates of a naive tensor formulation. The package is a drop-in replacement for the CPU-based reference DScribe, reproducing its constructor signature, feature ordering, and output to within ${\sim}10^{-6}$, and accepts structures directly as Atomistic Simulation Environment (ASE) Atoms objects. On an NVIDIA Grace--Blackwell (GB200) node, a single Blackwell GPU generates the full descriptor-plus-Jacobian workload for a 1000-molecule water cluster up to two orders of magnitude faster than DScribe, i.e., from $13\times$ to $227\times$ across the entire $(n_{\max}, l_{\max})$ hyperparameter map, with the speedup growing with the angular band limit $l_{\max}$. Descriptor-only generation scales as $t \propto n^{1.34}$, close to linear, up to a million-atom water cluster, which is processed in 19.5s on one GPU and, through a shared-memory multiprocessing driver, in 5.7s on the four GPUs of the node, demonstrating $\sim$90% parallel efficiency with no sign of saturation as devices are added. These results bring on-the-fly SOAP evaluation for large-scale condensed-phase simulation within reach.

</details>


### [144] [Performance of a minimally empirical local-hybrid density functional for molecular chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06162)
*Erin R. Johnson,Kyle R. Bryenton*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出仅有三个经验参数的极小经验局域杂化泛函LHnz，利用依赖于相关长度和有效交换-空穴归一化的局域混合分数，在GMTKN55分子热化学基准上超越所有现有全局杂化泛函且无大离群值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离域化误差被认为是密度泛函理论中最大的挑战之一，局域杂化泛函有望最小化该误差，但现有高精度局域杂化泛函往往高度经验化且形式复杂（涉及范围分离、校准函数、幂级数甚至神经网络），需探索简单形式的可能性。

Method: 构建最小经验的局域杂化泛函形式LHnz，使用无色散交换，并令局域混合分数依赖于相关长度和有效交换-空穴归一化，仅含三个经验参数，避免复杂结构。

Result: 在GMTKN55分子热化学基准上，LHnz以仅三个参数超过了所有现有全局杂化泛函的表现，且无大离群值。

Conclusion: 表明简单的局域杂化形式即可在热化学精度上超越复杂泛函，说明减少经验复杂度并利用物理约束是可行的，为发展非经验化或更低经验化的DFT方法提供新方向。

Abstract: Delocalisation error has been argued to be the greatest outstanding challenge in density-functional theory (DFT). One of the most promising routes to minimise this error is development of local hybrid functionals, in which the fraction of exact-exchange mixing is position dependent. However, existing local hybrids capable of good thermochemical accuracy are often highly empirical, and tend to have complicated functional forms that involve some combination of range separation, calibration functions, power-series expansions, or even neural networks. In this work, we explore the limits of what can be achieved with a minimally empirical local hybrid functional form that avoids such complexities. The ``LHnz'' functional is proposed, which uses dispersionless exchange and a local mixing fraction dependent on the correlation length and effective exchange--correlation hole normalisations. With only three empirical parameters, LHnz is shown to outperform all existing global hybrid functionals for the GMTKN55 molecular-thermochemistry benchmark with no large outliers.

</details>


### [145] [Tensor-based Approximation of Molecular Kinetics: Generator Learning, Reaction Coordinates and Incremental Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06446)
*Minakshi Verma,Peter Benner,Feliks Nüske*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于张量列（TT）格式近似无穷小生成元的方法，用于分析分子动力学模拟中的长时程动力学（亚稳态和转变时间尺度），并通过张量积基求解变分特征值问题，支持态依赖扩散场和反应坐标逐步增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子动力学模拟中长时程动力学分析面临巨大计算挑战，传统方法难以高效辨识亚稳态和转变时间尺度；已有变分方法在反应坐标较多时基函数组合爆炸，需要可扩展且能利用低秩张量结构的求解框架。

Method: 从低能生成元本征值的变分近似出发，用张量积基离散化特征值问题；推导出张量算子在TT格式下的解析表达式，并设计高效算法求解相应线性问题；同时处理态依赖扩散场情形，并支持逐步增加反应坐标数量而无需重新从头求解变分问题。

Result: 以快速折叠蛋白的MD模拟和TICA坐标作为反应坐标，验证了该方法能有效识别长时程转变和亚稳态。

Conclusion: 该方法为高维反应坐标下的长时程动力学分析提供了一种高效、可扩展的途径，有望推广到更复杂的生物分子模拟系统。

Abstract: We present an approach to analyze long-timescale kinetics of molecular dynamics simulations - meta-stable states and transition timescales - by a tensor-based approximation of the infinitesimal generator. We start from the variational approximation of low-lying generator eigenvalues, and discretize the associated eigenvalue problem using a tensor-product basis. We derive analytical expressions for the resulting tensor operators in tensor train (TT) format, and provide efficient algorithms to solve the corresponding linear problems. We also treat the case of a state-dependent diffusion field, which is relevant when working in reaction coordinates. We show how the number of reaction coordinates can be gradually increased without having to solve the variational problem from scratch. Using MD simulations of fast-folding proteins and TICA coordinates as reaction coordinates, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method at identifying long-timescale transitions and meta-stable states.

</details>


### [146] [Out-of-Distribution Inverse Design of Elastic Networks with Differentiable Graph Neural Network Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06655)
*Sergey A. Shteingolts,Salman N. Salman,Ron Levie,Dan Mendels*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图神经网络分子动力学模拟器的可微逆设计框架，通过短时动力学初始化与基于物理的模拟内精修，使材料性质优化能够显著超出训练数据的分布范围，并在无序弹性网络上成功生成强拉胀、非训练应力-应变响应、局域缺陷及跨尺度设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器学习逆设计依赖结构-性质模型，需要大量训练数据，且外推能力差，难以在训练分布之外发现具有目标性能的新材料。

Method: 采用可微的图神经网络分子动力学模拟器，将短时动力学初始化与模拟过程中的物理约束精修相结合，使优化过程在模拟中沿物理轨迹进行，从而突破训练数据分布限制，并实现跨系统规模的泛化。

Result: 在无序弹性网络上，仅用泊松比0.1至0.4的非拉胀系统训练，即可设计出泊松比低至-0.3的强拉胀网络；还能生成训练范围外的应力-应变响应、训练集中不存在的局域力学缺陷，并将训练规模从<200节点泛化到5000节点网络的优化。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了利用小型、分布受限的训练数据集实现大范围外推的材料逆设计能力，为发现训练分布外极端或新奇力学性能材料提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Machine-learning-based inverse design can accelerate the discovery of materials with targeted properties, but conventional structure--property models often require large training datasets and generalize poorly beyond their training distribution. Here, we present a differentiable inverse design framework based on a graph neural network molecular dynamics simulator. By combining a short dynamical initialization with physics-based refinement during simulation, the framework enables optimization well beyond the conditions represented in the training data. Using disordered elastic networks, we show that a simulator trained only on non-auxetic systems with Poisson's ratios between 0.1 and 0.4 can design strongly auxetic networks with values as low as -0.3. The framework also produces stress--strain responses outside the range observed during training, creates localized mechanical defects absent from the training data, and generalizes across system size, enabling optimization of networks tested up to 5000 nodes despite being trained only on networks with fewer than 200 nodes.

</details>


### [147] [First-order Degenerate Symmetry-Adapted Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07215)
*Dominik Cieśliński,Tijs Karman,Anthony Scemama,Michał Hapka,Piotr S. Żuchowski*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种简并对称适应微扰理论（dSAPT），用于量化简并或近简并电子态间的构型混合，并将其分解为静电与交换（Pauli排斥）贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简并电子态主导准分子光物理及圆锥交叉附近的分子动力学，但传统SAPT无法描述这些区域中电子构型的混合，需要一种能处理简并性的理论。

Method: 在SAPT框架中引入一阶简并微扰，采用弱对称强制，并比较两种消除简并的方式（先/后施加反对称化）；单体波函数用CASSCF，部分体系用CIS，计算H₂–F(²P)与H₂–NO(²Π)及水和苯准分子。

Result: 交换效应在中等分子间距时已显著贡献构型混合；H₂–NO中二阶色散能对二聚混合角的角度依赖不可或缺；Pauli排斥在短间距下控制激发离域。

Conclusion: dSAPT能有效分解简并态间的相互作用，揭示交换与色散对简并体系构型混合及激发离域的关键角色。

Abstract: Degenerate electronic states govern the photophysics of excimers and the molecular dynamics near conical intersections. In regions where potential energy surfaces are degenerate or nearly degenerate, intermolecular interactions mix the corresponding electronic configurations. We introduce a first-order degenerate formulation of symmetry-adapted perturbation theory (dSAPT) with weak symmetry forcing that quantifies the mixing in terms of electrostatic and exchange (Pauli repulsion) contributions. We compare two alternative ways of resolving the degeneracy, in which antisymmetry is enforced either after or before diagonalization of the first-order eigenproblem. Using CASSCF monomer wave functions, we apply dSAPT to two model spatially degenerate systems, H$_2$--F(${}^2P$) and H$_2$--NO(${}^2Π$), and show that exchange effects contribute significantly to configuration mixing already at intermediate intermolecular separations. For the H$_2$--NO(${}^2Π$) complex, we further demonstrate that second-order dispersion energy is essential for qualitatively reproducing the angular dependence of the diabatic mixing angle. Finally, using water and benzene excimers with monomers described either by CASSCF or CIS wave functions, we show that Pauli repulsion controls delocalization of the excitation at short intermolecular separations.

</details>


### [148] [Mixed quantum-classical evolution in open molecular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07272)
*Michael Galperin,Abraham Nitzan*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 针对开放非平衡分子体系，提出了一种基于赝粒子非平衡格林函数（PP-NEGF）的混合量子-经典表述，导出了密度矩阵运动方程，并揭示了其与现有方法的关键差异；进一步分析表明，将完整的量子-经典动力学约化为最少切换面跳跃（FSSH）方法所需的近似在绝热面接近时是不合理的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放非平衡分子系统的量子-经典混合描述尚不完善，现有公式在非平衡与开放特征下可能缺失重要物理内容，且从全量子描述到常用近似方法（如FSSH）的约化条件缺乏严格检验。

Method: 以完全量子化的PP-NEGF方法为起点，引入开放系统的经典核动力学，推导密度矩阵运动方程；并与先前公式对比，进而分析FSSH约化所依赖的近似条件。

Result: 得到了量子-经典演化的密度矩阵方程，明确了与先前公式的差异；证明了FSSH所需的近似在绝热面近简并时不再成立。

Conclusion: 所提出的PP-NEGF启发的混合量子-经典框架能更正确处理开放非平衡效应，而FSSH等简并面跳跃方法在绝热面接近时失效，需谨慎使用或发展更合理的近似方案。

Abstract: We consider a mixed quantum-classical formulation for open nonequilibrium molecular systems. We employ the pseudoparticle nonequilibrium Green's function (PP-NEGF) method as a fully quantum description and a starting point for introducing classical nuclear dynamics for an open system. We use this formulation to derive a density-matrix equation of motion describing the quantum-classical evolution. We compare our results with previous formulations and highlight important differences related to the nonequilibrium and open character of the molecular system. We also analyze the approximations needed to reduce full quantum-classical dynamics to the fewest-switches surface hopping (FSSH) method and show that these approximations are unreasonable when adiabatic surfaces come close to each other.

</details>


### [149] [Strong Evidence for Formation of Hydroxyl Anion via 2-Particle-1-Hole Feshbach Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07324)
*Siddique Ali,Meenakshi Rana,Soumya Ghosh,Narayan Kundu,Aryya Ghosh,Dhananjay Nandi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 以2-丙醇为模型体系，结合高精度CAP-EOM-EA-CCSD计算与飞行时间质谱，揭示了7–11 eV能量范围内OH⁻生成主要由2p-1h Feshbach共振主导；通过非绝热跃迁和特定长寿命共振态（尤其态25和28）将附加电子局域到σ*(C–OH)轨道，实现位点特异性断键。理论预测与实验观测的8.6 eV处OH⁻产额峰高度一致，并表明该机制可推广至更广泛的有机/无机分子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解解离电子附着（DEA）驱动的羟基阴离子（OH⁻）生成机制，对有机和无机分子的辐射化学、等离子体化学及环境过程具有重要意义。作者希望以2-丙醇为模型，从理论上阐明OH⁻形成的电子态本质和动力学路径，并借助实验验证，从而提出可推广的位点特异性碎裂机制。

Method: 采用高层次的CAP-EOM-EA-CCSD（复吸收势-运动方程-电子亲和力-耦合簇）方法计算阴离子共振态与势能曲线；结合飞行时间（ToF）质谱实验测量OH⁻产额随电子能量的变化；利用势能曲线中的避免交叉分析非绝热布居转移，并通过存活概率（survival-probability）分析识别能持续足够时间以驱动碎裂的长寿命共振态。

Result: 在7–11 eV电子能量区间，OH⁻生成主要由双粒子-单空穴（2p-1h）Feshbach共振控制；势能曲线表现出密集的阴离子态流形和大量避免交叉，促进C–OH键解离过程中的非绝热转移。存活概率分析筛出六个长寿命共振态，其中态25和态28为主要驱动者，它们将附加电子有效注入局域化的σ*(C–OH)反键轨道。实验观测到OH⁻产额在8.6 eV处显著峰值，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了2-丙醇中OH⁻形成的共振主导机制，证明特定Feshbach共振通过避免交叉和非绝热动力学实现定向的C–OH断键，且这种位点特异性碎裂机制具有普适性，可为理解更广泛分子的DEA过程提供理论框架和实验参考。

Abstract: This study investigates the formation of the hydroxyl anion (OH$^-$) via dissociative electron attachment in 2-propanol as a model system for studying both organic and inorganic molecules.
