### [160] [Kibble-Zurek Dynamics in Two-dimensional Frustrated Systems with a Neural Foundation-state Subspace Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05556)
*Linda Mauron,Luciano Loris Viteritti,Zakari Denis,Riccardo Rende,Giuseppe Carleo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种神经基态子空间（NFS）方法，用于模拟量子多体系统在连续相变附近的近绝热动力学，通过将薛定谔方程约化为少数线性系数的演化，实现了对二维横场Ising模型和阻挫J1-J2 Heisenberg模型（最大16×16）的Kibble-Zurek标度与临界指数的定量提取，论证了从Néel到量子自旋液体的连续相变特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用量子多体系统被驱动跨越连续相变时产生的普适标度（如Kibble-Zurek机制）是探测平衡临界性的重要动力学探针，但二维数值模拟需要精确的实时演化且需覆盖多系统尺寸和驱动速率，目前极具挑战性。

Method: 引入神经基态子空间方法：用一个基础神经网络量子态表示沿驱动路径的基态流形，并通过保真度选择出一小组固定变分子空间；将多体薛定谔方程简化为少数线性系数的演化，且投影算符可在多次扫描中途重复使用，从而高效处理近绝热动力学。

Result: 在二维横场Ising模型上验证了方法，恢复的Kibble-Zurek标度和临界指数与基态量子蒙特卡洛估计定量一致；应用于阻挫正方格子J1-J2 Heisenberg模型（最大16×16），在J2/J1=0.49处给出ν=1.23(15)和η=0.409(19)（固定z=1），与静态估计相符，支持该相变为连续临界行为。

Conclusion: NFS方法为二维强关联系统近绝热量子动力学提供了可靠高效的计算途径，并从动力学角度为Néel到自旋液体转变的连续临界性提供了强数值证据。

Abstract: Universal scaling generated when a strongly interacting quantum many-body system is driven across a continuous phase transition provides a dynamical probe of equilibrium criticality. Accessing this regime numerically in two dimensions is challenging because it requires accurate real-time evolution of correlated many-body states over many system sizes and driving rates. We introduce a Neural Foundation-state Subspace (NFS) method for near-adiabatic dynamics. A foundation neural-network quantum state represents the ground-state manifold along the driving path, and a small fidelity-selected subset defines a fixed variational subspace. The many-body Schrödinger equation then reduces to the evolution of a few linear coefficients, with projected operators reusable across ramp times. We validate the method on the two-dimensional transverse-field Ising model, recovering the expected Kibble-Zurek scaling and critical exponents in quantitative agreement with ground-state quantum Monte Carlo estimates. Applied to the frustrated square-lattice $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model up to $16 \times 16$ clusters, our approach provides strong numerical evidence of Kibble-Zurek mechanism across the Néel-to-spin-liquid transition at $J_2/J_1=0.49$, yielding ${ν=1.23(15)}$ and ${η=0.409(19)}$ at fixed $z=1$, consistent with static estimates and supporting the proposed continuous critical behavior.

</details>


### [161] [Deviation from Fermi-liquid $T^2$ resistivity caused by collective transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05724)
*Kamran Behnia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出费米液体中电阻率随温度二次方关系的偏离在弱关联与强关联金属中方向相反：弱关联金属因声子附加散射而向上偏离，强关联金属则表现为向下偏离，可能源于散射减弱或额外导电通道；在UPt3、Sr2RuO4和过掺杂LSCO中观测到的归一化超额导电幅度与液氦3中声模式传热贡献相当，暗示可能存在共同起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释费米液体T^2电阻率行为在有限温度下偏离的物理机制，尤其是强关联金属中向下偏离的起源尚未明确。作者借正常液氦3中已识别的声模式热导通道，探讨强关联金属中额外传导或散射减少的可能性。

Method: 通过对比弱关联与强关联费米液体中电阻率偏离的方向与成因，引入液氦3中声模式对热输运的贡献以及Bridgman公式在经典液体热导率中的适用性，并利用UPt3、Sr2RuO4和过掺杂LSCO的实验数据，比较其在T/T_F约0.02处的归一化超额电导幅度。

Result: 三种强关联金属在低温费米液体区的向下偏离对应的超额导电幅度，与液氦3中声模式传热贡献的归一化幅度相当；这支持额外声子/声模式导电通道或有效散射减少的解释，而非弱关联金属中声子附加散射主导的向上偏离。

Conclusion: 尽管结果尚不能确证因果联系，但数据表明强关联金属中的费米液体偏离可能与液氦3中声模式热导具有共同的物理起源，为理解强关联金属输运性质提供了新视角，并提示需进一步实验验证。

Abstract: `Fermi-liquid behavior' is nowadays used as a shorthand for quadratic temperature dependence of the electrical resistivity. In a metal, for this to occur, electrons must be cooled well below the Fermi degeneracy temperature, $T_{\mathrm{F}}$. A departure from this behavior is observed at finite temperature. Here, we note that this departure has opposite signs in two distinct types of Fermi liquids. In weakly correlated ones, the upward departure implies a higher exponent of the inelastic resistivity, attributable to \textit{additional scattering} by phonons. In contrast, there is a downward deviation in strongly correlated metals, which implies either \textit{reduced scattering} or \textit{additional conduction}, as in the case of normal liquid $^3$He, in which a distinct contribution to heat transport by a sound mode has been recently identified. Such a channel of conduction smoothly transforms across $T_{\mathrm{F}}$ to the one captured by the Bridgman formula for thermal conductivity of classical liquids. In three metals (UPt$_3$, Sr$_2$RuO$_4$, and heavily overdoped LSCO), according to the available experimental data, the normalized amplitude of excess conductivity at $T/T_{\mathrm{F}}\simeq0.02$ is comparable to what has been seen in $^3$He, suggesting, without establishing, a common origin.

</details>


### [162] [Ultrafast electron crystallography reveals the atomic pathway of a light-driven correlated insulator-to-metal transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05747)
*Arthur Niedermayr,Hongyi Xu,Chin Shen Ong,Patrik Thunström,Michael Yannai,Jianyu Wu,Gaolong Cao,Lior Kornblum,Ido Kaminer,Oscar Grånäs,Xiaodong Zou,Jonas Weissenrieder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过超快三维电子衍射技术，解析了VO2在绝缘体-金属相变过程中的瞬态原子坐标，发现V-V二聚化和锯齿运动沿线性轨迹演化，并结合量子多体计算揭示了二聚化导致电子能隙打开及塌缩的机制，将时间分辨衍射发展为瞬态晶体学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关材料中超快相变通常仅从衍射峰强度或位移推断，难以获取三维原子轨迹，从而无法确定哪些原子运动驱动电子性质变化及其耦合方式。

Method: 引入超快三维电子衍射技术，对瞬态晶胞及内部V和O坐标进行精修，并结合将每个V-V对视为关联单元的量子多体计算方法。

Result: 精修得到的原子坐标在相变过程中沿实空间线性轨迹演化，而非早期推断的非线性或顺序路径；量子多体计算表明V-V二聚化产生能级劈裂形成电子能隙，非局域电子相互作用进一步增强能隙，失去二聚化则能隙塌缩。

Conclusion: 该工作将时间分辨衍射从序参量追踪转变为瞬态晶体学，直接建立原子轨迹与关联材料电子机制之间的联系。

Abstract: Ultrafast phase transitions in correlated materials are often inferred from selected diffraction peak intensities or diffraction peak displacements, leaving the underlying three-dimensional atomic trajectories elusive. Resolving these trajectories is essential for identifying which atomic motions drive changes in electronic properties and how they couple to electronic degrees of freedom. We address this challenge in vanadium dioxide (VO2), a correlated oxide with a near-room-temperature transition between the insulating monoclinic (M1) phase and metallic rutile (R) phase. For this purpose, we introduce ultrafast three-dimensional electron diffraction, which enables refinement of the transient unit cell and internal V and O coordinates, revealing the V-V dimerization and zigzag motion during the phase transition. The refined atomic coordinates follow a linear trajectory in real space during the transition, in contrast to nonlinear or sequential transformation pathways inferred from more indirect observables in earlier work. Quantum many-body calculations treating each V-V pair as a correlated unit show that dimerization creates the level splitting responsible for the electronic gap, which is further enhanced by nonlocal electronic interactions between the paired V atoms. The gap collapses when dimerization is lost. This work turns time-resolved diffraction from order-parameter tracking into transient crystallography, directly connecting atomic trajectories to electronic mechanisms in correlated materials.

</details>


### [163] [Magnetization processes and spin dynamics across field-induced phase transitions in the quasi-two-dimensional quantum magnet Cu$_2$(OH)$_3$Br](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06246)
*Anneke Reinold,Dirk Wulferding,Laur Peedu,Kirill Amelin,Urmas Nagel,Toomas Rõõm,Zhiying Zhao,Patrick Pilch,Changqing Zhu,Hans Engelkamp,Lucas Berger,Denis I. Gorbunov,Yurii Skourski,Kwang-Yong Choi,Thomas Lorenz,Zhe Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过太赫兹光谱、拉曼光谱与高场磁化测量，研究了准二维自旋1/2体系Cu2(OH)3Br中磁场对磁化与自旋动力学的影响，揭示了磁场方向依赖的自旋重排、自旋翻转跃迁及铁磁/反铁磁链间耦合随场的重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解由交替耦合的铁磁Cu1链与反铁磁Cu2链构成的准二维自旋体系中，磁场如何调控低能磁激发谱及耦合子系统间的相互作用。

Method: 结合太赫兹光谱、拉曼光谱以及高场磁化测量，系统测量不同磁场方向与强度下的磁激发谱、磁化曲线及声子/磁子/自旋子激发。

Result: 发现磁场垂直链时（B∥a和B∥c*）谱在自旋翻转跃迁处发生突变重构；B∥b时无自旋翻转，而是铁磁子系统逐渐极化、激发谱连续演化并强烈依赖太赫兹辐射偏振；高场下低场复杂谱退化为少数宽激发，表明铁磁与反铁磁链间耦合减弱；拉曼光谱分辨出磁子、自旋子及多个声子模式并追踪其温度与磁场演化。

Conclusion: 该工作完整绘制了磁场在Cu2(OH)3Br中重新组织耦合铁磁与反铁磁子系统并驱动场致相变的演化图像，增进了对混合磁相互作用体系自旋动力学的理解。

Abstract: We present magnetic-field-dependent evolution of magnetization and spin dynamics in the quasi-two-dimensional spin-$1/2$ magnet $\mathrm{Cu_2(OH)_3Br}$, consisting of alternately coupled ferromagnetic Cu1 and antiferromagnetic Cu2 spin chains. Terahertz spectroscopy reveals a pronounced field-direction dependence of the low-energy magnetic excitation spectrum. For magnetic fields applied perpendicular to the spin chains, $B\parallel a$ and $B\parallel c^*$, the spectra undergo abrupt reconstructions at the spin-flop transitions identified independently by high-field magnetization measurements. For $B\parallel b$, by contrast, no spin-flop occurs; instead, the excitation spectrum evolves continuously with field and exhibits a strong terahertz radiation polarization dependence as the ferromagnetic Cu1 subsystem becomes progressively polarized. At higher fields, the complex low-field spectrum is replaced by a reduced set of broad excitations, consistent with a weakening of the coupling between the ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic chain subsystems. Complementary Raman spectroscopy resolves magnon and spinon excitations alongside several phonon modes and traces the characteristic temperature- and magnetic-field-dependent evolution of the magnetic excitations. The combined spectroscopic and magnetization results map out how a magnetic field reorganizes the coupled ferromagnetic and antiferromagnetic subsystems in $\mathrm{Cu_2(OH)_3Br}$ across field-induced phase transitions.

</details>


### [164] [Signatures of spin-wave dynamics in quasiparticle interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06398)
*Alireza Akbari,Peter Thalmeier*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究局域-巡游反铁磁体中动量分辨的准粒子干涉（QPI），发现静态反铁磁序重构能带，而单磁子过程通过自能产生动力学位移和展宽。QPI谱在低能磁子发射边以下以实部主导的色散修正为主，以上则出现虚部导致的强度展宽与重分布，且动态响应在更高能仍存在并对隧穿偏压呈强不对称。结果表明傅里叶变换隧穿谱可区分静态磁重构与动态自旋波重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域-巡游反铁磁体系中电子与磁子耦合如何影响准粒子干涉，并判断QPI能否作为区分静态磁序和动态自旋波重整化的实验探针。

Method: 将静态反铁磁序导致的能带重构与单磁子过程产生的动量、频率相关自能结合，在Born杂质散射框架下直接纳入动力学重整化，计算并分析QPI谱的演化。

Result: QPI谱呈现特征性交叉：低于低能磁子发射边时主要受自能实部影响的色散修正；高于该能标后自能虚部引起显著展宽和散射强度再分布。动力学响应可延续到高磁子能以上，并在粒子-空穴不对称能带中对隧穿偏压表现出强不对称。

Conclusion: 傅里叶变换隧穿谱能够区分静态反铁磁能带重构与动态磁子自能重整化，为研究局域-巡游反铁磁体的电子结构提供了有效手段。

Abstract: We investigate momentum-resolved quasiparticle interference (QPI) in a localized-itinerant antiferromagnet, where ordered local moments are exchange coupled to conduction electrons. Static antiferromagnetic order reconstructs the electronic bands, while one-magnon processes further dress the quasiparticles through a momentum- and frequency-dependent self-energy. Incorporating this dynamical renormalization directly into the Born impurity scattering, we find a characteristic crossover in the QPI spectrum. Below the lower magnon-emission edge, the modification is predominantly dispersive and governed by the real part of the self-energy, whereas above this scale its imaginary part produces pronounced broadening and redistribution of the scattering intensity. The dynamical response persists beyond the upper magnon energy and is strongly asymmetric in tunneling bias for a particle--hole-asymmetric band. These results show that Fourier-transform tunneling spectroscopy can distinguish static magnetic reconstruction from dynamical spin-wave renormalization.

</details>


### [165] [NMR evidence of pressure-induced structural transition and enhanced spin fluctuations up to 14~GPa in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06472)
*Zhanlong Wu,Kefan Du,Shuo Li,Tong Shi,Ying Chen,Qingxin Dong,Rui Zhou,Rong Yu,Juanjuan Liu,Bosen Wang,Jinguang Cheng,Weiqiang Yu,Yi Cui*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高压11B核磁共振测量（最高14 GPa），揭示了SrCu2(BO3)2中存在两个压力诱导的单斜相，并明确了其压力-温度相图中的新量子态：低温下的无隙三维反铁磁相、中温区的二维短程有序反铁磁相，以及高温区的扩展关联顺磁区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrCu2(BO3)2作为研究磁阻挫导致量子相与量子相变的重要平台，其压力诱导的结构与磁相变一直存在争议，亟需通过实验手段澄清高压下的真实物相与临界行为。

Method: 采用高压11B核磁共振（NMR）技术，在高达14 GPa的压力范围内测量SrCu2(BO3)2的NMR谱及自旋-晶格弛豫率1/T1，并结合谱线劈裂、展宽和弛豫率的温度/场依赖关系分析相变与临界行为。

Result: 发现两种压力诱导的单斜相；在4 GPa以上、10 K以下，谱线快速展宽及1/T1的幂律行为证实无隙3D反铁磁相；在20 K附近出现依赖场的谱线劈裂，表明存在2D短程有序反铁磁相；30 K以上1/T1呈次线性幂律，对应扩展的关联顺磁态。

Conclusion: 该研究为SrCu2(BO3)2高压下长期争议的磁相图提供了明确的NMR证据，揭示了从三维长程有序到二维短程有序再到关联顺磁态的演化序列，对理解阻挫量子磁性具有重要意义。

Abstract: The Shastry-Sutherland compound SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ has attracted considerable interest as a platform for exploring quantum phases and quantum phase transitions driven by magnetic frustration. The pressure-induced structural and magnetic phase transitions in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$, however, remain controversial. To address this issue, we performed high-pressure $^{11}$B nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) measurements on SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ up to 14~GPa. The NMR spectra reveal two pressure-induced monoclinic phases. With pressure above 4~GPa and with temperature below 10~K, the rapid broadening of the NMR spectrum and the power-law behavior in the spin-lattice relaxation rate $1/T_1$ provide clear evidence for a gapless 3D antiferromagnetic (AFM) phase in the monoclinic phase. At an intermediate temperature range around 20~K, the emergence of the field-dependent NMR line splits resolves a two-dimensional, short-range ordered AFM phase; at temperature above 30~K, the sublinear power-law behavior of $1/T_1$ identifies an extended correlated paramagnetic regime.

</details>


### [166] [Electronic correlations and fluctuating lattice distortions in vanadium dioxide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06707)
*Antonio Picano,Martin Eckstein,Francesco Grandi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机半经典动力学平均场论扩展，将关联电子与涨落晶格畸变联合演化，适用于非线性电子-声子耦合和多耦合晶格模式，并应用于VO2单斜相两种对称性不同的畸变，发现两个畸变在不同温度熔化，产生绝缘体、中间金属和高对称相，其中一种跃迁依赖晶格涨落熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关联材料中的金属-绝缘体转变常伴随晶体结构变化，但传统相干晶格近似只考虑经典畸变坐标，忽略晶格熵，而电子和晶格熵的分配往往决定转变温度。已有方法局限于线性耦合和单一模式，无法描述非线性多模耦合及晶格涨落效应。

Method: 发展了动力平均场论的随机半经典扩展，将关联电子与涨落晶格畸变同时演化，支持非线性电子-声子耦合和多个耦合晶格模式。得到的朗之万方程包含由关联电子子系统自洽生成的确定性力、阻尼和关联噪声，且采用非平衡动力平均场论处理电子子系统。

Result: 对VO2单斜相最小双轨道模型应用，发现两种对称性不同的畸变在明确分离的温度下熔化，产生绝缘相、中间金属相和高对称相。两个转变机制不同：二聚化熔化由电子关联驱动，相干晶格近似即可描述；而高对称结构恢复需要晶格涨落熵的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架能够捕获晶格涨落熵驱动的结构转变，并因采用非平衡动力平均场论，未来可扩展到具有非热电子分布的光激发系统。

Abstract: Metal-insulator transitions in correlated materials are often accompanied by a change of crystal structure. They are commonly described within a coherent lattice approximation, which combines a correlated treatment of the electrons with a lattice represented by one or a few classical distortion coordinates fixed by minimizing an energy. The structural degrees of freedom then carry no entropy of their own, even though the partition of the transition entropy between electrons and lattice is often what decides the transition temperature. Here we develop a stochastic semiclassical extension of dynamical mean-field theory in which correlated electrons and fluctuating lattice distortions are evolved together, extending previous formulations from linear to nonlinear electron-phonon interactions and from a single to several coupled lattice modes. The resulting non-conservative Langevin equations have deterministic forces, damping, and correlated noise generated self-consistently by the interacting electronic subsystem. Applying the approach to a minimal two-orbital model containing the two symmetry-distinct distortions of the monoclinic phase of vanadium dioxide, we find that the two distortions melt at well separated temperatures, giving an insulating, an intermediate metallic, and a high-symmetry phase. The two transitions have different origins: the melting of the dimerization is driven by the coupling to the correlated electrons and is captured already within the coherent lattice approximation, whereas the restoration of the undistorted structure requires the entropy of the fluctuating lattice. Since the electronic subsystem is treated within nonequilibrium dynamical mean-field theory, the framework can be extended in future work to photoexcited systems with nonthermal electronic distributions.

</details>


### [167] [Ginzburg-Landau Theory for Non-Invertible Symmetry-Breaking Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06751)
*Vibhu Ravindran,Luisa Eck,Xie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文遵循Ginzburg-Landau精神，利用对称拓扑场论（SymTFT） formalism，为超出传统朗道范式、由非可逆对称性破缺描述的(1+1)D拓扑/退禁闭相变建立场论描述，并以Rep($S_3$)对称性为例展示如何从(2+1)D拓扑序的Lagrangian代数提取局域序参量代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义朗道范式提出一类非传统相变可理解为广义对称性的对称性破缺相变，尤其(1+1)D中的拓扑或退禁闭转变可映射为非可逆对称性破缺。然而如何从该理解提取普适动力学性质仍不清楚，因此需要类似Ginzburg-Landau的场论框架来描述这些临界点的局域涨落。

Method: 采用Ginzburg-Landau哲学，用非可逆对称性的局域序参量涨落构建临界点的场论描述。核心工具是对称拓扑场论（SymTFT），通过(2+1)D拓扑序的Lagrangian代数识别(1+1)D局域序参量所满足的代数结构，并系统演示完整步骤。

Result: 以具有简单非可逆对称性Rep($S_3$)的gapped相之间的转变为实例，完整展示了广义Ginzburg-Landau程序的可行性，说明如何利用SymTFT formalism从拓扑数据获得描述相变临界行为的场论。

Conclusion: 该工作为非可逆对称性破缺描述的相变提供了系统的场论构造方法，将Ginzburg-Landau框架推广至非可逆对称性情形，有助于进一步理解这类相变的普适动力学性质。

Abstract: Recently, a generalized Landau paradigm has been proposed, in which a broad class of "unconventional" phase transitions lying beyond the original symmetry-breaking framework of Landau are understood as the symmetry-breaking transitions of generalized symmetries. In particular, in (1+1)D, such "topological" or "deconfined" transitions can be mapped to the symmetry-breaking transitions of non-invertible symmetries. How to extract the universal dynamical properties of the transitions from this understanding? In this paper, we follow the Ginzburg-Landau philosophy and develop a field-theoretic description of such critical points in terms of the fluctuations of local order parameters, now of non-invertible symmetries. The Symmetry Topological Field Theory formalism plays a central role in our analysis, allowing us to identify the algebra of local order parameters from the Lagrangian algebra of a (2+1)D topological order. We illustrate the key ideas and the full steps of this generalized Ginzburg-Landau procedure using the transitions between gapped phases with a simple non-invertible symmetry --- the Rep($S_3$) symmetry.

</details>


### [168] [Spin--orbit-enhanced correlation sensitivity and anomalous magnetic response in non-dimerized $4d^4$ ilmenite CdRuO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06902)
*Yuya Haraguchi,Hayato Yatsuzuka,Hiroko Aruga Katori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: CdRuO₃是名义Ru⁴⁺ 4d⁴的钛铁矿结构，具有非二聚化R-3̅结构、等价的Ru-Ru键和强畸变的RuO₆八面体；实验表现非金属非Arrhenius电阻率、有限残余线性比热和弱非单调磁化率，理论表明自旋轨道耦合显著增强电子关联效应并在U_eff=2.5 eV时打开约55 meV直接带隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究钌基钛铁矿CdRuO₃中Ru 4d⁴电子在边共享蜂窝层内的关联与自旋轨道耦合协同效应，检验理论预测的非二聚化结构分支以及多轨道金属态是否成立。

Method: 使用粉末X射线衍射确定晶体结构；测量压制颗粒电阻率、比热和磁化率；进行PBE+U、PBE+SOC+U及匹配的非磁性计算，考察U_eff对电子结构的影响。

Result: CdRuO₃为非二聚化R-3̅结构，Ru-Ru键等价但RuO₆畸变强烈；电阻率非金属但非Arrhenius，比热存在有限线性项。PBE+U无SOC时在U_eff≤3 eV保持金属或半金属，而PBE+SOC+U对U_eff非常敏感，在U_eff=2.5 eV时于Γ点打开约55 meV直接带隙。扣除稀薄居里-外斯缺陷贡献后，磁化率弱非单调，既不符合普通泡利响应，也不符合独立自旋S=1图像。

Conclusion: CdRuO₃实现了钌钛铁矿预测的非二聚化结构分支，但并非简单的稳定多轨道金属，而是呈现由自旋轨道耦合与关联共同作用的复杂电子态。

Abstract: We report the synthesis and physical properties of CdRuO$_3$, a nominal Ru$^{4+}$ $4d^4$ ilmenite with edge-sharing RuO$_6$ honeycomb layers. Powder X-ray diffraction establishes a crystallographically non-dimerized $R\bar{3}$ structure with equivalent Ru-Ru bonds and a strongly distorted RuO$_6$ environment. The compacted-pellet resistivity is nonmetallic but non-Arrhenius, while the heat capacity contains a finite residual linear term. Matched nonmagnetic calculations show that PBE+$U$ without spin-orbit coupling remains metallic or semimetallic up to $U_{\mathrm{eff}} = 3$ eV, whereas PBE+SOC+$U$ exhibits a strong $U_{\mathrm{eff}}$ dependence and opens a direct gap of approximately 55 meV at $Γ$ for $U_{\mathrm{eff}} = 2.5$ eV. Spin-orbit coupling therefore markedly enhances the correlation sensitivity of the non-cubic Ru $t_{2g}$ manifold. After subtraction of a dilute Curie-Weiss defect contribution, the susceptibility remains weakly nonmonotonic and is inconsistent with both an ordinary Pauli response and independent spin-only $S = 1$ moments. CdRuO$_3$ thus realizes the non-dimerized structural branch predicted for ruthenium ilmenites, but not a simple robust multiorbital metal.

</details>


### [169] [Raman signatures of a non-reciprocal magnetic phase transition in Ca$_2$RuO$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07565)
*Giacomo Jarc,Giovanni Tartaglia,Francesco Gabriele,Filomena Forte,Anita Guarino,Angela Montanaro,Enrico Maria Rigoni,Nitesh Khatiwada,Costanza Lincetto,Gabriele Bartolini,Antonio Mastropasqua,Shahla Yasmin Mathengattil,Marco Malvestuto,Muhammad Waqee Ur Rehman,Rosalba Fittipaldi,Joachim Deisenhofer,Alexander A. Tsirlin,Antonio Vecchione,Mario Cuoco,Daniele Fausti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ca₂RuO₄在低温下呈现反常Mott绝缘态，伴有类Higgs振幅模式。研究发现沿c轴的外加磁场驱动一个磁性一级相变，表现为新的磁性模式出现及声子与Higgs模式耦合改变，两者均显示非互易磁滞，但面内磁振子不受影响，暗示进入四极子场控态，并触发晶格与Higgs模式的杂化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Ca₂RuO₄中强电子关联、磁弹耦合和自旋轨道耦合相互作用导致的独特磁行为，尤其是低温柔磁性绝缘态及其类Higgs激发特性，并探索磁场调控下可能的新相变和量子态。

Method: 利用外加沿c轴的磁场下的拉曼散射测量，观察磁场诱导的磁性模式出现、声子与Higgs振幅模式耦合变化，并通过分析磁滞回线和磁振子散射特征判断相变性质与磁性序演化。

Result: 观测到磁场驱动的磁性一级相变：出现新的磁起源模式，声子与Higgs模式的耦合被显著修改，二者均呈现非互易磁滞；但面内磁振子拉曼散射不受影响，证明偶极反铁磁序保持。

Conclusion: 相变后的状态可能是一种新的四极子场控态，其涨落结构触发晶格与磁Higgs模式杂化，从而产生观测到的拉曼特征，为理解该材料磁场诱导量子相变提供了关键光谱证据。

Abstract: The magnetic behavior of Ca$_2$RuO$_4$ represents a unique puzzle due to the interplay of strong electronic correlations, magneto-elastic interactions, and large spin-orbit coupling. At low temperatures, an anomalous Mott insulating state emerges, characterized by a complex antiferromagnetic order with a collective amplitude excitation of the magnetic moment which has been discussed in analogy with the Higgs mode. We report here evidence of a magnetic first-order phase transition driven by an out-of-plane magnetic field along the crystallographic $c$-axis of Ca$_2$RuO$_4$. Raman measurements in magnetic field reveal the emergence of mode of magnetic origin and a concomitant modification of the coupling between a phonon and the Higgs amplitude mode. Both the Raman features are characterized by a non-reciprocal hysteresis in magnetic field. Surprisingly, the observed phase transition does not affect the Raman scattering from the in-plane magnons, indicating that the dipolar antiferromagnetic order is preserved. This is consistent with the onset of a new quadrupolar field-controlled state, whose fluctuations structure can trigger an hybridization between the lattice and the magnetic Higgs mode resulting in the observed Raman features.

</details>


### [170] [Exact fermionic dual of the Bose-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07830)
*Lei Su,Ivar Martin,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过费米化规范（广义Jordan-Wigner变换），将具有全局U(1)对称性的Bose-Hubbard模型精确映射为玻色子-费米子复合体的费米模型；在一维中该对偶覆盖软芯相互作用，低能下归结为sine-Gordon与Thirring模型的等价，并用DMRG验证了Luttinger定理的新表现及紧致玻色子CFT描述的临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作确立了带Z2对称性的精确玻色化/费米化对偶，但如何将这种费米化规范应用于具有连续U(1)对称性的Bose-Hubbard模型，从而获得超越硬芯极限的精确费米子描述，仍待发展。作者旨在建立适用于软芯相互作用的精确对偶，并揭示其低能物理与Luttinger定理的新联系。

Method: 利用费米化规范实现广义Jordan-Wigner变换，对Bose-Hubbard模型进行精确对偶，构造由玻色子和费米子组成的费米复合体；在一维推导其对偶形式并分析低能极限；使用密度矩阵重正化群(DMRG)数值验证对偶关系、相图与临界性质；讨论向高维和一般玻色系统的推广。

Result: 在1D得到扩展硬芯BH模型与无自旋Fermi-Hubbard模型精确映射的推广，现可覆盖软芯相互作用；低能下恢复sine-Gordon与Thirring模型的已知等价性；无能隙相中费米复合体关联函数的振荡波矢由其密度固定，这是Luttinger定理的新体现；DMRG验证了该精确对偶，并显示无能隙相和相变由紧致玻色子CFT描述。

Conclusion: 该构造为玻色系统与费米系统之间提供了新的精确对偶路径，并能自然推广到一般玻色系统及更高维度，为光晶格中玻色-费米混合物及其他强关联量子系统的研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Recent developments have established exact bosonization and fermionization with a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry as dualities through gauging. In this work, we apply fermionic gauging, which realizes generalized Jordan-Wigner transformations, to the Bose-Hubbard (BH) model with a global $U(1)$ symmetry and derive an exact dual description in terms of fermionic composites, built from bosons and fermions. In 1D, this duality generalizes the exact mapping between the extended hard-core BH model and the spinless Fermi-Hubbard model to now cover soft-core interactions. At low energies, the mapping reduces to the well-known equivalence between the sine-Gordon model and the Thirring model. The oscillation wave vector of the fermionic composite correlation function in the gapless phase is fixed by their density, providing a novel manifestation of Luttinger's theorem. We verify the exact duality using density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations and demonstrate that the gapless phase and the phase transition are governed by the compact boson conformal field theory. Our construction naturally extends to generic bosonic systems and higher dimensions, opening new avenues for studying Bose-Fermi mixtures in optical lattices and other strongly correlated quantum systems.

</details>


### [171] [Hysteretic Coherence Collapse Across the First Order CDW Transition in 1T-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07923)
*Turgut Yilmaz,Anil Rajapitamahuni,Asish K. Kundu,Menka Jain,Elio Vescovo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过ARPES研究1T-TaS2中近公度（NC-CDW）与公度（C-CDW）电荷密度波相变，发现C-CDW相中靠近费米能级的带隙内态在加热进入NC-CDW相时突然消失、冷却回C-CDW相时重新出现，表现出明显的热滞回线，直接提供了该一级相变的谱学证据，且该态的变化与电阻率变化紧密关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-TaS2中NC-CDW到C-CDW的一级相变对其奇异电子行为至关重要，但低能电子结构在此相变过程中的谱学演化尚不清楚，尤其是C-CDW相中的平带和带隙内态的起源及其与相变的关系需要深入理解。

Method: 采用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES），结合光子能量依赖测量区分不同低能特征态的谱权重演化，并进行变温ARPES测量，覆盖加热和冷却循环以追踪电子结构随温度的变化，同时与电阻率变化进行关联。

Result: C-CDW相中观测到通常与下Hubbard带相关的平带，以及一个更靠近费米能级的独特带隙内态。光子能量依赖测量显示二者谱权重演化不同。变温测量表明带隙内态在加热进入NC-CDW相时突然坍缩，冷却返回C-CDW相时又急剧重现，呈现明显热滞，且该态的消失与恢复与电阻率变化高度同步。

Conclusion: 带隙内态的突变性消失与恢复及其热滞行为直接证实了C-CDW/NC-CDW转变的一级相变本质，并表明该带隙内态与跨越相变的电子结构重构密切相关，可能是理解1T-TaS2中奇异电子性质的关键。

Abstract: The first order phase transition between the nearly commensurate (NC-CDW) and commensurate (C-CDW) charge density wave phases in 1T-TaS2 underpins its exotic electronic behavior, yet the spectroscopic evolution of the low energy electronic structure across this transition remains crucial to understand. Using angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), we investigate the low temperature C-CDW phase, characterized by a flat band commonly associated with the lower Hubbard band and a distinct in-gap state located closer to the Fermi level. Photon energy dependent measurements distinguish these two low energy features through their different spectral weight evolution. Temperature dependent ARPES across heating and cooling cycles reveals that the in-gap state undergoes an abrupt collapse upon heating into the NC-CDW phase and re-emerges sharply upon cooling back into the C-CDW phase. This pronounced thermal hysteresis provides direct spectroscopic evidence of the first order nature of the transition. Furthermore, the disappearance and recovery of the in-gap state closely track the corresponding changes in resistivity, highlighting its intimate connection to the electronic reconstruction across the C-CDW/NC-CDW phase transition.

</details>


### [172] [Vertical quantum confinement in bulk MoS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07950)
*Jairo Obando-Guevara,Álvaro González-García,Marcin Rosmus,Natalia Olszowska,César González,Miguel Ángel González-Barrio,Antonio Tejeda,Arantzazu Mascaraque*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过ARPES实验在块体MoS2中直接观测到量子限制态，确认其为垂直量子阱态，并发现其束缚能随量子数呈线性而非二次依赖，归因于机械剥离形成的多层堆叠与类抛物量子阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类块体MoS2中探索是否存在可观测的量子限制效应，并理解其形成机制及能带色散特性，为研究多层堆叠中的量子现象和光学性质提供途径。

Method: 使用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）测量块体MoS2的电子结构，分析k_z色散、光子能量依赖的强度调制以及量子阱态束缚能与量子数的关系，并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算验证。

Result: 在Γ点观察到量子阱态；这些态在k_z方向无色散且随光子能量出现显著强度调制，证实垂直量子限制；束缚能随n呈线性关系而非二维电子气的二次关系，符合类抛物线量子阱；DFT支持机械剥离致多层堆叠脱离块体的解释。

Conclusion: 块体MoS2中可由机械剥离诱向量子限制，该现象不仅可用于探索子带间跃迁以调控光学特性，也为研究不同厚度多层堆叠中的基础量子现象提供了新手段。

Abstract: We experimentally observe quantum confinement states in bulk MoS$_2$ using Angle-Resolved Photoemission Spectroscopy (ARPES). The band structure at the $\overlineΓ$ point reveals quantum well states (QWSs) linked to vertical quantum confinement of the electrons, confirmed by the absence of dispersion in k$_z$ and a strong intensity modulation with photon energy. Notably, the binding energy dependence of the QWSs vs $n$ does not follow the quadratic dependence of a two-dimensional electron gas. Instead, a linear behaviour is observed that is consistent with a parabolic-like quantum well. This confinement arises from the mechanical exfoliation preparation method, which leads to the detachment of a multilayer stack from the underlying bulk. This is confirmed by Density Functional Theory (DFT) calculations. The quantum confinement in bulk-like MoS$_2$ not only offers the opportunity to explore intersubband transitions to exploit optical properties but also provides a means to study fundamental quantum phenomena in multilayer stacks of different thicknesses.

</details>


### [173] [Selective coupling of high-order phonons in La2-xSrxCuO4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08076)
*Ke-Jun Xu,Paulina Majchrzak,Nathan Giles-Donovan,Sangheon Kim,Jaewon Choi,Jun Okamoto,Ganesha Channagowdra,Hsiao-Yu Huang,Di-Jing Huang,Seiki Komiya,Takao Sasagawa,Shuichi Wakimoto,Wei-Sheng Lee,Zhi-Xun Shen,Thomas P. Devereaux,Dung-Hai Lee,Robert J. Birgeneau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）在铜氧化物超导体La2-xSrxCuO4中发现了一种新的电子-声子耦合机制：仅在约85、180和330 meV处出现尖锐且无色散的激发峰，分别对应Cu-O键拉伸声子的基频、双声子和四声子高阶激发，且显著区别于拉曼光谱；该反常偶数阶选择性耦合随掺杂迅速消失，提示可能源于绝缘铜氧化物中的局域配对准粒子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统晶体中载流子与晶格振动强耦合时，高阶振动激发遵循Franck-Condon包络；而铜氧化物高温超导体中存在强电子-声子耦合，需要探测其是否呈现常规行为或存在非传统机制，尤其要理解电荷动力学与晶格自由度在新兴电子相中的作用。

Method: 利用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）对La2-xSrxCuO4进行测量，从未掺杂到10%空穴掺杂范围内追踪低能激发谱，并与拉曼光谱及相关声子模式进行对比，分析激发的能量位置、线宽、色散关系和掺杂演化。

Result: 未掺杂样品中观察到约85±5 meV、180±7 meV和330±5 meV的三个尖锐且无色散的峰，能量分别对应Cu-O面内键拉伸声子、双声子与四声子激发；该能区内其他拉曼活性模式强度可忽略不计。在10%掺杂下这些高阶声子峰已不可分辨，低能激发被具有色散的等离激元主导，表明该现象显著依赖掺杂且与常规Franck-Condon图像存在鲜明冲突。

Conclusion: 观测到的偶数阶声子选择性耦合以及在能量、强度上与拉曼谱的系统性差异，不能用常规Franck-Condon电子-声子耦合解释，暗示绝缘铜氧化物中存在非传统耦合机制，可能涉及局域配对的准粒子，为理解高温超导相关电子-声子耦合与配对机理提供了新线索。

Abstract: In crystals and molecules with strong coupling between the charge carriers and atomic vibrations, high order harmonics of the vibrational excitations can be observed with intensity following the Franck-Condon envelope. Here, we uncover a new regime of electron-phonon coupling in La2-xSrxCuO4 using resonant inelastic X-ray scattering. In the undoped compound, we find sharp peaks at approximately 85$\pm$5 meV, 180$\pm$7 meV, and 330$\pm$5 meV with no observable dispersion, and an absence of appreciable intensity from other modes observed in Raman spectroscopy in this energy range. The 180 meV and 330 meV excitations have energies approximately consistent with two-phonon and four-phonon in-plane Cu-O bond stretching mode, suggesting that the resonantly excited valence electrons strongly couple to this mode. These excitations exhibit significant doping dependence, eventually becoming unresolvable at 10% hole doping, where the low energy charge excitations are dominated by dispersive plasmons. The observation of selective coupling to even-ordered phonons and stark contrast with the Raman spectra indicate anomalous electron-phonon coupling beyond the Franck-Condon picture. One intriguing possibility is the existence of locally paired quasiparticles in insulating cuprates.

</details>


### [174] [Textures as a phase-transition probe for quantum spin chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08120)
*Heitor P. Casagrande,Isaac M. Carvalho,William J. Munro,Krissia Zawadzki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子纹理的粗糙度度量在量子相变探测中的应用，证明其能有效识别Heisenberg XXZ模型的相变点，尤其在Δ=1处表现出尖锐特征，可能与BKT转变和各向同性SU(2)点的自旋关联层次结构相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纹理作为量化相干性和量子门识别的工具已被提出，但其在量子相变中的应用尚未充分探索。本文旨在验证粗糙度度量能否作为简单有效的相变探测工具，并揭示其与自旋关联层次的关系。

Method: 建立计算基下粗糙度与自旋关联函数层次的联系，定义全局基态粗糙度（global rugosity）和不同磁化扇区基态粗糙度（symmetry-resolved rugosity），用于研究Heisenberg XXZ模型的相图，并分析两个转变点处的行为。

Result: 两个相变点均出现不同的粗糙度特征，尤其Δ=1处即使在小系统下也呈现尖锐特征，表明纹理编码的关联层次对该点高度敏感。

Conclusion: 粗糙度度量可作为量子相变探测的有效简单工具，其对XXZ模型各向同性SU(2)点的敏感性可能反映了BKT转变处自旋关联层次结构的特殊性质。

Abstract: The idea of quantum texture has been recently proposed and used as a tool for quantifying coherences and for quantum gate identification. In this work we offer a study on its usage to quantum phase transitions, demonstrating the rugosity metric as a simple tool for effective phase-transition probing. We establish the link between rugosity in the computational basis and the hierarchy of spin correlators, and analyze rugosities defined in the global ground-state and in ground-states belonging to different magnetization sectors (to which we refer to as global vs symmetry-resolved rugosities) to study the phase diagram of the Heisenberg XXZ model. We find distinct rugosity signatures at both transition points. In particular, a sharp feature appears at $Δ=1$ already for small systems, revealing a pronounced sensitivity of the correlation hierarchy encoded by the texture to this point. Since the BKT transition coincides with the isotropic $SU(2)$ point of the XXZ model, this behavior may reflect a particular sensitivity of rugosity to the structure of the spin-correlation hierarchy at isotropy.

</details>


### [175] [Observation of multipartite spin entanglement in a cuprate chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08134)
*S. F. R. TenHuisen,Z. Shen,V. Bhartiya,V. Menon,P. Sharma,H. Padma,Z. Guan,W. He,M. K. Lajer,J. Li,D. Banerjee,J. Pelliciari,I. A. Zaliznyak,G. D. Gu,M. D. Lukin,S. Johnston,M. P. M. Dean,V. Bisogni,Y. Wang,M. Mitrano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示利用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）测量多体自旋纠缠，通过将RIXS截面投影到自旋激发通道，直接从自旋涨落谱提取量子Fisher信息，并在准一维铜氧化物Sr2CuO3中探测到至少7体自旋纠缠，这是固体中迄今报道的最大纠缠深度之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料被认为承载高度纠缠的量子态，但如何在实验中直接探测和量化这种纠缠一直是一个长期未解决的难题。

Method: 使用共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）作为多体自旋纠缠探针；将RIXS散射截面投影到适当的自旋激发通道，证明可以从测量的自旋涨落谱中直接提取量子Fisher信息。

Result: 在模型一维铜酸盐Sr2CuO3中检测到至少7体自旋纠缠，这是固体中迄今报道的最大纠缠深度之一；观测到的纠缠深度与Hubbard模型预期一致，并在高温下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了RIXS作为实验探测多体自旋纠缠的有效工具，并为将测量扩展到电荷、轨道以及非平衡态体系提供了平台。

Abstract: Quantum materials are believed to host highly entangled states of matter, but probing and quantifying such entanglement has long remained experimentally elusive. Here, we use resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) as a probe of multipartite spin entanglement. By projecting the RIXS cross section onto the appropriate spin excitation channel, we show that the Quantum Fisher Information can be extracted directly from the measured spin fluctuation spectrum. We detect at least 7-partite spin entanglement in the model one-dimensional cuprate Sr$_2$CuO$_3$, revealing one of the largest entanglement depths yet reported in a solid. The observed entanglement depth agrees with Hubbard-model expectations and persists to elevated temperatures. Our results establish RIXS as an experimental probe of many-body spin entanglement and provide a platform for extensions to charge and orbital sectors and nonequilibrium settings.

</details>


### [176] [Successive Phase Transitions from a Composite Fermion Liquid to a Fractional Quantum Hall State at ν=3/2 Driven by In-Plane Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08141)
*Xinghao Wang,L. N. Pfeiffer,A. Gupta,K. W. Baldwin,K. W. West,Rui-Rui Du*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在超高迁移率GaAs量子阱中，仅靠面内磁场B_||即诱导出ν=3/2偶数分母分数量子霍尔态；随B_||增大，系统依次从复合费米子液体转变为软能隙FQH态，再经拓扑相变进入硬能隙强健FQH态，并伴随ν=19/13子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索偶数分母分数量子霍尔态能否在纯面内磁场条件下产生，并理解其背后的拓扑相变机制与拓扑序工程途径。

Method: 在超高迁移率GaAs量子阱中系统改变面内磁场B_||，通过输运测量追踪ν=3/2附近量子态演化，分析能隙性质、相变临界场及伴随的子态，并讨论可能的理论图像。

Result: 观察到两次连续跃迁：约B_||=12.2T时由复合费米子液体进入软能隙FQH态；约B_||=14.7T时经拓扑相变进入硬能隙强健FQH态，同时出现ν=19/13的伴随态。

Conclusion: 面内磁场通过k空间费米轮廓劈裂可实现拓扑序的调控与诱导，为构造新奇偶数分母分数量子霍尔态提供了新思路。

Abstract: We report an even-denominator fractional quantum Hall state at ν = 3/2 induced entirely by in-plane magnetic field B_|| in an ultra-high-mobility GaAs quantum well. As B_|| increases, the system undergoes two successive transitions: from a composite fermion liquid to a soft-gap FQH state (B_||~12.2 T), then via a topological phase transition to a hard-gap robust FQH state (B_||~14.7 T), accompanied by a daughter state at ν = 19/13. We present systematic data, and discuss a possible scenario in interpreting these findings. Our work demonstrates that topological order may be engineered through k-space Fermi contour splitting under an in-plane magnetic field.

</details>


### [177] [Kane-Lubensky phonons in Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08262)
*Hong-Hao Song,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明，在具有自旋-晶格耦合的烧绿石阻挫反铁磁体（如铬基尖晶石体系）中，通过磁场选择特定自旋态并诱导晶格畸变，可将系统转化为具有P4_3 32对称性的拓扑Maxwell晶格；该体系中可存在受拓扑保护、非Goldstone模式的零频Weyl线声子，其低温声子比热呈T^2行为而非传统Goldstone声子的T^3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维Maxwell晶格中是否存在Kane-Lubensky型零能Weyl线声子，并寻找可实现这类拓扑零模的实际材料平台；同时希望建立拓扑零频声子与其他自由度强耦合的低能体系，以催生奇异声子介导现象。

Method: 理论上结合磁拓扑力学与自旋-晶格耦合模型，以烧绿石阻挫反铁磁体为对象；通过外加磁场选择自旋构型，使晶格发生畸变并弯曲相邻键，从而构造拓扑Maxwell晶格；随后分析其声子谱、拓扑保护性质及低温热力学行为。

Result: 系统呈现出受拓扑保护的体零频Weyl线声子，且这些无隙模并非Goldstone模；由一维零模流形导致低温声子比热C_ph~T^2，区别于常规Goldstone声子的C_ph~T^3；该行为可望在Cr基尖晶石体系中实现。

Conclusion: 研究建立了阻挫磁体中拓扑Maxwell力学与自旋自由度耦合的低能平台，为大量拓扑零频声子与其他自由度强耦合提供了可能，开辟了探索声子介导奇异现象和材料应用的新途径。

Abstract: We show that zero-energy gapless Weyl-line phonons of the Kane-Lubensky's type can arise in the three-dimensional Maxwell lattice frustrated Mott insulators through the magnetopological mechanics. In a pyrochlore antiferromagnet with the spin-lattice coupling, a magnetic field selects the spin state whose lattice distortion generates a $P4_3 32$ topological lattice. More crucially, the spin-lattice coupling and the spin configuration cause the bending of the neighbouring bonds, and converts the system into the topological Maxwell lattice. Remarkably, the resulting system is found to host the bulk zero-frequency Weyl-line phonons protected topologically, and these gapless phonons are not Goldstone modes. Unlike the conventional ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^3}$ for the Goldstone phonons, these one-dimensional zero-mode manifolds yield a characteristic low-temperature phonon specific heat ${C_{\rm ph}\sim T^2}$. Our results could find applications in the Cr-based spinel systems, and moreover, we establish a low-energy platform where an extensive number of topological zero-frequency phonons can strongly couple to other degrees of freedom, opening a route to exotic phonon-mediated phenomena.

</details>


### [178] [Theory of Kondo hybridization wave in Kondo lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08467)
*Yin Zhong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于方形晶格Kondo晶格模型的大N费米子平均场理论，提出了重费米子体系中涌现的Kondo杂化波（KHW）序的微观理论，系统确定了稳定的调制KHW相及相图，并解释了UTe2和1T/1H-TaS2中的实验观测，预言了用于区分不同KHW态的面内电导率各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近STM实验在重费米子超导体UTe2和人工Kondo晶格体系1T/1H-TaS2中发现了空间调制的Kondo杂化波序，这与传统重费米子物理中Kondo杂化空间均匀的范式相矛盾，迫切需要微观理论解释其起源和性质。

Method: 采用规范方形晶格Kondo晶格模型，在大N费米子平均场近似下发展微观理论，系统搜索并识别稳定的调制KHW相，建立基态相图，并对比实验表征其谱学与输运特征。

Result: 发现主要的Q=(0,π) KHW相产生单轴条纹调制的Kondo杂化能隙，能复现UTe2中的空间调制模式；其固有的晶胞加倍调制正好解释1T/1H-TaS2中的谱学特征；并预言了面内电导率各向异性可作为区分不同波矢KHW态的有效输运判据。

Conclusion: 该工作为实验观测到的KHW序提供了微观基础，建立了理解重费米子材料中涌现调制杂化现象的统一理论框架。

Abstract: Recent scanning tunneling microscopy experiments have discovered emergent spatially modulated Kondo hybridization wave (KHW) order in the heavy fermion superconductor UTe$_2$ and the artificial Kondo lattice system 1T/1H-TaS$_2$, challenging the conventional paradigm of spatially uniform Kondo hybridization in heavy fermion physics. Here, we develop a microscopic theory for KHW order based on the canonical square-lattice Kondo lattice model within the large-$N$ fermionic mean-field approximation. We systematically identify stable modulated KHW phases and establish their ground-state phase diagram. The prominent $\boldsymbol{Q}=(0,π)$ KHW phase yields uniaxial stripe modulation of the Kondo hybridization gap, which faithfully reproduces the spatial modulation pattern observed in UTe$_2$. Moreover, its inherent unit-cell-doubling modulation precisely accounts for the spectroscopic features measured in 1T/1H-TaS$_2$. We further predict a characteristic in-plane conductivity anisotropy that serves as a definitive transport fingerprint to discriminate KHW states with distinct ordering wavevectors. Our work provides a microscopic foundation for the newly observed KHW order and establishes a unified theoretical framework for understanding emergent modulated hybridization phenomena in heavy fermion materials.

</details>


### [179] [High-resolution angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy with tunable magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08498)
*Jairo Obando-Guevara,Huu-Thong Le,Emiliano Corcino Aracena,Sayan Singha,Kyungchan Lee,Yingdong Guan,Zhiqiang Mao,Dezhe Jin,Chaoxing Liu,Heike Pfau*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对磁光ARPES中电子轨迹像差导致谱畸变的问题，提出通过后处理模拟和二维动量映射等方法，在强像差条件下高精度恢复电子结构并保持高动量分辨率，为扩展到大磁场提供了路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用磁场调控量子现象是材料研究的关键手段，磁光ARPES虽能提供动量与能量分辨的场依赖电子结构，但电子轨迹像差会引发显著谱畸变与展宽，即使小磁场也如此，因此需恢复高分辨率电子结构的方法。

Method: 采用激光ARPES系统研究偶极场中Bi2Se3，结合精细的电子轨迹数值模拟进行后处理重建；确定二维动量映射、微米级束斑和精确磁场模拟为关键技术要求，并利用圆二色性获取磁-自旋耦合信息。

Result: 即使在强轨迹像差下也能高精度恢复电子结构且保持高动量分辨率；验证了关键实验条件，同时展示圆二色性可额外揭示磁场与自旋的耦合，并通过模拟得到缩放规律。

Conclusion: 实验成果与来自模拟的标度律为向更大磁场环境的磁光ARPES发展提供了明确路线图。

Abstract: The control and perturbation of quantum phenomena with magnetic fields is an indispensable tool in materials research. Recently, in-situ field tuning was implemented into angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (magneto-ARPES), which provides direct momentum and energy-resolved information of the field-dependent electronic structure. However, aberrations of the electron trajectories were shown to be substantial, leading to significant spectral distortions and broadening even in small fields. Here we show that the electronic structure can be recovered from magneto-ARPES spectra with high accuracy while maintaining high momentum resolution even when strong trajectory aberrations are present. We studied Bi$_2$Se$_3$ in a dipole field of a coil using a laser-based ARPES system. The electronic structure was reconstructed in post-processing using detailed electron trajectory simulations. We identify two-dimensional (2D) momentum mapping, a micron beam spot size, and precise numerical field simulations as critical technical requirements for high-resolution magneto-ARPES. We show how circular dichroism can provide additional information about the coupling of the magnetic field to the spin. The experimental achievements and the scaling laws from our simulations provide a road map towards magneto-ARPES in larger fields.

</details>


### [180] [Schwinger boson perturbation theory for spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg magnets: phase diagram and dynamical response near Kitaev spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08548)
*Daiki Sasamoto,Joji Nasu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于Schwinger玻色子的微扰框架，用于自旋S Kitaev-Heisenberg模型。以纯Kitaev模型的自旋液体鞍点作为未微扰态，利用Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分解，通过随机相位近似计算磁不稳定性和动态自旋关联，确定了S=1/2到2的量子自旋液体与磁有序相之间的相界，并计算了自旋动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev-Heisenberg模型因包含量子自旋液体和磁有序竞争而备受关注，但已有的自旋S推广研究缺乏统一的基于自旋液体态的微扰方法，无法在热力学极限下可靠计算磁不稳定性附近的动力学性质。

Method: 采用Schwinger玻色子表示，将纯Kitaev模型的spin-liquid鞍点作为未微扰态；利用Kitaev-Heisenberg模型特有的Klein对偶性将哈密顿量精确分解为对偶不变和对偶变号两部分；将以对偶不变部分作为未微扰项，而对偶变号部分视为微扰，进行随机相位近似（RPA）。

Result: 确定了S=1/2、1、3/2和2时自旋液体与相邻磁有序相的相界；自旋液体区域随S增大快速收缩，在S=2时变得很窄。对S=1的自旋动力学计算表明，修正后的动态自旋结构因子在有限能量处保持宽的双自旋子连续谱，而低能谱权重在相邻磁相的有序波矢处软化。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在热力学极限下计算磁不稳定性附近的自旋动力学，为研究大自旋Kitaev-Heisenberg模型中的量子涨落与磁有序竞争提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We develop a Schwinger boson perturbative framework for the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model. The spin-liquid saddle point of the pure Kitaev model is used as the unperturbed state, and magnetic instabilities and dynamical spin correlations are evaluated around this saddle point. We decompose the Hamiltonian exactly into two parts by exploiting the Klein duality intrinsic to the Kitaev-Heisenberg model. We perform the random-phase approximation by taking the part invariant under the duality transformation as the unperturbed term and treating the remaining term, which changes sign under it, as the perturbation. We determine the phase boundaries separating the quantum spin-liquid regimes from the adjacent magnetically ordered phases for $S=1/2$, $1$, $3/2$, and $2$. The spin-liquid regions shrink rapidly with increasing $S$ and become very narrow at $S=2$. We also compute the spin dynamics of the spin-$S$ Kitaev-Heisenberg model, focusing on $S=1$, and find that the dressed dynamical spin structure factor retains a broad two-spinon continuum at finite energies, while the low-energy spectral weight softens at the ordering wave vectors of the adjacent magnetic phases. Our framework thus enables thermodynamic-limit calculations of spin dynamics near magnetic instabilities.

</details>


### [181] [Magnetic phases of Kondo lattice materials Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08580)
*Jiawen Zhang,Yanan Zhang,Mingyi Wang,Ye Chen,Yu Liu,Yongjun Zhang,Michael Smidman,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对Ce5RhGe2和Ce5IrGe2单晶进行了电阻率、比热和磁化强度测量，并与Ce5CoGe2比较。三者同属正交Pnma结构，晶格常数从Co到Rh到Ir单调增大，体现负化学压力效应。a轴均为易磁化方向。Ce5RhGe2呈铁磁有序，居里温度约11.5 K，行为接近Ce5CoGe2；Ce5IrGe2则在12.7 K和11.8 K发生两次连续磁转变，场下出现多个变磁转变，形成约Ms/5和Ms/3的磁化平台，低温下出现显著零场磁滞回线，表明其基态为亚铁磁态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过用更大半径的Rh和Ir替代Co，系统研究同构Ce5TGe2（T=Co, Rh, Ir）中晶格膨胀（负化学压力）对Ce基磁性、磁有序类型及场致转变成分的调控。

Method: 生长Ce5RhGe2与Ce5IrGe2单晶，利用电输运电阻率、比热和沿三个主晶轴的磁化强度测量表征其磁性质，并与已有Ce5CoGe2数据进行结构和磁性对比。

Result: 发现所有化合物均为正交Pnma结构，晶格常数Co<Rh<Ir；a轴为易磁化轴。Ce5RhGe2为铁磁体，TC≈11.5 K。Ce5IrGe2出现TM1=12.7 K和TM2=11.8 K两个连续磁转变，并存在多级变磁转变和Ms/5、Ms/3磁化平台；易轴方向的低场变磁转变随温度降低移向更低场，最终形成零场附近明显的磁滞回线。

Conclusion: 从Co到Rh，磁性仍以铁磁为主，而到Ir时体系演化为具有复杂磁结构和亚铁磁基态的磁性状态，表明负化学压力可显著改变Ce5TGe2体系的磁交换作用与基态。Ce5IrGe2中类似平台的磁结构及变磁转变为其关键特征，值得进一步微观磁结构研究。

Abstract: Single crystals of Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ and Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ have been systematically investigated by electrical resistivity, specific heat, and magnetization measurements. Together with Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$, all three compounds crystallize in the orthorhombic \emph{Pnma} structure, with the lattice parameters increasing monotonically from Co to Rh to Ir, consistent with the effect of negative chemical pressure. Magnetization measurements along the three principal crystallographic axes identify the \emph{a} axis as the easy magnetization direction throughout the series. Ce$_5$RhGe$_2$ exhibits ferromagnetic ordering with a Curie temperature of approximately 11.5 K and shows magnetic behavior closely resembling that of Ce$_5$CoGe$_2$. In contrast, Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ undergoes two successive magnetic transitions at $T_{\rm M1}=12.7$ K and $T_{\rm M2}=11.8$ K, and there are multiple metamagnetic transitions under magnetic fields, giving rise to magnetization plateaus at fractions of the saturation magnetization $M_{\rm s}$ of approximately $M_{\rm s}/5$ and $M_{\rm s}/3$. The low-field metamagnetic transition along the easy axis shifts to lower field with decreasing temperature, and eventually a pronounced hysteresis loop is observed about zero-field, establishing that Ce$_5$IrGe$_2$ exhibits a ferrimagnetic ground state at the lowest measured temperatures.

</details>


### [182] [$g$-wave altermagnetic order parameter in hematite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08693)
*Tianren Wang,Yuehong Li,Yu Feng,Andong Liu,Yuetong Wu,Qian Zhao,Yujie Yan,Wei Luo,Xin Tong,Yi Lu,Yao Shen,Stefano Agrestini,Jaewon Choi,Qisi Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用非弹性中子散射定量测量了赤铁矿中交变磁振子劈裂的动量依赖关系，首次确定其具有g波对称性，并识别出相反手性的非简并磁振子分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体兼具反铁磁体的零净磁化与动量相关的自旋劈裂，其磁振子能带劈裂可直接探测交变磁序，并有望实现手性选择性磁振子输运，但此前该劈裂在动量空间中的对称性尚未被定量确定。

Method: 对α-Fe₂O₃单晶进行非弹性中子散射实验，系统绘制磁振子劈裂随动量的变化，并与基于交变磁模型的线性自旋波理论计算进行对比。

Result: 观测到劈裂沿节线方向消失、偏离节点处达到最大，揭示出交变磁序参量的g波对称性；理论计算进一步将非简并分支指认为相反手性的磁振子，并证明劈裂源于对称性不等价的长程交换相互作用。

Conclusion: 该结果首次定量确定了交变磁性手性磁振子的动量空间对称性，结合赤铁矿的高磁有序温度和低磁振子阻尼，使其成为低耗散、对称选择性磁振子应用的有力平台。

Abstract: Altermagnets combine the vanishing net magnetization of antiferromagnets with momentum-dependent spin splitting. Magnon band splitting provides a direct probe of altermagnetic order and may enable chirality-selective magnon transport, yet the momentum-space symmetry of this splitting has not been determined quantitatively. Here we use inelastic neutron scattering to map the momentum dependence of altermagnetic magnon splitting in hematite ($α$-Fe$_2$O$_3$). The splitting vanishes along nodal directions and reaches maxima off the nodes, revealing the $g$-wave symmetry of the altermagnetic order parameter. These results agree with linear spin-wave theory calculations based on the altermagnetic model, which further identify the nondegenerate branches as magnons of opposite chirality and trace the splitting to symmetry-inequivalent long-range exchange interactions. Our results provide the first quantitative determination of the momentum-space symmetry of altermagnetic chiral magnons. These findings, together with hematite's high magnetic ordering temperature and low magnon damping, establish it as a promising platform for low-dissipation, symmetry-selective magnonic applications.

</details>


### [183] [Examining Convergence of Cluster Perturbation Theory and Cluster Coupled Cluster for J1-J2 Heisenberg Spin Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08768)
*Jeffrey Keyes,Carlos Jimenez-Hoyos*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在热力学极限下将基于簇平均场(cMF)的微扰论和耦合簇计算扩展至cPT7与cCCSDTQ5，验证了能量序列收敛，并讨论了簇基计算的高效实现策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅将cMF作为零阶波函数用于cPT4和cCCSD，虽对J1-J2 Heisenberg模型得到合理近似，但未充分验证其核心主张：cMF可将强关联系统转化为弱关联系统，从而使微扰论和耦合簇等弱关联方法适用。要确认该主张，必须证明PT和CC级数确实收敛。

Method: 将先前的簇方法计算扩展至更高阶：微扰论推进到cPT7，耦合簇推进到cCCSDTQ5；同时针对热力学极限下更昂贵的计算，讨论了如何高效执行基于簇的计算（如利用簇结构、局域性或稀疏性等技巧）。

Result: 扩展后的计算显示能量随微扰阶数和耦合簇激发阶数增加而逐步收敛，从而支持cMF框架下弱关联方法可用的论断。

Conclusion: 研究表明cMF加微扰论/耦合簇方法在热力学极限下能够收敛，进一步证实cMF将强关联系统转化为弱关联系统以适用标准弱关联方法的有效性，并提供了高效实现更高阶计算的关键思路。

Abstract: In previous work, it was shown that using the cluster mean field(cMF) wavefunction as the zeroth order wavefunction for perturbation theory and coupled cluster produces a reasonable approximation for the J 1-J 2 Heisenberg model in the thermodynamic limit(TDL). However, this work was limited to cPT4 and cCCSD. The central claim of cMF is that it takes strongly correlated systems and makes them weakly correlated so that PT weakly correlated methods can be used. To confirm that this claim is true, it is important to show that the PT and CC series converge. In this paper, we extend the previous calculations to cPT7 and cCCSDTQ5 where we show that the energy is converging. We also discuss how to perform cluster based calculations efficiently in the thermodynamic limit, which is crucial for these more expensive calculations.

</details>


### [184] [Positive Temperature Coefficient of Anisotropy due to $d^6L$ Groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08927)
*Rachel Nickel,Kurt Kummer,Johan van Lierop*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 揭示ε-Fe₂O₃中Fe 3d与配体氧之间的杂化（而非传统强自旋轨道耦合伙伴）是产生正磁各向异性温度系数dK/dT>0的关键；Cr掺杂通过削弱Fe-O杂化改变电子基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解ε-Fe₂O₃在缺乏明显自旋轨道耦合子系统时仍出现dK/dT>0的物理机制，并探索金属-配体杂化对磁各向异性的调控作用。

Method: 对比研究纯ε-Fe₂O₃和Cr掺杂ε-Fe₂O₃（Cr掺杂可削弱Fe-O杂化），分析其从低温非公度磁相到高磁各向异性磁性相转变过程中的电子结构与基态演化。

Result: 纯ε-Fe₂O₃中鉴定出d⁶L配体空穴基态；Cr掺杂导致杂化减弱，基态变为d⁶与d⁶L²的混合，表明金属-配体杂化强度直接决定基态构型。

Conclusion: 金属-配体杂化可作为调控磁性、电子和轨道特性的有效途径，为无需重元素自旋轨道耦合即可获得正dK/dT>0的高各向异性材料设计提供新思路。

Abstract: Positive anisotropy temperature coefficients ($dK/dT>0$) usually arise from hybridization between magnetic 3d states and strongly spin-orbit-coupled (SOC) subsystems. Yet $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ shows $dK/dT>0$ (125-200~K) without an obvious SOC partner. We study pure and Cr-doped (with weakened Fe-O hybridization) $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ across the transition from low temperature incommensurate to high-anisotropy magnetic phases. We identify a $d^6L$ groundstate in $ε$-Fe$_2$O$_3$ while reduced hybridization yields $d^6 + d^6L^2$ in the Cr-doped system, highlighting metal-ligand hybridization as a route to tune magnetic, electronic and orbitronic properties.

</details>


### [185] [Real-time Dyson expansion for the nonequilibrium $GW$ approximation: Correlated spectra and dependence on the reference propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08932)
*Erik Schroedter,Jan-Philip Joost,Michael Bonitz,Vojtech Vlcek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了基于实时Dyson展开（RT-DE）的非平衡GW自能方案，参考传播子可为一般形式（含Hartree-Fock、静态屏蔽交换及关联传播子），以时间线性成本获得含动力学关联的光谱；在驱动双带长程相互作用晶格模型中，平均场与静态屏蔽参考给出最准确的光谱（含卫星结构），而GKBA参考虽能描述散射诱导的占据动力学但会人为展宽/劈裂谱峰；该方法可扩展至超越精确对角化的大体系，解析价带激子复制品及激子摇晃卫星结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡电子光谱需超越平均场的关联描述；全双时GW模拟随传播时间至少立方标度，而时间局域近似或GKBA通常仅在谱中保留平均场特征；现有RT-DE仅在二阶Born近似及平均场参考传播子下恢复动力学关联，缺少非平衡GW自能的高效广义方案。

Method: 在实时Dyson展开(RT-DE)框架下引入非平衡GW自能，参考传播子采用一般形式但要求其非对角时间演化具有时间局域性；具体评估了Hartree-Fock传播子、含非平衡屏蔽的静态屏蔽交换传播子，以及基于HF-GKBA的关联传播子，并对驱动双带长程相互作用晶格模型进行精确对角化基准测试。

Result: 基准显示平均场与静态屏蔽参考产生最准确的光谱，能捕捉平均场缺失的卫星结构；GKBA参考最优描述散射导致的占据数动力学，但人为加宽并劈裂谱峰。在大体系应用中，该方法分辨出价带激子复制品与激子摇晃卫星，且这些结构随激发密度增加而重塑。

Conclusion: RT-DE与GW自能的结合为大规模非平衡光谱计算提供了高效且准确的新途径，不同参考传播子各有优势：平均场/静态屏蔽侧重谱精度，GKBA侧重占据动力学；该方案可探索远超精确方法规模的非平衡激子与卫星结构。

Abstract: Electronic spectra provide direct insight into the excitations and correlations of condensed matter systems. Their description requires electron correlations beyond mean field. In equilibrium, the $GW$ approximation has become the method of choice for many materials. Extending this approximation to nonequilibrium, however, is challenging. Full two-time $GW$ simulations within the nonequilibrium Green's functions approach scale at least cubically with propagation time, whereas improved scaling schemes such as time-local (adiabatic) approximations or the generalized Kadanoff--Baym ansatz usually retain only mean-field character in the spectra. The recently introduced real-time Dyson expansion recovers dynamical correlations in the spectrum at time-linear cost, but has so far been restricted to the second-order Born approximation with mean-field reference propagators. Here we formulate and implement the RT-DE for the nonequilibrium $GW$ self-energy with reference propagators of general form whose off-diagonal evolution is time local. In particular, we assess Hartree--Fock propagators, propagators with statically screened exchange with nonequilibrium screening, and a correlated propagator based on the Hartree--Fock GKBA. Benchmarks against exact diagonalization for a driven two-band lattice model with long-range interactions show that the mean-field and statically screened references yield the most accurate spectra, including satellite structures absent at mean-field level, whereas the GKBA-based approach best captures scattering-induced occupation dynamics, but artificially broadens and splits spectral peaks. When applied to large systems that are beyond the reach of exact methods, the scheme resolves the excitonic replica of the valence band and satellites identified as exciton shake-up, as well as their reshaping with increasing excitation density.

</details>


### [186] [On spectral properties of Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09067)
*Miroslav Hopjan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 扩展的广义Kadanoff-Baym ansatz（GKBA）用于恢复平均场传播子GKBA在碰撞积分中隐藏的谱信息。在Hubbard团簇中，中等相互作用下其谱函数与完整Kadanoff-Baym方程一致，但强相互作用下出现显著偏差甚至谱函数为负，表明GKBA在强关联区存在局限并可能产生非物理谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GKBA使用平均场传播子可大幅降低计算成本（随传播时间线性标度），但其时间对角结构掩盖了输运方程碰撞积分内的谱性质，需要恢复和研究这些隐藏的谱特征。

Method: 采用扩展的广义Kadanoff-Baym ansatz（extended GKBA）来恢复谱性质，并以Hubbard团簇为模型，将其基态谱函数与标准Kadanoff-Baym方程结果进行比较。

Result: 在中等相互作用强度下，扩展GKBA与标准Kadanoff-Baym方程的谱函数吻合良好；但在强相互作用下出现显著偏差，且扩展GKBA给出的谱函数变为负值。

Conclusion: 尽管GKBA在计算上具有优势，但在强相互作用区存在重要局限性；当相互作用足够强时，可能产生非物理的谱性质。

Abstract: The Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz with mean-field propagators is increasingly used to simulate non-equilibrium quantum fermionic and bosonic systems. Compared with the full Kadanoff--Baym equations, its mean-field propagators substantially reduce computational cost, enabling simulations with computational effort that scales linearly with propagation time. However, the time-diagonal structure of the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz obscures spectral properties within the collision integral of the transport equation. Here, we recover and investigate these hidden spectral properties using the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz. For a Hubbard cluster, we compare ground-state spectral functions obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz against those from standard Kadanoff--Baym equations. At moderate interaction strengths, the spectral functions show good agreement. At large interaction strengths, however, significant deviations emerge, and the spectral function obtained with the extended Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz becomes negative. These results demonstrate that, despite its computational advantages, the Generalized Kadanoff--Baym Ansatz has important limitations in strongly interacting regimes and may produce unphysical spectral properties when interactions become sufficiently large.

</details>


### [187] [Green's Functions from Sample-based Krylov Quantum Diagonalization: An Impurity Solver for Dynamical Mean-Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09147)
*Jay Patel,Chakradhar Rangi,Ka-Ming Tam*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 将基于采样的Krylov量子对角化(SKQD)从基态计算推广到单粒子格林函数计算：在N±1粒子扇区构造并采样幺正Krylov子空间，经典计算扇区连接重叠，借助Lanczos连分式重建格林函数，只需浅电路与短时演化采样，无需辅助比特。在单杂质安德森模型上恢复谱函数，并有望用于近期量子硬件上的DMFT杂质求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算用于DMFT等强关联体系时需要高效计算格林函数，但现有算法多依赖深电路或昂贵测量。SKQD在基态问题上具有浅电路、无辅助比特的优势，却尚未推广到谱函数计算。本文旨在扩展SKQD以计算单粒子格林函数，从而在近-term量子硬件上支持更多浴格点的DMFT模拟。

Method: 在N±1粒子数扇区分别构造并采样幺正Krylov子空间；所有扇区连接矩阵元（如涉及产生湮灭算符的重叠）通过经典计算获得。格林函数通过Lanczos连分式重建。量子处理器只需制备初始态并采样短时间演化下的子空间态，保持浅电路和无ancilla特点，规避了深电路和量子相位估计等昂贵步骤。

Result: 在粒子-空穴对称的单杂质安德森模型链几何中，使用离散浴表示（用于DMFT）验证了该方案。仅需全希尔伯特空间的一小部分即可恢复谱函数；在跨越金属-绝缘体转变的多个相互作用强度下，主要谱特征均被准确再现。

Conclusion: SKQD可有效推广至格林函数与谱函数计算，且保持近-term可行的硬件需求。该方法为DMFT提供了一类新的量子杂质求解器路径，有潜力在近期量子设备上处理比当前实际可用的更多浴格点，扩展了量子对角化方法在强关联电子体系中的应用。

Abstract: We generalize the sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization (SKQD) method from ground-state calculations to the evaluation of single-particle Green's functions. By constructing and sampling unitary Krylov subspaces in the N +/- 1 particle-number sectors and evaluating all sector-connecting overlaps classically, the approach reconstructs the Green's function via a Lanczos continued fraction while retaining the shallow-circuit, ancilla-free character of SKQD. The quantum device is required only to prepare and sample short-time evolutions. Applied to the particle-hole-symmetric single-impurity Anderson model in chain geometry, with the discrete bath representation used in dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), the method recovers the spectral function using a relatively small fraction of the full Hilbert space. Across a range of interaction strengths that spans the metal-insulator transition, the main spectral features are reproduced. These results suggest that SKQD-based Green's-function calculations may allow DMFT impurity solvers with a larger number of bath sites on near-term quantum hardware than is currently practical.

</details>

### [188] [Multiplicity of surface polariton configurations in bi-isotropic media with anomalous Hall current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06081)
*Alex Q. Costa,Pedro D. S. Silva,Manoel M. Ferreira*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了简单介质与双各向同性介质界面上的表面极化激元（SPs），涵盖无损耗和小损耗情形，并扩展至含反常霍尔效应（AHE）的双各向同性介质。推导了无损耗条件下非Dyakonov表面极化激元的存在条件；在小损耗下发现传播长度可随磁电参数出现局部极大值；含AHE时出现更丰富的传播配置，且存在一个磁电参数范围使表面波传播被禁止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双各向同性介质因其磁电耦合特性可支持新颖的表面极化激元，但现有研究对其存在条件、损耗影响及与反常霍尔效应结合的行为尚不充分，尤其是缺乏对传播长度异常行为和禁止区的理解。

Method: 采用理论分析方法：在无损耗情况下推导介电常数与双各向同性参数的约束条件；在小损耗假设下分析表面等离激元极化激元的特征长度；对含AHE的界面系统考察不同参数配置下的表面波解，并进行参数空间分类。

Result: 无损耗时存在非Dyakonov表面极化激元，给出了具体存在条件；小损耗时传播长度随磁电参数变化呈现局部极大值，此为手性表面极化激元的特征；含AHE时发现多种传播构型，出现不同传播状态与共存区域，且非零AHE贡献会在磁电参数空间中形成表面波传播的禁止区。

Conclusion: 双各向同性介质可实现具有独特损耗特性和禁止区行为的表面极化激元，传播长度局部极大值与AHE导致的禁止区可作为识别这类手性表面极化激元的实验可观测特征。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate surface polaritons (SPs) arising at the interface between a simple dielectric and a bi-isotropic medium, in the lossless regime and in the presence of small losses. For the former, non-Dyakonov surface polaritons can arise for positive permittivities and magnetoelectric parameters. General restrictions on the permittivities and bi-isotropic parameter (existence conditions) are derived. For the latter, the effect of small losses is considered, and the characteristic surface plasmon-polariton lengths are discussed. The propagation length can exhibit a local maximum for specific values of the magnetoelectric parameter, an unusual new behavior that can be regarded as a signature of these chiral surface polaritons. Surface waves in an interface involving a bi- isotropic medium with an anomalous Hall effect (AHE) are also examined. Multiple configurations for their occurrence are obtained, revealing a richer scenario with distinct regimes of propagation and coexistence in the parameter space. The presence of a non-null AHE contribution creates a magnetoelectric range for which the surface wave propagation is forbidden, providing an additional signature of surface-polariton propagation in bi-isotropic media with an AHE contribution.

</details>


### [189] [Single Dirac Fermions on a Lattice: A Unified View of Chiral and Parity Anomalies in Condensed Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06349)
*Shun-Qing Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文从晶格视角统一理解手性反常和宇称反常，核心问题是单个无质量狄拉克费米子的实现，强调高能带和布里渊区作为物理的紫外完备化的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子反常通常在连续场论中表述，正则化可能破坏经典对称性，但晶体中紧致布里渊区和高能带提供了物理的紫外完备化，需要统一理解反常在晶格中的实现和保护机制。

Method: 发展一种统一的晶格视角，通过构建有限低能区域（对称性恢复）嵌入高能区域（破坏对称性并完成晶格理论）的图景，分析1+1维、3+1维的量子化手性响应和2+1维的半整数量子霍尔响应，并联系近期量子材料的实验进展。

Result: 在1+1和3+1维中，量子化手性响应受费米面附近局域手性对称性保护；在2+1维中，半整数霍尔响应属于具有镜面对称性的单个无质量狄拉克带，而非显式破缺对称性的一般有质量狄拉克绝缘体。

Conclusion: 晶格视角揭示了低能对称性如何从高能区域涌现并保护反常量子化响应，为理解量子材料中的反常现象提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Quantum anomalies are usually formulated in continuum field theory, where regularization may violate a classical symmetry. In a crystal, the compact Brillouin zone and the high-energy bands make that ultraviolet completion physical. This Perspective develops a unified lattice view of chiral and parity anomalies through the problem of realizing a single massless Dirac fermion. The central motif is a finite low-energy region in which the relevant symmetry is restored, embedded in a high-energy sector that breaks the symmetry and completes the lattice theory. In $1+1$ and $3+1$ dimensions, the quantized chiral response is protected by local chiral symmetry near the Fermi surface. In $2+1$ dimensions, the half-integer Hall response belongs to a single massless Dirac band with mirror symmetry, not to a generic massive Dirac insulator that breaks the symmetry explicitly. In both cases the lattice perspective reveals how the low-energy symmetry emerges from the high-energy sector and protects the anomalous and quantized response. Recent experimental progress in quantum materials is discussed in light of this unified view.

</details>


### [190] [On average stress within sub-regions of granular media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06884)
*Matthew R. Kuhn*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 针对边界可穿越颗粒的任意子区域，本文研究了颗粒材料中平均应力的精确计算问题，给出了可精确计算的应力分量与子区域类型，并在不可精确计算时提供误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在颗粒材料中，常需计算较大颗粒集合内部小区域的平均应力，且实际子区域边界往往穿过颗粒而非沿颗粒边界，给应力平均的精确计算带来困难。

Method: 通过分析子区域边界与颗粒相交的几何与力学关系，推导特定应力分量和特定类别子区域的平均应力精确表达式，并对不满足精确条件的情形构造误差界。

Result: 确定了可精确计算平均应力的条件与子区域类型，证明某些应力分量在特定子区域中可精确获得，对一般情况给出了合理且可用的误差范围。

Conclusion: 子区域边界穿过颗粒时，平均应力并非普遍可精确计算，但在明确条件下可实现精确计算，否则可利用误差界保证近似结果的可靠性。

Abstract: The paper concerns computation of average stress within small sub-regions of a larger static granular assembly, where the sub-region's boundary is allowed to pass through the assembly's particles. An exact average is computed for certain stress components and for certain categories of sub-regions. The paper also identifies those choices of sub-regions for which exact stress components may be exactly computed, but when these conditions are not met, provides reasonable bounds on the error.

</details>


### [191] [Topological Edge States and Collective Radiation in a One-Dimensional Atomic Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07616)
*Arda Deniz İyican,Ahmet Levent Subaşı,Özgür Çakır*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究了一维交替间距的偶极耦合量子发射器链在真空电磁场中的拓扑与集体辐射性质，建立了具有长程相互作用和集体耗散的非厄米SSH型模型，分析了无限链的复能带、有限链的束缚态与远场辐射，揭示拓扑保护边界态和系统尺寸依赖的亚辐射态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子发射器阵列中真空介导的长程相互作用与集体耗散如何产生非厄米拓扑现象，以及亚辐射和超辐射态如何与拓扑边界态相互关联，为量子光学和拓扑光子学的交叉提供新的理解。

Method: 将交替间距的量子发射器链映射为扩展的非厄米Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型，在单激发子空间中构造有效哈密顿量；对无限链计算复能带结构和复Berry相位，对有限链采用精确对角化研究本征态，并分析不同本征态的远场辐射图样。

Result: 无限链中存在与波矢位于光锥外对应的亚辐射模式以及实部能带交叉参数区间；复Berry相位在反转对称和谱隙存在时保持量子化。有限链中可区分超辐射、亚辐射和边界态，拓扑非平庸且实部体能隙存在时出现边界态；亚辐射态衰减被强烈抑制且具有系统尺寸依赖性；远场辐射特征由本征态的衰减性质、空间局域性和原胞振幅的宇称决定。

Conclusion: 该体系为研究非厄米多体拓扑态与集体辐射效应的结合提供了一个可行平台，边界态和亚辐射态的实空间与辐射特性可由拓扑参数调控，并对未来设计可控的量子光源或辐射通道具有指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate the topological and collective radiative properties of a one-dimensional diatomic chain of identical quantum emitters with alternating spacing, coupled to the electromagnetic vacuum. The system realizes an extended, non-Hermitian Su-Schrieffer-Heeger-type model with vacuum-mediated long-range interactions and collective dissipation. We focus on the single-excitation manifold, where the dynamics are described by an effective Hamiltonian. For an infinite chain, the complex band structure reveals subradiant modes associated with wave vectors outside the light line, as well as parameter regimes where real-part band crossings occur. The bulk topology is characterized by a complex Berry phase, which remains quantized in the presence of inversion symmetry and a spectral gap. For finite chains, using exact diagonalization, we identify superradiant, subradiant, and edge states with distinct decay rates and spatial profiles. Edge states emerge in the topologically nontrivial regime when a bulk gap in the real part of the energy spectrum is present, while subradiant states exhibit strongly suppressed decay with system-size dependence. Finally, we analyze the far-field radiation patterns associated with different classes of eigenstates and show that the emission characteristics reflect the decay properties, spatial localization, and the parity of the unit-cell amplitudes.

</details>


### [192] [General solution of the Dirac equation for electrons bound by a charged atomic chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07632)
*Alexander Eremko,Larissa Brizhik,Vadim Loktev*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 在狄拉克理论框架下对带电原子链束缚的电子系统进行了研究，获得了狄拉克方程的普遍解析解与电子能量的解析表达式，并证明该系统可引入类似氢原子的主量子数。研究表明电子完全公有化，能量可作为占据数的函数计算；主量子数为n的谱带分裂为n个亚带，呈现精细结构，其分裂尺度已被计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维带电原子链束缚电子的相对论量子力学行为，为其能谱结构和电子集的体化机制提供严格的狄拉克理论描述，并揭示与氢原子量子数相类似的能级规律以及精细结构的起源。

Method: 针对带电原子链所产生的束缚势，在狄拉克理论中建立并求解电子态的方程组，获得一般解析解；进而推导电子能量的解析表达式，引入主量子数并分析占据数对能量的影响，最后计算谱带的精细结构分裂尺度。

Result: 获得了电子波函数和能量的普遍解析解；能量表达式中可自然定义主量子数，证实电子态完全公有化且能量取决于占据数；每个主量子数n对应的谱带分裂为n个亚带，并给出了精细结构分裂的定量尺度。

Conclusion: 带电原子链束缚电子系统在狄拉克理论中被完整解析求解，证明了系统兼具氢原子式量子化与电子完全公有化的特征，其精细结构分裂为实验探测和理论预测一维相对论束缚态提供了明确依据。

Abstract: The system of electrons bound by a charged atom chain is studied within the Dirac theory. The general analytical solution of the Dirac equation is obtained. Analytical expression for electron energy is derived from which it follows that the principal quantum number can be introduced for electron states in such a system, similar to the case of a hydrogen atom. We prove that electrons bound by the atomic chain, are fully collectivized and the energy can be calculated as a function of the occupation number. The spectral band with the principal quantum number $n$ is shown to be split into $n$ subbands forming the fine structure. The scale of the fine structure splitting is calculated.

</details>


### [193] [Transport properties and topological phase transitions for a Creutz-Su-Schrieffer-Heeger ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08146)
*K. A. González,S. Bravo,L. Rosales,P. A. Orellana*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文研究Creutz-Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（CSSH）梯子模型的电子、拓扑与输运性质，发现其存在一个平凡相和三个非平凡拓扑区域，并证明透射谱可复现各拓扑相；还导出非拓扑平带条件，并展示其与连续谱中束缚态（BICs）形成直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CSSH梯子中电子结构与拓扑相的关系，并理解平带、BICs与输运特性之间的内在联系，以建立拓扑性质、平带形成和电子输运的统一图像。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型和Green函数形式，计算能谱、局域态密度（LDOS）和电子透射，并分析不同拓扑相；随后推导非拓扑平带出现的条件及其与BICs形成的关系。

Result: 识别出一个平凡相和三个不同非平凡拓扑区域；透射特征能反映不同拓扑相；非拓扑平带条件同时为BICs的形成提供必要条件，表明拓扑特性、平带与输运之间存在直接关联。

Conclusion: CSSH梯子中拓扑性质、平带与电子输运相互联系，该研究为理解此类准一维系统的拓扑分类及束缚态现象提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the electronic, topological, and transport properties of a Creutz-Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (CSSH) ladder. Using a tight-binding model within the Green's function formalism, we calculate the energy spectrum, local density of states (LDOS), and electronic transmission. We first determine the energy spectrum of the CSSH ladder and analyze the different topological phases present in the system, identifying one trivial phase and three distinct nontrivial regions. We then study electronic transport and show that the transmission reproduces the different topological phases through characteristic transport signatures. Finally, we derive the conditions for the emergence of non-topological flat bands and demonstrate that these bands also provide the necessary conditions for the formation of bound states in the continuum (BICs). Our results establish a direct connection between the topological properties, flat-band formation, and electronic transport in the CSSH ladder.

</details>


### [194] [Exact Phase-Space Rotation in the Trapped Quantum Calogero Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08987)
*Akash Sarkar*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过量子Lax对结构，为含外谐振子势的量子Calogero模型构造了厄米Wigner算子，其期望值满足精确的相空间演化方程，表现为周期2π/Ω的刚性旋转，且在Ω→0时退化为无陷阱模型的自由流方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Calogero模型在谐振子陷阱中的等时动力学缺乏微观相空间描述，现有方法通常限于特定初态或微扰阶，需要从量子Lax对出发建立精确的相空间图像。

Method: 基于量子Lax-对结构构造厄米Wigner算子，推导其期望值的精确相空间演化方程d_t ρ + λ d_x ρ - Ω² x d_λ ρ = 0，适用于任意初态和所有相互作用阶数；并通过矩分析检验守恒量与哈密顿量的关系，同时取Ω→0极限验证无陷阱情形的自由流方程。

Result: 该演化方程描述相空间中的刚性旋转，周期为2π/Ω，实现Calogero模型等时动力学的微观体现；相空间密度的矩形成旋转多重态而非独立守恒量，其中二次扇区内唯一的守恒组合正比于陷阱哈密顿量；Ω→0时方程精确约化为无陷阱模型的自由流方程。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为有陷阱量子Calogero模型提供了精确到全相互作用阶数的相空间动力学描述，揭示了其等时旋转结构与Lax对的内在联系，并为可积多体系统的量子相空间方法奠定了新基础。

Abstract: We develop a microscopic phase-space description of the quantum Calogero model in the presence of an external harmonic confining potential. Building on the quantum Lax-pair structure, we construct a Hermitian Wigner operator whose expectation value obeys the exact phase-space evolution equation d_t rho + lambda d_x rho - Omega^2 x d_lambda rho = 0 for arbitrary initial states and to all orders in the interaction strength. The resulting dynamics is a rigid rotation in phase space with period 2 pi/Omega, providing a microscopic realization of the isochronous dynamics of the trapped Calogero model. We further show that the moments of the phase-space density form rotating multiplets rather than independent conserved quantities. In particular, within the quadratic sector, the unique conserved combination is proportional to the trapped Hamiltonian, providing a nontrivial consistency check of the construction. In the limit Omega -> 0, the equation reduces to the exact free-streaming equation of the untrapped model.

</details>
